Each rubric corresponds to a relation in the tree, preserving the structure of the knowledge being evaluated. Agents receive only the research query. This structure allows us to measure breadth across major research directions and depth along related knowledge chains, with their geometric mean giving overall coverage. The benchmark includes 144 cases across 24 domains and 44,102 rubrics. Evaluating 16 deep research agents reveals different strengths in breadth and depth, with the best-performing agent reaching 57.04% overall coverage. Rubric judgments agree closely with human annotations, and agent rankings remain stable across the tested metric configurations.

</details>


### [72] [$μ$-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-User Task Completion in Shared Interaction Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34920)
*Qihao Lin,Guanxu Chen,Shuhang Zheng,Xiaoya Lu,Jingyi Yang,Terumi Chiba,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Zhuan Shi,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多用户语音智能体基准测试，用于评估智能体能否在动态对话中同时完成多人任务，并遵守各自的权限与隐私要求。测试显示，现有系统仍难以稳定处理多人、多轮互动，尤其是开放权重模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享空间中的语音智能体需要协调彼此关联的多人请求，同时维护每位用户的权限、隐私边界和任务约束。现有评测较少覆盖参与者在会话过程中持续回应、改变需求的闭环互动，因此难以检验系统在真实多用户场景中的可靠性。

Method: 作者构建了 μ-Bench，针对闭环、全双工的多用户语音交互进行联合任务评测。模拟参与者拥有相互隔离的目标、知识和权限，并会根据智能体发言及工具执行结果动态调整回应。基准涵盖二十个领域、百种情境，共八百个案例，变化因素包括群体规模、声学条件和后续需求；其中四百零八例支持工具调用，三百九十二例不支持。评测要求所有用户目标、隐私边界和终止状态同时满足，并另设更严格的指标以排除未经授权的工具调用；同时通过归属判断、对话线索追踪和协调能力诊断定位失败原因。

Result: 在十三种语音智能体中，专有系统的最高联合任务成功率为：无工具调用场景百分之六十二点二四，支持工具调用场景百分之六十五点四四；开放权重模型均低于百分之二十。诊断结果显示，开放权重模型的主要短板是多轮对话中的用户归属判断和线索追踪。对照实验还发现，在能力较强的系统中，群体规模增大也会持续降低联合任务成功率。

Conclusion: μ-Bench通过可响应的多用户模拟和可验证的会话级结果，为共享空间语音智能体提供了更严格的评测平台。实验表明，现有系统在多人任务协调、隐私与权限约束以及多轮信息追踪方面仍有明显改进空间。

Abstract: Voice agents in shared spaces must satisfy interdependent multi-user requests while upholding per-user authority, privacy, and task constraints. Existing evaluations offer limited coverage when participants react throughout a session. We introduce $μ$-Bench, a benchmark for joint task completion in closed-loop, full-duplex multi-user voice interaction. Simulated participants act from isolated profiles of goals, knowledge, and permissions, adapting to the agent's speech and tool outcomes. Across 20 domains, 100 scenarios vary group size, acoustics, and follow-up demands to form 800 cases (408 tool-augmented, 392 tool-free). Joint Task Success (JTS) requires all user goals, privacy boundaries, and terminal states to hold jointly, while Pure JTS further disallows unauthorized tool calls; Attribution, Threading, and Coordination (ATC) diagnostics localize failures. Across 13 voice agents, proprietary systems peak at 62.24\% tool-free and 65.44\% tool-augmented success, while open-weight models remain below 20\%. ATC diagnostics show that open-weight models lag primarily in multi-turn attribution and threading, and controlled contrasts reveal that larger groups consistently degrade joint task success across capable systems. By coupling reactive multi-user simulation with verifiable session-level outcomes, $μ$-Bench provides a rigorous testbed for shared voice agents.

</details>


### [73] [Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces for Hard Math Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34928)
*Lukas Aichberger,Adam Golinski,Arno Blaas,Arwen Bradley,Jacob Springer,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Michael Kirchhof,Sinead Williamson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出多样化替代推理轨迹方法：从未通过验证的推理轨迹中定位高不确定性步骤，生成不同的替代推理，再补全并筛选完整轨迹。实验显示，该方法比独立蒙特卡洛采样覆盖更多难题，并能提供有效的模型训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理模型的后训练依赖最终答案通过验证的推理轨迹。但对于难题，独立采样很难找到这类轨迹，导致可用训练数据稀缺。论文希望从已有的失败推理中挖掘新的解题路径，提升难题覆盖率和数据利用效率。

Method: 该方法从一条被拒绝的推理轨迹出发，将其拆分为多个步骤，并依据结合步骤位置的加权不确定性进行排序。针对排名靠前的步骤，利用参考答案提供的引导和元思考生成刻意不同的替代方案，再以替代步骤为基础补全完整推理轨迹，并通过验证筛选可接受结果。

Result: 在贪心解码未能解决的深度数学十万级数据集题目上，按生成轨迹数量对齐后，该方法比独立蒙特卡洛采样多找回百分之三十七的问题。每找回一个问题的估算生成成本几乎相同，而每条可接受轨迹的成本降低百分之三十。使用该方法挖掘的轨迹对八十亿参数指令模型进行后训练后，该模型在数学五百题基准与美国数学邀请赛的六项评测中，有五项取得最高点估计成绩。

Conclusion: 重新审视并改造失败轨迹，能够补充单纯独立采样难以发现的解题路径，扩大难题覆盖范围，并以较低的单条有效轨迹成本构建有用的后训练数据。结果支持将该方法作为数学推理数据挖掘的有效途径。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) for mathematical reasoning often relies on accepted traces whose final answers pass a verifier. For hard math problems, however, such traces can be rare under independent Monte Carlo (MC) sampling. To address this, we propose Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces (DART), a trace-mining method that starts from a rejected trace, decomposes it into reasoning steps, and ranks them by position-weighted uncertainty. It then generates deliberately distinct alternative candidates for highly ranked steps using reference-answer guidance and a meta-thought before completing the resulting traces. On greedy-unsolved DeepMath-103K problems, DART recovers 37% more problems than MC sampling at a matched number of generated traces. Its estimated generation cost per recovered problem is nearly identical, while its cost per accepted trace is 30% lower. Post-training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on traces mined by DART achieves the highest point estimate in five of six evaluations across MATH-500 and AIME benchmarks. These results demonstrate that revisiting rejected traces with DART can expand problem coverage and provide effective post-training data.

</details>


### [74] [Tokenisation is not in APX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34938)
*Violeta Kastreva,Philip Whittington,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 除非多项式时间与非确定性多项式时间相等，否则不存在能以任意固定常数近似比求解该问题的多项式时间分词算法；即使字母表只有一个符号，直接分词也难以高效近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词是文本处理中的基础问题，但其优化难度和近似求解能力尚不明确。论文旨在刻画不同分词方式、目标函数及字母表规模下，分词问题能否被多项式时间算法有效近似。

Method: 论文通过复杂性理论中的归约，证明分词优化问题的近似困难性。分析范围涵盖直接分词与自底向上分词、压缩目标与单元语法对数似然目标，以及二元或更大字母表；另针对一元字母表考察直接分词。

Result: 除非多项式时间与非确定性多项式时间相等，否则对于上述主要设定，不存在能达到任意固定常数近似比的多项式时间算法，因此这些问题不属于常数因子可近似类。即使字母表为一元，直接分词仍是近似困难问题，且不存在多项式时间近似方案。

Conclusion: 论文表明，分词的高效近似求解存在根本的计算复杂性障碍；这一结论跨越多种分词策略、优化目标和字母表规模，意味着实际算法通常需要依赖启发式方法或接受近似质量与计算效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that, unless P=NP, no polynomial-time algorithm can find a tokeniser whose objective is within any constant factor of the optimum, meaning that tokenisation is not in the APX complexity class. We prove this for direct and bottom-up tokenisation, under both a compression or unigram log-likelihood objectives, and for binary or larger alphabets. We further show that, even over unary alphabets, direct tokenisation is APX-hard, and thus admits no polynomial-time approximation scheme.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Learning the Platform, Not the Learner: Sequencing Shortcuts in the Offline Evaluation of Educational Recommender Systems and LLM Rankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33240)
*Youssef Jdidou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文指出，教育推荐系统的离线准确率往往主要反映平台既有的出题与课程结构，而非对学习者需求的理解；采用考虑课程结构的评估后，个性化推荐的价值在多数数据集上更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育推荐系统通常依据日志中的下一项活动预测能力进行离线比较，但下一项活动常由教师、辅导系统或平台引擎决定。因此，模型可能只是在复现平台的选择规律，却被误认为理解了学习者。论文旨在区分这两种能力，并检验现有评估是否低估了个性化推荐的实际价值。

Method: 研究在来自五个平台的八个公开数据集上，比较四种仅依据最近一次活动的结构预测方法、十种个性化推荐方法，以及参数规模从五亿到七十亿的开放权重大型语言模型排序器，并采用两种评估协议。研究还分析未见过的活动转移和学习者自主选择的步骤，开展具有已知学习者需求的模拟实验，并提出按课程结构对测试步骤加权的评估方式，以及一份课程纲要感知评估清单和用于计算推荐方法相对结构基线提升的程序包。

Result: 结构预测方法达到所有方法中最佳准确率的百分之八十一至百分之百，中位数为百分之九十五，其预测能力主要来自群体活动转移，而非课程顺序。最佳个性化方法在五个数据集上显著优于其他方法，排序折损累计增益@10提升零点零零七至零点一零二；在一个数据集的四次训练中，有三次也观察到显著优势。所有方法在训练中未出现过的转移以及学习者自主选择的步骤上准确率都大幅下降。大型语言模型排序器在随机负例下表现尚可，但通常不明显优于词语匹配基线；面对符合课程结构的合理负例时，其表现接近随机水平或显著落后于转移启发式，且不随模型规模增大而明显改善。模拟实验显示，结构方法所占的预测优势会随平台决定步骤的比例变化；普通离线准确率甚至会把理解学习者需求的理想方法排在模仿平台的方法之后。对结构上较难预期的测试步骤加权后，真实数据中有七个数据集呈现显著的个性化信号。

Conclusion: 教育推荐系统的常规离线准确率不能可靠代表其对学习者的个性化能力，因为它很容易受到平台决策结构的支配。评估应区分平台安排与学习者选择，并关注结构难以预测的步骤；按结构意外程度加权，能够更有效地揭示推荐方法是否真正响应学习者需求。

Abstract: Educational recommenders are usually compared offline, by how well they predict the next activity in logs of tutoring systems, learning applications and MOOCs, where the next activity is largely decided by the platform: teachers assign problem sets, tutors draw problems until mastery, engines select questions. On eight public datasets from five platforms, we compare four structural predictors, which use nothing about the learner beyond the last activity, with ten personalised recommenders and with open-weight LLM rankers from 0.5 to 7 billion parameters, under two protocols. The structural predictors reach between 0.81 and 1.00 of the best accuracy of any method (median 0.95), mostly through population transitions rather than the course order; the best personalised recommender is significantly better on five datasets, by 0.007 to 0.102 NDCG@10, and on Eedi in three of four training runs. Every method is far less accurate on steps along transitions never seen in training and on steps chosen by learners. LLM rankers look competent with random negatives but are never much better than a word-matching baseline, and with negatives that are plausible under the course structure they stay close to random or well behind the transition heuristic, whatever their size. In a simulation with known learner needs, the structural share tracks the share of steps decided by the platform, and offline accuracy ranks an oracle of the learners' needs below imitators of the platform. Weighting test steps by how unlikely the structure makes them restores much of the link with usefulness and, on the real data, reveals a significant personalisation signal on seven of the eight datasets. We propose a syllabus-aware evaluation checklist and release a Python package that reports the lift of any recommender over the structure.

</details>


### [76] [Multi-Modal Causal Disentanglement for Multi-Domain Sequential Audio Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33628)
*Yu Ren,Pengcheng Li,Weijun Xu,Hengzhong Zhou,Shufeng Ye,Qiang Xiao,Dongjing Wang,Chuanjiang Luo,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向多领域音频推荐的多模态因果解耦框架，通过分离跨领域共享偏好与领域专属偏好、引入多模态语义标识并补全未活跃领域的用户表示，缓解负迁移与跨领域冷启动；公开数据、工业数据及线上测试均验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐平台中的歌曲、歌单和播客等领域具有异质性。联合训练容易被高流量领域主导，使弱势领域受到负迁移；与此同时，传统基于用户或物品标识的协同信号难以连接语义相关但行为重叠较少的领域。因此，研究需要既抑制有害跨领域影响，又加强语义层面的领域联系。

Method: 框架从因果视角将用户行为建模为领域不变偏好与领域专属偏好的共同结果，并将用户表示拆分为这两部分。通过领域对抗不变性约束和正交约束，将跨领域交互限制在共享偏好子空间，减少有害迁移路径。另从预训练文本、视觉和音频特征中通过残差量化构造可训练的语义标识嵌入，以端到端方式适配推荐任务；并利用迁移网络为用户补全其未活跃领域的表示，以缓解跨领域冷启动。

Result: 在公开数据集和工业数据集上，方法相较于强基线均取得稳定提升。网易云音乐线上对照测试显示，该方法在三个领域的主要流量场景中均带来持续收益，体现出大规模实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究将因果解耦、多模态语义建模和跨领域表示迁移结合起来，针对多领域音频推荐中的负迁移、语义关联不足及冷启动问题提供了统一方案。实验与线上结果支持其有效性和工业部署价值。

Abstract: Modern music platforms expose users to heterogeneous audio content domains such as songs, playlists, and podcasts. Multi-domain sequential recommendation (MDSR) seeks to exploit such cross-domain behavior, yet two obstacles persist in practice. First, joint optimization across heterogeneous domains is prone to negative transfer, with high-traffic domains dominating the shared parameter space and suppressing weaker domains. Second, conventional ID-based collaborative signals fail to bridge domains that are semantically related but exhibit little behavioral overlap. This paper presents CAMAR, a multi-modal causal disentanglement framework for multi-domain audio recommendation. From a causal perspective, user behavior is modeled as the joint outcome of a domain-invariant preference and domain-specific preferences. Guided by this view, CAMAR factorizes user representations into domain-invariant and domain-specific components and enforces domain adversarial invariance together with orthogonality, restricting cross-domain interaction to the invariant subspace and suppressing harmful transfer paths. To strengthen cross-domain semantic coupling, CAMAR derives trainable semantic-ID embeddings from pre-trained text, vision, and audio features through residual quantization, yielding an end-to-end multi-modal adaptation tailored to recommendation. A transfer network imputes user representations for inactive domains, mitigating cross-domain cold start. Experiments on both public and industrial datasets demonstrate consistent gains over strong baselines. In online A/B tests on NetEase Cloud Music, one of China's largest music streaming platforms, CAMAR delivers consistent gains across all three domains in their main traffic scenarios, validating its effectiveness in multi-domain audio recommendation at scale.

</details>


### [77] [Structure-Aware Multimodal Retrieval Under Data Scarcity, Privacy Constraints and Out-of-Domain Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33649)
*Argyrios Zafeiriou,Dionysios Koulouris,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需领域标注、可在本地运行且不暴露图像内容的图版检索流程；在三个专业领域中，其检索效果明显优于跨模态编码器和自动生成的图像描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法医病理、牙科和地质学等专业领域的图像检索面临三重限制：缺少标注数据与训练样本，资料往往不能离开本地设备，通用图文模型又难以适应训练数据之外的专业图像。论文因此探索利用文档页面结构进行检索，减少对图像内容识别和领域训练的依赖。

Method: 流程先根据页面几何布局检测图像与文本框，再将框的位置关系绘制成示意图，交由本地开放权重的80亿参数视觉语言模型为每张图片匹配说明文字。匹配时模型看不到原始像素或文字。研究在三个私有语料库上评估说明文字检索，并与词汇检索、自动生成的图像描述、五种图文编码器及文档解析器进行比较，由人工盲评相关性。

Result: 几何配对在三个领域的正确率分别为96%、92%和77%，高于表现较好的文档解析器的76%、45%和53%；使用托管模型处理示意图也达到相近准确率，而将坐标直接写成文字会使准确率下降22个百分点。基于说明文字的前五位归一化折损累积增益分别为0.85、0.87和0.76，优于最佳跨模态编码器的0.54、0.51和0.27，以及自动生成描述的0.41、0.28和0.30。配对错误会进一步影响检索结果。

Conclusion: 利用页面几何结构进行图像与说明文字配对，可在无领域训练的条件下实现准确的专业图版检索，并可由小型本地模型完成。该方法整体优于通用及生物医学图文编码器；其几何示意图不包含图像或文字内容，有助于保护敏感资料。研究同时公开了代码与提示词。

Abstract: Purpose: Figure retrieval from reference atlases in expert domains like forensic pathology, dentistry and geology must work without labels or training pairs, on content that often cannot leave local hardware, and on images outside the training data of image-text encoders. This work proposes a structure-aware pipeline that satisfies all three, retrieving figures through the page structure recovered by layout geometry and a local open-weight 8B VLM. Methods: A geometry-based pairing method is introduced, in which the model receives a schematic of the detected boxes and selects each picture's caption without access to pixels or text. Caption retrieval is evaluated on three private corpora (29 books, 10,421 figures, 218 queries) with blind human grading, against a lexical baseline, generated descriptions and five image-text encoders. Results: Geometric pairing was correct for 96%, 92% and 77% of pictures in the three domains, against 76%, 45% and 53% for the better of two document parsers. A hosted model given the schematic was equally accurate, while coordinates as text lost 22 points. Caption retrieval obtained an nDCG@5 of 0.85, 0.87 and 0.76, against 0.54, 0.51 and 0.27 for the best cross-modal encoder and 0.41, 0.28 and 0.30 for generated descriptions. Pairing errors carried over to retrieval. Conclusion: The proposed pipeline achieves accurate figure retrieval with small local models and no in-domain training, where general and biomedical image-text encoders fall far behind. Its geometry-only pairing is the most accurate of five methods and exposes no content, even to a hosted model. Code and prompts are released.

</details>


### [78] [OneQuery: Unified Sequence-to-Structure Generation for Multilingual E-commerce Query Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33653)
*Mingyu Zhang,Jiejie Qin,Jianhui Ji,Rong Xiao,Meishan Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向多语言电商搜索的统一生成式查询理解框架，通过结构化生成和渐进式训练提升多项查询处理任务的效果与部署实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统查询理解系统将语言识别、拼写纠错、翻译和查询标注等任务分开优化，容易产生错误累积，也难以利用任务间的关联，且维护成本较高。多语言电商搜索因此需要更统一、稳健的查询理解方案。

Method: 提出统一生成框架“统一查询”，以电商领域大语言模型为基础，将四项任务整合为受预定义模式约束的“序列到结构”自回归生成过程，并采用三阶段渐进式训练流程，结合多任务强化学习进行对齐。

Result: 摘要称，该框架在各项任务上均持续优于基线方法，并能适配不同规模的基础模型。在速卖通电商搜索场景中的线上实验也验证了其实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 将多项查询理解任务统一为受约束的结构化生成，有望缓解传统流水线的错误累积与任务割裂问题。实验结果表明，该方案兼具任务效果和工业部署潜力。

Abstract: Multilingual e-commerce search requires robust query understanding to convert cross-lingual user queries with varied linguistic patterns into standardized semantic representations that support retrieval and ranking. However, traditional Query Understanding systems suffer from error accumulation, separately optimized subtasks that ignore their mutual correlations, and high maintenance costs. To address these issues, this paper proposes OneQuery, a unified generative framework for multilingual query understanding based on domain-specific large language models in e-commerce. We reformulate four subtasks, namely Language Recognition, Spelling Correction, Translation, and Query Tagging, into a unified Sequenceto-Structure autoregressive generation process constrained by predefined schemas, and design a three-stage progressive training pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning alignment. OneQuery demonstrates consistent gains over baselines across all tasks and remains effective across different backbone sizes, and online experiments in the AliExpress e-commerce search scenario further confirm its practical value for industrial deployment.

</details>


### [79] [Privacy-Aware Adaptive Client Selection for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33890)
*Ali Shams Saryazd,Abolfazl Gandomi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种隐私感知的联邦客户端自适应选择方法，旨在减少查询广播并利用检索反馈优化客户端选择；现有结果显示查询接收方减少70%，但尚不足以证明答案质量得到保持，且画像扰动不构成查询或语料内容的保密保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦检索增强生成需要从分散在多个客户端的语料中检索证据。将查询广播给所有客户端会增加检索负担，也会扩大查询暴露范围。因此，论文希望在减少参与客户端数量的同时，尽可能维持证据检索与最终回答质量。

Method: 提出一种在线客户端选择方法，综合考虑经过扰动的语义画像、证据反馈、延迟、数据传输成本和探索因素，从多个客户端中动态选择参与检索的客户端。实验设置为十个客户端中选择三个，并评估端到端表现及检索表现。

Result: 归档的端到端基准中，证据重合度为0.372，回答准确率为0.287；选择三个客户端使查询接收方数量减少70%。但检索评估复用了全量广播基线，且采用不同的融合平局处理规则，因而比较并不完全一致。由于各客户端语料互不重叠，排序融合带来的证据重合度上限为0.375，故0.372不能简单视为充分的相关性证据。回答准确率低于固定标签诊断值0.645；同时缺少生成器原始回答，无法核验解析器是否正确提取答案。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了减少查询广播范围的潜力，但现有实验尚不能确认其在降低通信与检索负担时保留了回答质量。后续需要使用校正后的基线开展新的答案级评估，并核验原始生成结果。此外，语义画像扰动并不能保证查询或语料片段的机密性，不能将其等同于完整的隐私保护。

Abstract: Federated retrieval-augmented generation retrieves evidence from distributed corpora. However, broadcasting queries to all clients increases the retrieval burden and query exposure. We present Privacy-Aware Adaptive Client Selection for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation (PACS-FedRAG), an online selector that takes into account perturbed semantic profiles along with evidence feedback, latency, payload cost, and exploration. In a ten-client setting where three clients are chosen, the archived end-to-end benchmark reports an evidence overlap of 0.372 and an answer accuracy of 0.287. The retrieval-only evaluation, on the other hand, reuses a full-broadcast baseline with a different fusion tie-breaking policy. Rank fusion across disjoint client corpora imposes an overlap ceiling of 0.375, limiting its interpretation as a relevance measure. Answer accuracy is below the fixed-label diagnostic of 0.645, and raw generator responses are unavailable for parser verification. Corrected baselines and a new answer-level evaluation are needed to assess whether quality is preserved. Selecting three clients reduces the number of query recipients by 70%. Profile perturbation does not provide confidentiality of queries or passages.

</details>


### [80] [MulCLIP: A Multi-level Alignment Framework for Enhancing Fine-grained Long-context CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34083)
*Chau Truong,Hieu Ta Quang,Zhenzhen Liu,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需区域候选框的多层级图文对齐框架，利用长文本的不同粒度改善视觉语言模型对细节描述的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型通常依赖简短图像描述进行训练，因此面对较长、包含丰富细节的文本时对齐能力不足。已有方法借助区域候选框将图像区域与句子对应，但会增加部署成本。本文旨在利用自然长文本的结构，在不依赖区域候选框的条件下实现细粒度对齐。

Method: 该框架针对不同文本粒度设计相互兼容的训练信号：通过全局对比对齐长描述与简短摘要；通过词语—图像块重建，在经过局部校准的特征上学习单个样本内部的词语与图像块语义关系；通过子描述聚合的图像块对齐，增强具有上下文的子描述定位能力。整体采用端到端训练，而非简单叠加彼此独立的目标。

Result: 在涵盖不同描述长度的基准测试中，该方法取得了持续的性能提升。消融实验表明，各训练目标具有互补性，联合使用能够改善整体框架表现。摘要未提供具体数值，因此无法判断提升幅度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用长文本自身的层次结构进行多粒度对齐，有望在避免区域候选框额外部署开销的同时，提升模型对详细描述的理解与图文匹配能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP excel at image-text alignment but struggle with long, detailed descriptions due to training on short captions. Recent methods address this limitation using region proposals to align visual regions with sentences, but at substantial deployment cost. We present MulCLIP, an end-to-end multi-level alignment framework that directly exploits natural long-text structure without region proposals. Instead of simply stacking objectives, MulCLIP aligns different textual granularities through compatible training signals: global contrastive alignment for long captions and short summaries, Word-Patch Reconstruction over locally calibrated features for within-sample word-patch semantics, and Subcaption-Aggregated Patch alignment for context-rich subcaption grounding. Experiments on benchmarks spanning varying caption lengths show consistent gains, and ablations confirm that the proposed objectives are complementary and jointly improve the overall framework. Code will be released at https://github.com/truongchau2602/MulCLIP.

</details>


### [81] [RecField: Embedding Recommendations into Spatial Movie Organization as Affordances for Serendipitous Encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34330)
*Kenta Oku*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究提出一种融入电影整理界面的推荐方式。结果显示，推荐未显著提升整体环境层面的偶遇感，但能增强部分电影引发观影兴趣、拓宽偏好的效果；其影响也可能通过促使用户自主探索而间接产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往惊喜式推荐研究多关注算法能否推荐意外且有用的内容，但惊喜体验也受到系统界面与用户活动的影响。研究因此将推荐视为支持其他活动的环境机会，而非用户的首要目标，并探讨如何在整理个人电影资料的过程中促成发现。

Method: 研究设计了名为“推荐场域”的空间化电影整理界面，用户可自由将电影放入不同收藏区，并查看按属性组织、标明推荐视角的推荐列表。研究通过组间实验招募五十七名参与者，在创建和整理个人电影资料这一主要任务中，比较有无推荐条件下的发现体验；评估同时覆盖整体环境层面与单部电影层面。

Result: 推荐条件未显著改变环境层面的总体感知惊喜，但与偶遇相关的体验评分呈上升趋势，效应较小。在单部电影层面，推荐条件下的收藏影片在激发观影意愿和拓宽偏好方面得分显著更高，而新颖性与意外性没有显著提升。被评估的收藏影片中，仅百分之十四直接来自推荐列表；即使只分析经由其他途径加入的影片，评分差异的效应量仍相近。

Conclusion: 嵌入其他活动中的推荐，既可能通过用户直接采纳推荐内容促成发现，也可能间接激发用户自主探索与重新评价。评估惊喜体验应兼顾整体环境和单项内容两个层面，不能仅依赖推荐列表的直接采纳率。

Abstract: Research on serendipity-oriented recommender systems has focused on algorithms that recommend unexpected yet useful items. However, serendipity is increasingly viewed as a user experience shaped by both algorithmic outputs and system features. This study positions recommendations not as the primary goal users pursue but as an environmental affordance that creates opportunities for discovery during other activities. We propose RecField, a spatial movie organization interface where users freely arrange movies into collection zones. For each collection, RecField provides attribute-based recommendation lists labeled according to their recommendation perspectives. A between-subjects experiment ($N$ = 57) examined the effects of recommendations on discoveries made during a foreground task of creating and organizing personal movie profiles. Although recommendations did not significantly affect environment-level perceived serendipity, ratings of encounter-related aspects tended to be higher, with small effect sizes. At the individual-movie level, favorites in the recommendation condition received significantly higher ratings for stimulating the desire to watch and broadening preferences, but not for novelty or unexpectedness. Notably, only 14\% of evaluated favorites in the recommendation condition were added directly from recommendation lists. Differences in ratings showed similar effect sizes even when the analysis was restricted to favorites added through other routes. These findings suggest that embedded recommendations can contribute to serendipitous experiences both directly through item acceptance and indirectly by prompting users' own exploration and re-evaluation. They also highlight the value of evaluating serendipity at both the environment and item levels and the limitations of relying solely on direct recommendation acceptance rates.

</details>


### [82] [ConTra: A Training Framework for Effective Contextual Retrieval via Prefix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34366)
*Jiajun Hou,Jingbang Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出轻量级前缀生成框架“康特拉”，在提升长文档分块检索效果的同时，加快索引构建，并增强系统抵御干扰文本的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档通常被切分为独立索引的文本块，但分块可能丢失文档身份和上下文信息，导致检索与排序效果下降。已有方案要么只把上下文编码进向量、无法供文本重排器使用；要么生成文本前缀，虽能服务向量检索和重排，却耗时较高，效果也可能不及上下文编码器。

Method: 提出“康特拉”强化学习框架，训练轻量级模型为每个文本块生成一个前缀。训练奖励综合利用冻结的嵌入模型和重排模型反馈、事实核验以及长度控制。索引构建时生成前缀，并保存增强后的文本及其向量，以便后续查询复用。论文还构建了真实语境基准，涵盖五类应用文档集合，并设置三种受控条件以考察干扰文本的影响。

Result: 在上下文检索测试集和真实语境基准上，二十亿参数版本优于采用特定提示方法的九十亿参数基线及四十亿参数上下文编码器，整体最佳成绩差距最高达三点一五分。针对短文档，其索引速度分别达到上述两种基线的七点七四倍和二点一五倍。仅在上下文检索测试集上训练后，该模型在真实语境基准重排评测中领先独立方法，且面对随机干扰和主题相似干扰时，比同规模提示基线更稳定。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在索引阶段生成并保存可复用的文本前缀，能够兼顾分块的上下文保留、检索与重排，并降低索引成本。该方法在多种文档集合和干扰条件下表现出较强效果与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Data management systems organize large text collections for tasks such as finding relevant studies and extracting contract clauses. Systems make long documents searchable by splitting them into independently indexed chunks. Chunking can separate text from its identifying details. The indexing challenge is to keep each chunk independently searchable while preserving the context needed to find and rank it. Existing methods either encode that context in chunk vectors or attach it as a text prefix. Context encoded only in vectors is unavailable to text rerankers. Prompted prefixes serve both vector search and reranking, but are costly to generate at indexing time and can underperform contextual encoders. We propose ConTra (Context That Travels), a reinforcement learning framework for training lightweight prefix generators. Its reward combines feedback from frozen embedding and reranking models with factual verification and length control. ConTra generates one prefix per chunk during index construction and stores the augmented text and its embedding for reuse across queries. To evaluate contextual retrieval across workloads and under interference, we introduce RealContextBench, a benchmark with five application collections and three controlled conditions that isolate the effect of distracting text. On ConTEB and RealContextBench, ConTra-2B outperforms the prompted 9B baseline using Anthropic's method and the PPLX-4B contextual encoder, with a margin of up to 3.15 points over their best overall scores, while indexing short documents 7.74 times and 2.15 times as fast as those baselines, respectively. Post-trained only on ConTEB, it leads the standalone methods on RealContextBench after reranking and is less sensitive than the prompted 2B baseline to random and topically similar distractors. Code, datasets, and trained models are available at https://github.com/SStan1/ConTra.

</details>


### [83] [Grounding User Preferences in Shared Multimodal Concepts for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34436)
*Ao Xu,Jinhui Huang,Wenqiang Tang,Tiehua Mei,Hongru Hou,Hengrui Chen,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种多模态知识图谱推荐框架，将物品中反复出现的语义因素归纳为可共享的规范化概念，并据此建模用户偏好；在三个公开基准的六项指标上均优于多类对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐方法虽能利用图像和文本增强物品理解，但用户偏好通常仍以与单个物品绑定的隐式信号表示。相同语义因素因而难以跨物品累积证据，也缺乏可持续共享的身份。论文旨在为这类因素建立统一、可寻址的表示，以改善偏好建模与迁移。

Method: 框架从物品图像和标题中归纳适应具体领域的概念词汇，将用户交互历史关联到有证据支持的概念，并联合建模直接的用户—物品交互信号与经由概念传递的偏好信号。通过让不同物品中的相同语义映射到共享概念，使相关偏好证据能够汇聚。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，该方法在论文报告的六项指标中均优于协同过滤、知识图谱、多模态以及近期基于大型语言模型或多模态大型语言模型的推荐方法。受控实验表明，规范化概念身份和个性化的用户—概念对应关系均有作用；移除或重连用户图对低活跃用户造成的性能影响尤其明显。

Conclusion: 研究表明，规范化的多模态概念可以成为有效的偏好建模与迁移单元，使跨物品的重复语义获得共享表示，并帮助系统累积偏好证据。论文还强调了准确建立个性化用户—概念联系的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation has substantially advanced item understanding by incorporating rich visual and textual content. Yet user preferences are still commonly encoded as implicit signals tied to individual items, leaving recurring semantic factors without persistent identities that can be shared across items. We introduce MKGE, a multimodal knowledge graph framework that represents these factors as canonical, addressable concepts. MKGE induces a domain-adaptive concept vocabulary from item images and titles, grounds each user's interaction history in supported concepts, and jointly models direct user-item evidence and concept-mediated preference signals. By turning recurring semantics into shared coordinates, MKGE enables evidence from different items to accumulate around the same preference factors. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate that MKGE consistently outperforms collaborative, knowledge-graph, multimodal, and recent LLM/MLLM-based recommenders across all six reported metrics. Controlled studies further demonstrate the importance of canonical concept identities and personalized user-concept correspondence, with especially pronounced gains over removing or rewiring the user graph for low-activity users. These results establish canonical multimodal concepts as effective units for preference modeling and transfer. Code, constructed knowledge graphs, and prompts are available at https://github.com/Garden1a-X/MKGE.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Semantic Density Does Not Predict Agent-Memory Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33184)
*Saksham Gupta,Yash Choudhary,Arpit Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，记忆库的语义密度及其他全局统计量，无法可靠预警智能体任务表现下滑；在长期任务中，真正导致失败的是无上限整库提示超出模型输入容量，而令牌增长可直接揭示这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化文本记忆能增强大语言模型智能体，但反复更新记忆可能损害任务表现。研究旨在检验记忆库的统计特征能否在留出任务准确率下降前提供可靠预警。

Method: 研究者在五类交互任务和两种抽象推理设置中，收集了十三种记忆方法的八百零五个快照，分析平均两两余弦相似度及另外十三项几何、检索和图结构统计量。记忆内容涵盖轨迹、规则、工作流、策略及关联记录，使用选择性检索或整库提示。研究还在该数据集上训练分类器，并不作调整地应用于最多包含五千项制作任务的另一组长期运行记录。

Result: 没有任何统计量能可靠预测准确率下滑的早期迹象；平均两两余弦相似度的中位预警提前量为零。以峰值准确率之后的检查点作为另一种下降判定时，高相似度项目对和近邻拥挤度变化较早，但表现并未稳定优于记忆条目数。分类器误报了百分之五十六点九的稳定、容量受限记忆库检查点；在任务成功率降至零之后，也只检出百分之十八的检查点。长期运行中，容量受限的记忆库保持稳定；唯一一次失败发生于无上限的整库提示超出模型输入容量，令牌数量增长则能直接预测该问题。

Conclusion: 全局语义密度不是可靠的记忆退化预警信号，局部拥挤指标也难以跨不同记忆设计稳定迁移。监控长期运行时，应区分记忆统计变化与实际容量约束；对于整库提示，追踪令牌增长比依赖语义几何统计更直接有效。

Abstract: Persistent textual memory can improve LLM agents, but repeated updates can erode task performance. We ask whether memory-store statistics warn before held-out accuracy declines. We registered mean pairwise cosine similarity ($ρ$) and 13 additional geometry, retrieval, and graph statistics on 805 snapshots from 13 memory methods across ALFWorld, AppWorld, ScienceWorld, WebShop, and two ARC-AGI settings. These methods store trajectories, rules, workflows, strategies, or linked records, using selective retrieval or whole-store prompting. No such statistic reliably predicted early signs of task accuracy decline, and $ρ$ had zero median lead. With the checkpoint after peak accuracy as a secondary decline point, measures of highly similar pairs and nearest-neighbor crowding changed earlier but did not consistently outperform stored-item count. We trained classifiers on this corpus and applied them unchanged to PropWorld runs of up to 5,000 crafting tasks. They falsely flagged 56.9% of checkpoints from size-limited stores with stable accuracy and detected only 18% of checkpoints after task success fell to zero. Thus, global semantic density did not provide a reliable warning, while local crowding did not transfer consistently across memory designs. In these long-horizon runs, size-limited stores remained stable, while the sole failure arose when unbounded whole-store prompting exceeded model input capacity, which token growth predicted directly.

</details>


### [85] [Fast and Scalable Abstract Interpretation for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33259)
*Avaljot Singh,Dushyant Bharadwaj,Yasmin Chandini Sarita,Heng Zhong,Charith Mendis,Gagandeep Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 斯cepter框架将神经网络抽象解释中的结构计算预先组织成可复用、可编译的张量程序，在保持精确算术下界结果一致的同时，大幅提升分析规模与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象解释可用于验证深度神经网络的性质，但精确分析开销较高。通用实现面对不同输入和待验证性质时会重复计算；结构计算与数值运算交错执行，也会降低图形处理器利用率，并限制张量编译器可整体优化的范围。

Method: 提出斯cepter框架，利用结构语义确定中间抽象值的结构，同时延后其数值求值。框架将连续操作中的结构计算合并，在认证前生成可执行张量程序；当抽象解释器和网络架构固定后，该程序可在不同权重和输入区域上复用。论文证明，在精确算术下，生成程序计算的界与通用解释器一致，并利用程序及其依赖关系支持张量编译和内存规划。

Result: 评估涵盖八种抽象解释器、四十四个神经网络，以及六种基线系统，并在图形处理器和中央处理器上进行测试。斯cepter可处理的网络规模最高达到相应基线已完成规模的百五十三倍；它能完成对残差网络五十层版和视觉几何组十六层版的深层多面体分析，而所有受测基线均未能完成。在双方均完成的配置中，相较于纽顿系统，图形处理器上的几何平均加速比为十一点九至四十四点四倍，峰值内存几何平均降低二点六倍。输入切分案例中，斯cepter证明了基线超时的六十八项性质。

Conclusion: 通过把抽象解释中的结构计算与数值求值分离，斯cepter实现了跨输入和权重的程序复用，并让张量编译器能够更充分地优化计算与内存。实验显示，该方法能显著扩展可分析网络的规模、加快验证并降低内存占用。

Abstract: Abstract interpretation can certify properties of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), but precise analyses remain expensive. General implementations repeat work when analyzing the same architecture under different inputs and properties. Interleaving this work with numerical operations limits GPU utilization and restricts how much of the analysis a tensor compiler can process together.
  We present Scepter, a framework for specializing DNN abstract interpreters through structural semantics. The semantics determine the structure of intermediate abstract values while deferring their numerical evaluation. Structural computations from successive operations can therefore be performed together before certification, generating an executable tensor program. With the abstract interpreter and DNN architecture fixed, the program can be reused with different weights and input regions. We prove that it computes the same bounds as the general interpreter in exact arithmetic. The generated program also exposes operations and their dependencies for tensor compilation and memory planning. We implement Scepter for the tensor programs that Neuton generates from ConstraintFlow specifications.
  We evaluate Scepter on eight abstract interpreters and 44 DNNs against six baseline systems on GPUs and CPUs. Scepter handles networks with up to 153x as many neurons as the largest completed by the corresponding baselines. It completes DeepPoly on ResNet50 and VGG-16, whereas none of the evaluated DeepPoly baselines completes either network. On configurations completed by both systems, GPU geometric-mean speedups over Neuton range from 11.9x to 44.4x across eight analyses, with a 2.60x geometric-mean reduction in peak memory. In an input-splitting case study, Scepter proves 68 properties for which the baselines time out.
  Code: https://github.com/uiuc-focal-lab/constraintflow.git

</details>


### [86] [Two Timescales of Forgetting in Fine-tuning of Language Models: Fast Behavioral Suppression and Slow Representational Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33302)
*Yilin Luo,Jin Hwa Lee,Andrew M. Saxe,Antonio Sclocchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，监督微调会先快速抑制模型生成某些内容，但这些内容对应的内部表征仍保留；表征遗忘则发生得更慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常先在广泛语料上预训练，再通过有限数据进行微调。然而，人们尚不清楚微调改变行为时，是否也会抹去支撑该行为的潜在表征。

Method: 研究者先让变换器模型在具有层级潜在结构的受控无上下文文法上预训练，再仅用其生成规则的受限子集进行监督微调。随后追踪被删减规则的生成行为、对应隐藏表征及网络参数变化，并在自然语言场景中检验结果。

Result: 微调后，被删减规则的生成能力会较快下降，其变化速度与词元和潜变量相关性的大小呈反向关系，但相应内部表征仍然保留。经过更长时间，随着网络参数缓慢漂移，这些表征才逐渐遗忘。自然语言实验也呈现出类似的时间尺度分离。

Conclusion: 微调造成的表面行为抑制，不等同于潜在表征被立即删除。行为改变与表征遗忘具有不同的时间尺度，这一现象可能普遍存在于语言模型的微调过程中。

Abstract: Today, a core pipeline of artificial intelligence systems is pretraining on a broad range of text corpora and then fine-tuning on specific tasks or desired behaviors. Yet we still have limited understanding of how fine-tuning on a restricted data distribution modifies representations acquired during pretraining. In particular, it remains unclear whether surface behavioral suppression happens together with the erasure of latent representations that support it. In this work, we study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) by pretraining transformers on a controlled context-free grammar with hierarchical latent structure, the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and fine-tuning with a restricted subset of its generative rules. While during pretraining the model's ability to generate with the correct grammar rules coincides with building the internal representations of the corresponding latent, we find that SFT exhibits a very different dynamics: (i) the generation of deleted rules changes on a fast timescale inversely proportional to the magnitude of the token-latent correlation; meanwhile, their corresponding representations stay intact; (ii) the hidden representations of the deleted rules become forgotten on a much longer timescale, associated with a slow drift of the network parameters. Finally, we observe analogous results in a realistic setup with natural language, suggesting that this timescale separation in forgetting is a more general phenomenon of fine-tuning language models.

</details>


### [87] [State Space Attention: Learning Context-Dependent Memory Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33625)
*Ege Çakar,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出状态空间注意力，通过学习在固定容量内保留哪些历史表示，提升有限记忆下的长上下文检索与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力的键值缓存会随上下文长度线性增长。若必须限制记忆容量，模型应保留哪些历史信息不应仅由固定规则决定，而应通过学习适应任务需求。

Method: 状态空间注意力维护数量有上限的历史表示，并使用标准注意力读取。学习型评分器会结合新到达的上下文及记忆中的其他内容，重新评估各片段的保留价值。训练时，研究者通过生成监督样本为评分器提供学习信号，并联合训练评分器与读取模块；保留策略可从可微的软加权逐步过渡到硬选择，也可从一开始就采用硬选择。固定上下文长度的变换器可视为始终保留最近词元的特例。

Result: 在检索、变量追踪、多跳推理和语言建模任务上，状态空间注意力在多数基准中接近完整注意力。面对长上下文检索，它在所有测试任务上都显著优于可读取记忆预算相同的变换器，并能匹敌或超过记忆预算约为其四倍的变换器，同时推理时记忆占用保持恒定。小规模语言模型与完整注意力之间仍存在差距，尤其是在一项长上下文综合评测基准上。研究还考察了模型训练与推理成本随上下文长度和模型规模的变化。

Conclusion: 学习式记忆保留能让固定数量的记忆词元覆盖更长上下文，并显著改善有限记忆下的检索与推理。由于读取仍采用标准注意力，该方法也有望与键值缓存淘汰策略结合；不过，其在部分长上下文评测上的能力仍未达到完整注意力水平。

Abstract: Standard attention's key-value cache grows linearly with context length. Bounding it raises the question of what a sequence model should retain for long-context reasoning. We argue this choice should be learned, and introduce State Space Attention (SSA), which learns to keep a bounded set of past representations and reads them with standard attention. A learned scorer rescores every chunk against newly arriving context and the rest of memory. A fixed-context Transformer is then the special case that always keeps the most recent tokens. We train the scorer and reader jointly, either moving gradually from a differentiable soft weighting of memories to hard retention, or with hard retention from the start. To produce signal for the scorer, we generate supervised samples during training, a technique we call recovery. We compare SSA with recurrent models, bounded-memory attention methods, and full-attention Transformers across retrieval, variable tracking, multi-hop reasoning, and language modeling. On our retrieval and reasoning benchmarks, SSA matches or comes within a few points of full attention on most tasks, far outperforms a Transformer with the same readable budget on every long-context retrieval task we evaluate, and matches or outperforms one with about 4 times that budget, while using constant inference memory. The largest remaining gap to full attention at small language model scale is on RULER. SSA makes a fixed number of tokens useful over a longer context, and since it reads memory with standard attention, we propose that it can be stacked with KV cache eviction methods. We also pretrain and post-train 400M-parameter models and measure how SSA's training and inference costs scale with context length and model size. Together, these experiments examine the capabilities and limitations of learned memory retention for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [88] [Looped Models are Fast Multi-Token Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33896)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Sean McLeish,Sehoon Kim,Jinwoo Kim,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于循环语言模型的自推测解码方法：在浅层循环中并行草拟多个未来词元，再由深层循环验证，最高可实现七点五倍加速且不损失解码结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型可以通过增加循环次数提升预测能力，而无需按比例增加独有模型参数。已有自推测解码利用浅层循环快速草拟、深层循环验证，但草拟阶段仍需调用完整参数，限制了加速效果。论文进一步观察到，浅层循环也能预测多个未来词元，因此希望并行草拟以提高推理速度。

Method: 提出“洪闸”方法：在输入后追加一组掩码词元作为后缀，并通过低秩适配训练模型，使浅层循环能够并行预测这些位置上的未来词元。随后由深层循环验证草拟结果。验证时，浅层草拟器还能利用深层前缀缓存中的计算信息，从而在保持无损解码的同时加快推理。

Result: 在七个模型、五项基准测试、多种批量大小，以及贪心解码和采样解码设置下，该方法在多数情况下是最快的无损解码方法，最高加速达七点五倍。分析表明，循环模型的全深度训练，以及草拟器对验证阶段深层前缀缓存的利用，是实现加速的关键。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，浅层循环不仅可用于草拟单个词元，也可并行草拟多个未来词元。结合深层验证与前缀缓存，这种方法能显著提升循环语言模型的推理效率，同时保持解码结果不变。

Abstract: Looped language models can adapt computation for predicting a token without a proportional rescaling in unique model parameters by adjusting the number of loops. This property has been used for self-speculative decoding, taking shallow loops as fast drafters and deep loops as verifiers, which is effective since drafting still uses full parameters. In this paper, we show that shallow loops can be also predictive of several future tokens and leverage this to drastically accelerate the inference of looped language models. To that end, we present Floodgates, a simple self-speculative decoding method that appends a block of mask tokens as a suffix and learns via low-rank adaptation to draft these tokens in parallel. Across seven models, five benchmarks, a range of batch sizes, and both greedy and sampled decoding, Floodgates is the fastest lossless decoding method in most cases, attaining up to 7.5x acceleration. Through analysis, we show that this speedup is enabled by all-depth training of looped models, with the shallow drafter's ability to leverage full-depth computation by conditioning on deep prefix cache evaluated during verification.

</details>


### [89] [DLaR: Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33962)
*Xiaocheng Luo,Peng Xu,Zaifu Zhan,Xiangyu Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态随机性控制框架，通过按推理步骤调节扰动强度、并在训练过程中逐步降低基础随机性，提升潜在推理的探索能力与连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理使大语言模型能够在连续嵌入空间中进行推理，但确定性推理难以探索多样化的推理路径。已有随机化方法虽然能增强探索，却通常对所有步骤施加相同程度的随机性，且不受约束的扰动可能破坏推理连贯性。因此，需要根据模型状态和训练阶段灵活控制随机性。

Method: 作者提出动态随机性控制框架，并在组相对策略优化强化学习框架中结合两种机制：一是自适应随机性调节，在每个潜在推理步骤中依据置信度与熵的融合信号调整扰动强度；模型不确定时增强探索，较有把握时则维持稳定。二是训练感知随机性调节，按照课程式计划逐步降低基础随机性。两者分别作用于推理步骤层面和训练过程层面，形成层次化的探索与利用策略。

Result: 在四个分布内和三个分布外的数学推理基准上，该方法在不同模型规模下取得最高平均表现。以通过率指标计，三十二亿参数规模的模型在单次采样与八次采样设置下分别达到百分之二十七点九和百分之五十点六；十五亿参数规模的模型分别达到百分之四十六点八和百分之六十八点二。

Conclusion: 研究表明，随机性不宜在所有推理步骤和训练阶段保持恒定。结合逐步自适应扰动与训练期间的随机性衰减，有望在扩大推理探索的同时维持连贯性，并提升数学推理表现。

Abstract: Latent reasoning enables LLMs to operate in continuous embedding space, yet existing methods suffer from deterministic inference that precludes exploration of diverse reasoning paths. While recent approaches inject stochasticity to restore exploration, we identify two fundamental limitations: \textit{uniform stochasticity} across all steps is suboptimal, and unconstrained perturbations disrupt reasoning coherence. We propose Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning (DLaR), a framework with two complementary mechanisms that operate at different temporal granularities within the GRPO reinforcement learning framework. At the token level, Adaptive Stochasticity Modulation (ASM) dynamically adjusts perturbation intensity at each latent step based on a confidence-entropy fusion signal, increasing exploration when the model is uncertain and preserving stability when confident. At the training level, Training-Aware Stochasticity Tuning (TAST) progressively decays the base stochasticity level following a curriculum schedule. Through this dual-level design, DLaR establishes a hierarchical exploration-exploitation strategy that operates at both token and training levels. Extensive experiments on four in-distribution and three out-of-distribution mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that DLaR achieves the highest average performance across model scales, reaching 27.9%/50.6% Pass@1/Pass@8 on Llama-3.2-1B and 46.8%/68.2% on Qwen2.5-1.5B. Code: \href{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}.

</details>


### [90] [Improving Tail-Augmented Speculative Drafting with Hidden State Mixing and Gradient Detach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34092)
*Yaniv Galron,Rabeeh Karimi Mahabadi,Maor Ashkenazi,Roger Waleffe,Talor Abramovich,Tiyasa Mitra,Yoav Miron,Dor Tsur,Sanjeev Satheesh,Jimmy Zhang,Jiaji Huang,Yonatan Geifman,Mostofa Patwary,Mohammad Shoeybi,Bita Rouhani,Ran Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两种仅在训练阶段使用的方法，改善自回归草稿器的训练与推理匹配；它们不增加推理开销，其中隐藏状态混合尤其适合较长草稿，隐藏状态梯度截断更适合较短草稿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多令牌预测模块和鹰式草稿器会复用目标模型的隐藏状态进行投机解码。但训练时输入的隐藏状态序列，与推理时由目标模型前缀和逐步生成的隐藏状态尾部组成的序列不同，造成训练—推理不匹配。已有的训练时测试方法会显式模拟推理过程，但需要额外显存和专门的注意力掩码。

Method: 提出两种训练专用方法。隐藏状态混合：针对同一预测目标，从骨干模型及不同预测步骤的隐藏状态中采样混合输入，使草稿器接触多种状态组合，而不显式模拟推理。隐藏状态梯度截断：随机阻断隐藏状态和嵌入路径上的梯度，同时保持前向计算不变。两种方法均不增加推理成本。

Result: 联合预训练与监督微调的实验显示，隐藏状态梯度截断在草稿前部位置取得最高接受率，隐藏状态混合则在后部表现最佳，因此前者更适合短草稿服务，后者更适合长草稿服务。在四种内嵌式模型及一款深度探索模型上，冻结骨干模型后使用隐藏状态混合增强多令牌预测，相比联合训练的多令牌预测，平均接受长度最高提升百分之五十二点二。对于鹰式草稿器，隐藏状态混合的接受表现可与训练时测试相当；在一项四步、八十亿参数模型的比较中，其训练峰值预留显存降低百分之四十八。

Conclusion: 无需改变推理流程或增加推理开销，仅通过训练阶段调整隐藏状态输入或梯度传播，就能有效改善自回归草稿器。隐藏状态混合在长草稿及显存效率方面具有优势，隐藏状态梯度截断则更有利于短草稿的早期接受率。

Abstract: Multi-token prediction (MTP) modules and EAGLE-style drafters support efficient speculative decoding by reusing target-model hidden states. Their autoregressive use, however, introduces a training-inference mismatch: training supplies sequences of hidden states from the backbone or a single MTP step, whereas inference attends to a target-model prefix followed by a growing tail of recurrently generated MTP states. EAGLE-3's training-time test (TTT) explicitly simulates this behavior during training, at the cost of additional memory and specialized attention masks. We introduce two training-only methods for improving autoregressive drafting. Hidden State Mixing (HSM) samples among backbone and MTP hidden states aligned to the same prediction target, exposing the drafter to mixtures of states from different MTP steps without explicitly simulating inference. Hidden State Gradient Detach (HSGD) is a regularization method that stochastically blocks gradients through hidden state and embedding pathways while preserving the forward computation. Neither method adds inference cost. In joint pretraining followed by supervised fine-tuning, HSGD yields the highest acceptance rates at early draft positions, while HSM performs best later in the draft. These acceptance rate trends favor HSM for long-draft-length serving and HSGD for short-draft-length serving. Furthermore, across four Nemotron models and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, HSM-based MTP boosting with a frozen backbone improves mean acceptance length by up to 52.2% over the co-trained MTP. For EAGLE-3 drafters, HSM offers competitive acceptance with TTT, with 48% lower peak reserved training memory in a four-step Qwen3-8B comparison. HSM implementations are publicly available in Megatron-LM and NVIDIA Model Optimizer.

</details>


### [91] [Single-Database Rewards Are Hackable: Counterfactual Execution for Trustworthy Text-to-SQL RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34363)
*Shreshth Rai,Rishii Kumar Singh,Aksh Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在文本转查询任务中，利用隐藏的反事实数据库副本检验查询，可减少错误查询和奖励投机被误判为正确；但这种奖励在强化学习训练中的收益有限，且单次随机种子的比较可能得出相反结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习奖励通常只检查查询在原数据库上的结果是否与参考答案一致。若数据库恰好缺少能暴露查询缺陷的数据，错误查询也可能碰巧得到相同结果，从而获得奖励，导致奖励信号失真并鼓励投机行为。

Method: 提出反事实数据库奖励：将最终查询同时运行于若干由代码生成、对训练者隐藏的数据库副本上。严格形式要求查询在所有副本上的结果都匹配才给奖励；均值形式则按匹配副本的比例给分。研究还比较了不同副本生成方式，并在一个受控实验中，以三种随机种子比较基于均值形式奖励的组相对策略优化训练。

Result: 在一千三百零二道合成审计题上，严格形式将错误查询获得全额奖励的比例从百分之二十九点五降至百分之十六点八，将奖励投机的比例从百分之五十四点一降至百分之一点九；二千八百四十七个正确查询中没有一个被拒绝。对生成器而言，即使不利用题目所属类别的信息，效果也相近。训练实验中，均值形式未提升领域内准确率，准确率为百分之八点四，对照组为百分之十一点七；在蜘蛛基准上则提高一点六个百分点。两种奖励对百分之五至七的训练样本给出不同判断。此外，有一个随机种子使两项性能差异的方向都反转。

Conclusion: 反事实数据库能够有效缓解单一数据库评测造成的偶然匹配，并显著抑制错误查询获得奖励；不过，评测防护效果并不必然转化为训练准确率提升。由于两种奖励在多数样本上判断一致、训练结果对随机种子敏感，评估奖励方案时应进行多种子实验，避免依赖单次结果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for text-to-SQL agents pays a query when its result matches the reference on one database. However, a wrong query can match by luck when the data lack the rows that expose its bug. We study a counterfactual-database (CF) reward that also runs the final query on a few hidden, code-generated copies of the database and pays only if all results match (strict form) or by the share matched (mean form). On 1,302 synthetic audit questions, the strict form lowers the share of buggy queries paid in full from 29.5% to 16.8% and of reward hacks from 54.1% to 1.9%, rejecting 0 of 2,847 correct queries; copies from a family-blind generator do about as well. In a controlled GRPO comparison on a 3.35B model with three seeds per arm, training on the mean form did not improve in-domain accuracy (8.4% vs 11.7%), because the two rewards disagreed on only 5-7% of rollouts; on Spider it gained 1.6 points. One seed reverses both signs, so single-seed reward comparisons mislead. Code: https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards ; data, models, demo: https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql

</details>


### [92] [Strong Starts, Simple Recipes: Unified Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation from Multimodal Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34397)
*Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Yuqi Xu,Yongfu Zhu,Zelin Peng,Ji Zhao,Shuai Dong,Wenpu Liu,Caoyuan Ma,Xuehui Wang,Ziyue Wang,Weichu Xie,Haiyuan Wan,Ngai Wong,Tong He,Wenqi Shao,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出统一多教师在线策略蒸馏：先将同一基础模型训练出的领域专家进行均匀合并，再以简洁的蒸馏流程整合能力；该方法在五个多模态领域、十九项基准测试中表现突出，且训练效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师在线策略蒸馏研究主要改进蒸馏过程，却通常从基础模型或多领域监督微调模型启动，把能力整合的重任留给后续蒸馏。论文因此将学生模型的初始状态视为关键设计变量，探索能否通过更强的起点降低蒸馏负担、提升整合效果。

Method: 提出统一多教师在线策略蒸馏。首先，对来自同一基础模型的多个领域专家进行无需训练的均匀参数合并，以此初始化统一学生模型；随后进行短程蒸馏，采用均匀领域采样和全词表反向库尔贝克—莱布勒散度，不使用采样计划、逐词加权或词表截断等额外机制。

Result: 在五个多模态领域和十九项基准测试上的实验显示：未经训练的均匀合并模型，已达到基础模型经过两百次蒸馏更新后的得分；短程蒸馏后，其表现可媲美得分更高的搜索式初始化方法。蒸馏提升了所有领域的表现，且在领域教师仍领先的领域增益最大。均匀采样与全词表反向散度匹配或超过所有受测替代方案。相同训练预算下，该方法优于开放式多教师在线策略蒸馏，并比其公开代码快三点三倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明，优质初始化比复杂的后续训练策略更为关键。将同源领域专家均匀合并，再配合短程、简洁的多教师蒸馏，能够有效整合多领域能力，并兼顾性能与训练效率。作者计划公开训练框架、领域专家检查点及统一学生模型。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates separately trained domain specialists into a single unified model. Existing methods focus on refining the distillation procedure while initializing the student from a base model or a multi-domain SFT checkpoint, which leaves capability integration largely to distillation itself. Instead, we treat the starting point as a design variable and propose Unified MOPD (Uni-MOPD), which pairs a strong start with a simple recipe: a training-free uniform merge of specialists from the same base model initializes the student, and short MOPD with uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL refines it. Across five multimodal domains and 19 benchmarks, our study establishes three findings. (1) The starting point matters more than subsequent training choices: before any training, the uniform merge already matches the score that the base model reaches after 200 MOPD updates, and after short distillation it matches higher-scoring, search-based starts. (2) Short MOPD recovers most of the capabilities the merge leaves unintegrated; it improves every domain, with the largest gains where the domain teacher still leads. (3) From a strong start, uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL match or exceed every evaluated sampling strategy and divergence, without schedules, per-token weighting, or truncation hyperparameters. Uni-MOPD outperforms Open-MOPD at the same training budget while training 3.3 times faster than its released code. We will release the training framework, specialist checkpoints, and unified student.

</details>


### [93] [Any-Rate: Half-Bit Multi-Precision LLM Quantization with Coded Bit-Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34402)
*Hoonki Lee,Haechan Shin,Hyuntaek Jung,Yeonu Choi,Sangyeob Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法从单一模型检查点支持二至八位量化，并在二至四位之间以半位为步长调整；在低位宽精度、解码速度和估算能耗方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套多精度量化能够通过读取权重的前导位，从单一检查点提供不同精度，适合设备资源受限且预算变化的部署场景。但现有方法通常只支持整数位宽，导致相邻精度间存在较大精度差距，且二位量化时性能下降严重。

Method: 提出“任意速率”量化方法，支持二至八位精度，并在二至四位范围内以半位为步长。该方法将部分位平面限制为由八个权重组成的扩展汉明码字，每个权重块对应半位开销；同一行的不同精度共享一个查找表，并对各精度分别设置优化目标进行联合编码，以减轻共享位平面造成的损失。

Result: 在四种大语言模型上的实验显示，该方法在二位和三位精度下达到多精度方法中的领先准确率：二位时保留半精度浮点模型准确率的百分之六十九；四位时与最佳方法的差距不超过一个百分点。其解码速度与现有内核大致相当；基于低功耗双倍数据速率内存的能耗模型估算，在准确率相同的条件下，能耗最多可降低百分之二十七。

Conclusion: 该方法通过编码约束、行级查找表共享和多精度联合优化，改善了低位宽量化，尤其是二位量化的精度，并提供细粒度的精度调节。结果表明，它有望在设备端部署中更灵活地平衡模型准确率、内存占用与能耗。

Abstract: Nested multi-precision quantization derives multiple precisions from a single checkpoint by reading only the leading bits of each weight, which makes it attractive for on-device deployment under varying memory and energy budgets. However, existing methods offer only integer bits, leaving large accuracy gaps between adjacent precisions and severe degradation at 2 bits. To address this, we propose Any-Rate, which serves 2-8 bits from one checkpoint, in half-bit steps over 2-4 bits. Any-Rate restricts some bit-planes to extended Hamming codewords in 8-weight blocks (0.5 bits per weight), shares one LUT per row across precisions, and encodes all precisions jointly with a separate target for each, reducing the loss from sharing bit-planes. Across four LLMs, Any-Rate achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among multi-precision methods at 2 and 3 bits, keeping 69% of FP16 accuracy at 2 bits, and is within one point of the best at 4 bits. It decodes about as fast as existing kernels, and an LPDDR energy model estimates up to 27% less energy at equal accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/hoonki2002/Any-Rate.

</details>


### [94] [When the Proxy Is Wrong: Mismatch-Corrected Bandits for Adaptive Data Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34707)
*Amine Saadi,Elissa Mhanna,Raphaël Féraud,Gwénolé Lecorvé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将代理模型与目标模型之间的奖励差异纳入训练数据分配，通过配对审计校正代理模型的判断，并在训练过程中合理分配审计次数，从而提升目标模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分配常借助成本较低的小型代理模型，为大型目标模型挑选训练数据。但两者对数据干预效果的排序可能不同，单纯依赖代理模型会导致次优分配，甚至产生随训练轮次线性增长的遗憾。论文旨在识别并校正这种模型间差异，同时控制对目标模型进行评估的成本。

Method: 作者将数据分配建模为多保真度老虎机问题，每个选项对应一种数据干预。代理模型每轮都提供奖励观测，目标模型则仅在少量审计轮次中对同一次更新提供配对观测。利用配对结果估计各选项的差异，并以残差修正代理奖励；审计重点放在不确定的决策上，且审计预算分散安排于整个训练过程。理论分析给出了遗憾分解与平稳环境下的下界；面对突变环境，则采用滑动窗口策略。

Result: 理论结果表明，仅依赖代理模型可能导致线性遗憾，且必须进行最低数量的配对审计；若在突变环境中过早耗尽审计预算，也可能造成线性遗憾。滑动窗口策略的遗憾上界为变化点数量、训练轮数及其对数项乘积的平方根量级。实验在民事评论分布偏移数据集上进行闭环验证：由小型双向编码器指导大型双向编码器训练。差异校正相较于目标模型预算相同的仅目标模型策略，提高了最差群体准确率；在审计次数相同的条件下，将审计分散到训练过程中可提升约六点三至十点五个百分点。

Conclusion: 代理模型与目标模型之间的差异不应被视为统一偏移或默认保留排序，而应通过同一更新上的配对审计逐项估计。差异校正与审计预算的合理调度，既有理论上的必要性，也能在实验中改善目标模型的最差群体表现。

Abstract: Data allocation methods often use a small proxy model to select training data for a larger target model. However, this proxy can rank data differently from the target, leading to suboptimal training performance. To address this problem, we model the mismatch between the proxy and target models and incorporate it into the allocation decision. To do so, the problem is cast as a multi-fidelity bandit, where each arm is a data intervention. The proxy reward is observed every round, whereas the target reward is revealed only on rare audits of the same update. These paired observations identify arm-wise mismatches without assuming a shared offset or preservation of rankings. Mismatch-corrected bandits correct the proxy with paired residuals, audit only uncertain decisions, and pace the audit budget over training. From a theoretical viewpoint, we prove a regret decomposition and lower bounds under stationarity. These bounds show that proxy-only allocation can incur linear regret and that a minimum number of paired audits is necessary. Under abrupt changes, using the audit budget early can also incur linear regret. A sliding-window policy that spreads audits over training achieves regret of order $\sqrt{Γ_T T\log T}$ where $Γ_T$ denotes the number of change points. Experimentally, we test the approach in a closed-loop proof of concept on CivilComments-WILDS, where BERT-Small guides the training of BERT-Large. Mismatch correction improves worst-group accuracy over a target-only policy with the same target-model budget. Spreading audits over training improves worst-group accuracy by 6.3 to 10.5 percentage points at the same audit count.

</details>


### [95] [Reading Fine-Tuning Diffs Through Lenses: Model Diffing for Backdoor Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34758)
*Maximilian Petrowitsch,Ben Connors,Chris Kapulkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文建立统一的基于词元读出的评分体系，用于比较微调后模型与基座模型之间的差异，以检测后门。基于权重差异并通过改进的「杰透镜」解码，整体表现最强，但不同检测方法面对不同规避手段时各有弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调可能在模型中植入后门，而现有检测方法依据权重、激活或输出等不同信息，难以直接比较其效果，也可能被针对性规避。论文旨在统一评估标准，并改进权重与激活类方法，考察各种方法在不同攻击条件下的可靠性。

Method: 作者提出基于词元读出的通用评分体系，在实验中比较后门模型与其基座模型。评估并改进权重差异和激活类检测方法，将「杰透镜」及其改进版「杰加加透镜」用于解码；同时与基于输出的自适应差异定位方法和差异挖掘方法比较，并测试隐藏单元置换、仅训练读取残差流的层，以及对干净输入上的后门词概率施加惩罚等规避情形。

Result: 在所有测试设置中，使用「杰加加透镜」都提升了权重类方法的表现；「杰透镜」则提升了其在单层内的排名。最强的单一方法是通过基座模型的「杰加加透镜」解码权重差异：在十二种设置中的十一种，能将指向后门的词元排进前十。对干净输入上的后门词概率施加惩罚，会显著削弱输出类和激活类方法，但不影响权重类方法；反之，置换隐藏单元或只训练读取残差流的层会掩盖权重类方法可识别的后门，却不会同样削弱输出类和激活类方法。

Conclusion: 改进后的权重差异解码是实验中最有效的单项检测方法，但其优势并非普遍可靠。权重类方法与输出、激活类方法对不同规避手段的敏感性互补；目前没有任何一种方法能够同时抵御文中测试的两类攻击，因此实际检测应考虑组合多种方法。

Abstract: We introduce a common scoring system based on token readouts to evaluate weight, activation and output-based model diffing methods for backdoor detection in fine-tuning processes. We also improve existing weight and activation based methods by incorporating the $J$-lens and its refinement, the $J{+}{+}$-lens. We test our setup in experiments where we compare a backdoored model with the base model from which it was fine-tuned. Our results show that incorporating the $J{+}{+}$-lens improves the weight-based method in all our settings, while the $J$-lens improves its ranks within single layers. The strongest single method is $ΔW J{+}{+}$, the weight difference decoded through the $J{+}{+}$-lens of the base model, which ranks a token naming the backdoor among its first ten tokens in 11 of our 12 settings. Moreover, we show that output and activation based methods such as ADL and Diff Mining can easily break if the backdoored model is penalised during fine-tuning for giving the backdoor's words probability on clean inputs, whereas the weight-based methods are not affected. Conversely, permuting the hidden units or training only the layers that read the residual stream hides the backdoor from the weight-based methods but not from ADL and Diff Mining. No single method resists both attacks.

</details>


### [96] [One Model, Many Modes: Evolving System Prompt Ensembles for Creative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34760)
*Daiwei Chen,Anda He,Binwei Yao,Junjie Hu,Ramya Korlakai Vinayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的系统提示词集成优化方法，在推理时兼顾生成质量、新颖性与多样性，提升大语言模型的创造性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐可能使大语言模型的输出分布集中于少数高奖励回答，造成模式坍缩并限制创造性。现有推理时方法往往偏重多样性，却较少确保每条回答同时具备较高质量和新颖性；而实际使用中，用户常需要的是单条出色且原创的回答。

Method: 研究将创造性生成形式化为多目标分布优化问题，并提出无需训练的系统提示词集成优化框架。该框架通过提示词演化与贪心最大—最小选择，在质量约束下挑选行为差异显著的系统提示词；生成时则为每条回答均匀抽取一个提示词，以诱导不同的输出分布。

Result: 在四类质量要求逐步提高的创造性生成任务及三种开放权重大语言模型上，该方法在十二种设置中的八种，按质量、多样性和新颖性的调和平均指标排名第一；没有其他基线方法超过两种。相较于基础采样，评审模型给出的多样性平均分从一点九二升至三点四三，新颖性从一点零零升至三点三四，质量则从三点七二降至三点三零，均采用五分制。研究还发现，扩大优化后的提示词集成规模会增加不同提示词所诱导的回答分布之间的差异。

Conclusion: 结果表明，优化系统提示词集成是一种有前景的推理时创造性增强方案，能够显著提高多样性和新颖性，同时以一定的质量下降为代价。该方法无需额外训练，并通过控制提示词选择来拓展模型的生成行为。

Abstract: Preference-aligned LLMs can exhibit mode collapse, concentrating their output distributions around a narrow set of high-reward responses. This limits creative generation, which requires quality, novelty, and diversity. Existing test-time methods often emphasize diversity, giving less attention to whether individual responses are both high-quality and novel. This distinction matters in practical use, where users may seek a single compelling, original response. We formalize creative generation as a multi-objective distributional optimization problem and introduce SEPO, a training-free framework for System Prompt Ensemble Optimization. The framework combines prompt evolution with greedy max-min selection to identify behaviorally distinct system prompts under a quality constraint, then uniformly samples one prompt per response at inference time. Across four creative generation tasks with increasing quality requirements and three open-weight models, SEPO ranks first in 8 of 12 settings on the harmonic mean of quality, diversity, and novelty, while no baseline leads in more than two. On average, it raises LLM-judged diversity from 1.92 to 3.43 and novelty from 1.00 to 3.34 over base sampling (1-5 scale), with quality decreasing from 3.72 to 3.30. We further show that expanding the optimized ensemble increases separation among prompt-conditioned response distributions. These results support system prompt ensemble optimization as a promising approach to improving LLM creativity at inference time.

</details>


### [97] [When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34777)
*Haowen Hou,Yicheng Liu,Zhen Huang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种在线构造局部训练目标的方法，利用当前策略失败轨迹中的候选片段及其后续表现改进长程智能体任务的强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程智能体任务中，基于可验证奖励的分组强化学习常遇到整组轨迹全部失败的情况，导致组内没有可用于策略更新的正向奖励信号。使用更强模型提供完整解答虽可缓解这一问题，但生成成本高，也会让学习过程依赖较长、与当前策略分布不一致的轨迹。

Method: 对于全部失败的轨迹组，方法在线从当前策略的一条失败轨迹中选择拼接点，再由提议模型从该点生成多个长度受限的候选选项。随后，以当前策略多次续写各选项，估计其价值；将价值为正的选项转化为按回报加权的局部训练目标，并据此训练当前策略。

Result: 在两类策略及两项长程任务——软件工程任务和深度研究任务——构成的四种设置中，该方法均优于组相对策略优化基线和完整轨迹引导基线，相较组相对策略优化提升了二点八至九点八个百分点。在最初失败的软件工程任务上，毕业率提高了十点八个百分点。进一步实验还显示，该方法在提升表现的同时减少了昂贵的提议模型计算。

Conclusion: 该方法通过评估当前策略能够延续的局部候选，而非依赖昂贵的完整解决方案，为全失败轨迹组提供了可学习的正向信号。实验结果表明，这种局部、在线的训练方式能有效提升长程智能体任务表现，并提高提议模型计算的使用效率。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often yields all-fail groups on long-horizon agent tasks: every sampled trajectory receives zero reward, leaving group-relative methods without a reward-based policy gradient. A common remedy supplies complete solutions from a stronger model, but full trajectories are expensive and expose the learner to long off-policy histories. We introduce SPLICE, which constructs local training targets from failed current-policy trajectories online. For each all-fail group, a proposal model selects a splice point in a current-policy trajectory and generates several bounded options from that point. We estimate each option's value using repeated current-policy continuations, convert positive estimates into a return-weighted local target, and fit the current policy on the options. We evaluate SPLICE across two policy families on two long-horizon agent tasks, Software Engineering with SWE-bench Verified and Deep Research with BrowseComp-Plus. SPLICE outperforms GRPO and full-trajectory guidance baselines in all four settings, improving over GRPO by 2.8--9.8 percentage points. On initially failed SWE tasks, it raises GRPO's graduation rate by 10.8 percentage points. Further experiments show that SPLICE improves performance while reducing costly proposal-model computation.

</details>


### [98] [Mixture-of-Loops Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34923)
*Lianghui Zhu,Shijie Wang,Xinggang Wang,Jinze Bai,Bo Wang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通过跨循环复用键值记忆提升表达能力与计算效率的循环式变换器，并在多种规模和任务上取得更高训练效率与更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式变换器通过重复使用共享层提升模型表达能力，但重复计算会降低效率。论文聚焦于循环之间应传递什么信息，以便更有效地复用计算结果，并突破训练时循环次数对推理能力的限制。

Method: 论文提出混合循环变换器，以注意力机制中的键和值作为跨深度、跨循环选择性复用的记忆。其残差流版本保留残差状态并读取各循环的局部键值，以增强表达能力；键值版本则从嵌入重新开始，结合当前循环的局部键值与前序循环较早位置的记忆键值，从而避免相同位置的跨循环依赖，并配合循环强制实现并行训练及超出训练循环范围的单次遍历解码。进一步提出Ｕ形结构，将记忆构建与预测读取分开，并跳过不影响预测的解码遍历。

Result: 在四千亿词元预训练中，Ｕ形模型在七亿、十五亿参数的稠密模型及三十亿参数的混合专家模型上优于对照模型。七亿参数模型达到一点七倍验证交叉熵词元效率；三十亿参数混合专家模型在十个领域的困惑度降低零点七五，十项任务准确率提高二点一个百分点。对八亿参数基座模型进行一千亿词元持续预训练时，十一类领域的交叉熵均低于匹配基线，平均词元效率提高一点四倍。小规模模型采用混合解码后，推理延迟与非循环模型基本相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以键值记忆为循环间传递信息的核心机制，并通过Ｕ形结构减少无效解码计算，可以兼顾循环模型的表达能力、训练效率和推理速度。该方法在多种模型规模与评测设置中展现出稳定收益。

Abstract: Looped Transformers boost expressivity by reusing shared layers across loops, yet at the cost of efficiency. Pushing their expressivity-efficiency frontier requires rethinking the core mechanism connecting adjacent loops: What should be passed as a Transformer loops? We introduce Mixture-of-Loops Transformer (MoLT), whose Mixture-of-Loops Attention (MoLA) uses keys and values (KV) as memory for selective reuse across depths and loops. Under loop residual, MoLT-R retains the residual stream and reads local KV across loops for expressivity. Under loop KV, MoLT-KV instead restarts from embeddings and reads current-loop local KV with preceding-loop memory KV at earlier positions for efficiency. MoLT-KV eliminates same-position cross-loop dependence and, paired with loop forcing for parallel training, unlocks single-traversal Mixture-of-Loops (MoL) decoding beyond the training loop horizon. To balance efficiency and quality, we introduce U-shaped MoLT (U-MoLT), which separates memory construction from prediction readout and skips unused decoder traversals without changing predictions. In 400B-token pretraining, U-MoLT outperforms OLMo3 across 700M/1.5B dense and 3B MoE models, achieving 1.7x validation cross-entropy (CE) token efficiency at 700M while improving 3B MoE 10-domain perplexity by 0.75 and 10-task accuracy by 2.1 percentage points. In 100B-token continued pretraining of Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, our U-MoLT adaptation lowers CE on all 11 domains over the matched baseline with 1.4x mean token efficiency. At inference, U-MoLT with MoL-Hybrid-2 matches non-looped OLMo3 latency at 996.6 vs. 1,000.6 ms on S-size (~113M) models. Code is available at https://github.com/hustvl/MoLT

</details>


### [99] [Can image generation help visual reasoning? An empirical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34935)
*Xueyang Yu,Yifan Jing,Taiming Lu,Linrong Cai,Zhipeng Cai,Yunyang Xiong,Gabriel Sarch,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，让模型生成辅助图像再回答视觉问题，整体上往往会降低准确率；多次编辑的效果则因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常借助文字进行推理，因此研究者提出，多模态模型或许也能通过图像辅助思考。论文检验模型能否先描述所需的辅助图像，再结合原图与生成图像回答视觉问题，以及这种做法是否能提升表现。

Method: 研究测试了十三组视觉语言模型与图像生成器的组合：模型先描述需要的辅助图像，生成器据此生成图像，模型再结合原图和生成图像作答。作者在十项任务上比较生成辅助图像与不生成时的准确率，并考察重复编辑图像以及在相同规划提示下加入生成图像的影响。

Result: 加入生成图像后，平均准确率下降三点七个百分点，十项任务中有九项出现下降。两种统一多模态模型各自在八项任务上准确率降低。在一项基准测试中，即使使用相同的规划提示，加入生成图像仍使准确率下降十点四七个百分点。反复编辑图像的结果因任务而异：图表问答与数学视觉推理任务的表现仍低于不生成图像时的基线，而另一项任务在编辑十次后提升三个百分点。

Conclusion: 生成辅助图像并不能普遍改善视觉问答，甚至可能引入误导并损害准确率。其效果取决于具体任务与编辑过程，因此不宜假设图像生成天然有助于多模态推理；实际应用中应逐任务验证收益。

Abstract: Language models think in words; then multimodal models might think in images. We test whether a generated helper improves the answer to a visual question. A model describes the helper it needs and then answers using the original and generated images together. Across 13 vision-language model and generator pairs, generation lowers mean accuracy by 3.7 percentage points, with declines on nine of ten tasks. Two unified models, BAGEL and Janus-Pro-7B, each lose accuracy on eight tasks. On VSTAR, adding a generated image to the same planning hint lowers accuracy by 10.47 points. Repeated image edits yield different outcomes across tasks. ChartQAPro and MathVista stay below their no-generation baselines, while ERQA gains three points after ten edits.

</details>


### [100] [Attributing Agentic Failure in Reasoning Vision-Language Models with Interactive 2D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33162)
*Pranav Guruprasad,Sean Rivera,Helen Lu,Arushi Jain,Hangliang Ren,Harshvardhan Sikka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文介绍一种用于分析智能体失败原因的交互式迷宫基准。三种视觉语言模型仅完成150次评测中的6次，且没有完成任何包含开关—闸门机制的关卡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准通常只报告总体成功率，无法区分失败究竟源于误读环境、未理解机制、操作顺序错误，还是出错后未能恢复；这些问题需要不同的改进方案。本文因此旨在构建便于归因分析、而非单纯排名的基准。

Method: 提出多网络2.0网格世界，一个可交互的迷宫环境。环境包含钥匙—门、开关—闸门、干扰物与诱饵，以及这些机制的有序组合；各类机制可独立增删或修改，同时保持渲染方式、动作空间和评分函数不变。研究通过对位置、朝向、物品持有情况及机制状态构成的联合状态进行广度优先搜索，计算每个关卡的最优动作数，以此确定难度并评估未完成的交互过程。评测方案固定为15个留出迷宫、共540个回合，并在50个迷宫上评测三种视觉语言模型。

Result: 三种模型在150次评测中总共完成6次；50个迷宫中有45个未被任何模型解出。所有包含开关—闸门机制的回合均未成功，显示该机制对受测模型构成了显著挑战。

Conclusion: 该基准通过在固定环境与评分条件下独立控制机制，并提供精确的最优动作数，为区分和研究智能体的具体失败原因提供了基础。初步结果表明，受测模型整体解谜能力有限，尤其难以处理开关—闸门机制；不过，这些发现来自早期版本和有限模型评测，仍需更广泛实验验证。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks report a success rate that confounds distinct causes of failure: misreading the environment, never working out how components of the environment operates, operating the components in the wrong order, and failing to recover from a mistake, each of which calls for a different fix. We present an early preview of MultiNet v2.0-Gridworld - an interactive maze environment built for attribution rather than ranking. Four different types of mechanisms, namely key-door pairs, switch-gate pairs, distractors and decoys, and ordered combinations of these mechanisms, can be added, modified, or removed independently while the renderer, the action space, and the scoring function stay fixed. A breadth-first search over the joint state of position, facing direction, inventory, and mechanism state gives every instance an exact optimal action count, which sets its difficulty and grades unfinished episodes. We fix the evaluation protocol on 15 held-out mazes over 540 episodes, then evaluate three vision-language models on 50 mazes. They solve 6 of 150 episodes, 45 of the 50 mazes were not solved by any model, and no episode containing a switch-gate mechanism was solved.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum Machine Learning for Signature Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33163)
*Nabila,Md Farhan Shahriyar,Nowshin Tabassum Puspa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离线手写签名防伪的量子机器学习方案，并提出结合图像特征提取与量子降维的混合流程；该流程在五折交叉验证中的平均准确率为百分之九十一点二六。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线签名仅提供静态图像，缺少笔压、书写时序和笔画顺序等动态信息，因此难以区分熟练伪造与真实签名。论文旨在探索量子机器学习及其混合模型能否提升此类二分类任务的识别效果。

Method: 研究首先将ZZ特征映射量子核与三种经典分类器结合，分别采用近邻分类、支持向量分类和随机森林。随后提出更深层的混合流程：冻结高效网络B0作为图像特征提取器，将其生成的一千二百八十维特征经量子兼容的主成分投影压缩至适合量子比特寄存器的子空间，再通过近邻规则判定类别。实验使用公开的签名图像数据集，并报告六种量子方案的消融比较、混淆矩阵、二维嵌入可视化及各类别的精确率、召回率和调和平均数。

Result: 所提混合流程在单次留出测试集上的准确率为百分之九十二点六二；分层五折交叉验证的平均准确率为百分之九十一点二六，标准差为一点一三个百分点。摘要未给出六种量子方案之间的具体对比数值，也未列出各类别指标的详细结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将预训练图像特征提取与量子嵌入结合，是离线签名防伪的一种有潜力的方案；其量子嵌入步骤也被设计为便于迁移至含噪中等规模量子硬件。不过，现有证据来自单一公开数据集，且测试集结果仅对应一次划分，实际泛化能力仍需在更多数据集和独立测试条件下验证。

Abstract: Offline handwritten signatures remain a primary behavioural biometric in legal and financial documents. Detecting skilled forgeries from a static raster image is hard because pen pressure, timing and stroke order are missing. This work studies a family of quantum machine learning pipelines for offline signature forgery detection on a public Kaggle corpus of images split into two classes. We pair a ZZ feature map quantum kernel with three classical learners: K-Nearest Neighbours, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. We then propose a deeper hybrid that uses EfficientNetB0 as a frozen feature extractor, reduces the resulting 1280 dimensional representation to a qubit register sized subspace through a quantum compatible principal component projection and decides class membership with a nearest neighbour rule. On a single held out test fold the proposed hybrid reaches 92.62 percent accuracy. On stratified five fold cross validation the same pipeline yields a mean accuracy of 91.26 percent with a standard deviation of 1.13 percent. The pipeline keeps a quantum embedding step that is portable to NISQ hardware. We report a controlled ablation across six quantum variants, confusion matrices, a t SNE projection and per class precision, recall and F1 score.

</details>


### [102] [Code availability is not enough: schemas for documenting and extending data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33167)
*H. Sherry Zhang,Roger D. Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以自然语言决策模式描述数据分析的方法，可据此自动生成并验证分析代码，也能扩展出多种备选分析方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析结果由数据清理、模型设定等一系列决策共同产生。现有代码往往将分析决策与实现细节混在一起，论文则可能分散描述关键决策及其理由，并遗漏较小的决策。因此，读者难以完整理解分析过程，也难以实际检验改变某项决策会如何影响结果。

Method: 作者提出一种中间表示模式，将数据分析表达为一组自然语言决策。大型语言模型可根据该模式直接生成并验证分析脚本；研究者还可通过备选方案文件指定不同决策，从而自动生成多种分析方案，构成可供比较的分析多重宇宙。

Result: 研究展示了两项应用。第一项针对一篇使用堆积吸烟数据的回归分析，构建的模式能够复现论文报告的估计结果，也能将审稿人建议的替代方案写入备选文件并自动转化为代码。第二项针对多分析者足球研究，在没有预先提供模式的情况下，将该流程用作可复现性检查。

Conclusion: 该模式在论文与代码之间提供了自包含的分析描述，清晰呈现分析决策及其扩展方式。它既有助于理解和复现既有分析，也降低了提出并比较替代分析方案的实施难度。

Abstract: A data analysis involves a sequence of decisions, from data cleaning to model specification, each of which can affect the reported result. Reproducing that result is not the same as understanding the sequence of decisions that produced it and the reasons for each: the code mixes those decisions with implementation details, while the manuscript states the major decisions and their rationale but scatters them across its sections and often leaves out the minor ones. A reader may then ask how the result would change under a different decision: the question itself is easy to pose, but implementing it is often non-trivial in practice. In this work, we propose a schema that represents a data analysis as a collection of decisions in natural language, from which large language models (LLMs) can generate and validate analysis scripts directly. This workflow can also expand a single analysis into a full multiverse of alternatives, specified in an alternative file, ready for comparison. We demonstrate the proposed workflow in two applications. In the first, we construct a schema for a published regression analysis of heaped smoking data and show that it reproduces the reported estimates, and that a reviewer-suggested alternative can be specified through the alternative file and turned into code automatically. In the second, where no schema is provided, we show how the workflow serves instead as a reproducibility check, applied to the many-analysts football study. As an intermediate representation between the manuscript and code scripts, our schema gives a self-contained account of an analysis and makes it extensible from a reader's perspective.

</details>


### [103] [Temporal Exposure to Byzantine Vulnerability Under Intermittent Participation in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33168)
*Sasa Maric Robert Abbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究客户端参与波动如何影响每轮拜占庭鲁棒性，指出总体恶意客户端比例低于阈值，并不保证每轮参与集合都满足鲁棒聚合的安全假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒聚合的安全性通常依据全体客户端中的拜占庭比例评估，但实际训练中客户端会间歇参与，导致单轮参与集合中的恶意比例可能偏高，甚至违反聚合规则的假设。论文希望厘清这种参与波动带来的风险及其时间累积效应。

Method: 论文形式化定义“动态参与暴露”，即全局拜占庭比例仍低于聚合规则容忍阈值，但某轮参与集合违反或接近违反鲁棒聚合所需假设的现象。在随机参与设定下，作者推导单轮暴露概率，分析活跃集合规模对观测拜占庭比例波动的影响，并考察多轮暴露的累积以及对手选择有利轮次参与的情形。

Result: 分析表明，参与不连续并不必然导致聚合失败；但活跃集合中的拜占庭比例会随参与规模和轮次而波动，因而暴露风险具有随机性和时间性。若对手能够择机参与，风险还可能受到策略性放大。摘要未提供具体数值或实验结果。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性不应仅被视为由全体客户端比例决定的静态属性，还应结合每轮参与集合及跨轮变化进行评估。论文建议通过设置最低活跃集合规模、进行时间维度监测、利用参与历史，并采用自适应聚合来降低动态参与暴露风险。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the effect of participation variability on per-round Byzantine vulnerability. We formalise Dynamic Participation Exposure (DPE), a phenomenon in which the global Byzantine fraction remains below an aggregation rule's tolerance threshold, while a participating client set violates, or approaches violation of, the assumptions required for robust aggregation. Under stochastic participation, we derive the probability of per round exposure and analyse how active-set size affects the variability of the observed Byzantine fraction. We further examine the accumulation of exposure over multiple rounds and consider adversaries that selectively participate during favourable rounds. Our analysis shows that intermittent participation does not automatically imply aggregation failure. Rather, it transforms Byzantine robustness from a static population-level property into a stochastic and temporal property. To mitigate this risk, we outline actionable design recommendations based on minimum active-set constraints, temporal monitoring, participation history, and adaptive aggregation.

</details>


### [104] [Geophysics-informed invertible operator network for Bayesian geophysical inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33178)
*Yangwen Zhang,Xintong Wang,Xiaofei Guan,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种融合可逆神经网络与深度算子学习的地球物理反演方法，可在观测稀疏时高效近似贝叶斯条件分布并进行不确定性采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球物理反演因观测稀疏和测量噪声而具有病态性。传统基于采样的贝叶斯方法需要大量正演计算，成本高昂；数据驱动方法在训练数据有限时又可能产生不符合物理规律的解。因此，需要兼顾计算效率、物理一致性与不确定性刻画的反演框架。

Method: 提出地球物理信息引导的可逆算子网络，将可逆神经网络嵌入深度算子学习框架。可逆网络作为分支网络，将模型参数映射为表征正演解的分支系数，以及表征反问题不确定性的潜变量。通过学习正演算子，并约束分支系数与潜变量在统计上相互独立，模型能够构建条件分布近似，并据此进行高效采样。

Result: 研究在三个代表性问题上进行了验证：一维面波频散、二维合成随机场模型的走时层析成像，以及地震切片模型。数值实验显示，该方法能适应不同问题设置，并在边界观测稀疏的情况下实现稳健且高效的条件采样。

Conclusion: 该方法将算子学习、可逆变换与不确定性建模结合，为稀疏观测条件下的贝叶斯地球物理反演提供了一种计算效率较高的方案。摘要报告了多种测试问题上的稳健表现，但未给出与基线方法的定量比较或误差指标。

Abstract: Geophysical inverse problems are inherently ill-posed because of sparse observations and measurement noise. Classical sampling-based Bayesian inference methods, such as Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), are computationally prohibitive because they require many forward simulations. Data-driven machine-learning methods often struggle to produce physically consistent solutions when training data are limited. To address these issues, we propose a geophysics-informed invertible operator network (GI-ION) that integrates an invertible neural network (INN) within a deep operator-learning framework for efficient Bayesian geophysical inversion. The INN serves as the branch network and transforms the model parameters into branch coefficients that represent the forward solution and a latent variable that captures uncertainty in the inverse problem. By learning the forward operator and enforcing statistical independence between the branch coefficients and the latent variable, GI-ION enables efficient sampling from a learned conditional approximation, even with sparse boundary observations. We validated GI-ION on three representative cases: 1D surface-wave dispersion and travel-time tomography for a 2D synthetic random-field model and a seismic-slice model. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust performance across diverse problem settings and efficient conditional sampling.

</details>


### [105] [Deep Learning-Based Mesoscopic Emission Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33179)
*Abdelkader Dib,Milos Balac,Antonio Sciarretta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出基于门控循环单元的直接排放预测模型，在大幅降低计算成本的同时，实现接近微观模型的排放预测精度，并能刻画真实氮氧化物排放水平及冷启动排放热点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通排放模型存在精度与计算效率之间的矛盾：宏观模型计算快，但依赖平均车速，容易产生系统性误差；微观模型精度较高，却难以支撑大规模模拟。研究旨在兼顾两者，开展可扩展的高分辨率城市排放建模，并减少对预先计算排放因子数据库的依赖。

Method: 研究构建直接排放预测模型，采用门控循环单元处理道路链接级的序列输入，包括车辆特征、道路属性和环境温度，预测二氧化碳与氮氧化物排放。模型使用超过一百万条合成行程进行训练；这些行程由真实全球导航卫星系统车速曲线与微观排放模型耦合生成。随后，研究将模型集成至基于活动的交通仿真流程，并应用于法国法兰西岛地区。

Result: 与混合式中观—微观参考模型相比，该模型在热启动和冷启动情景下的二氧化碳预测误差差异不超过百分之零点三五，氮氧化物不超过百分之一点八七；计算运行时间仅为参考模型的百分之二点二。基于遥感实测数据的验证表明，该模型能够准确再现欧洲二至六级柴油车的真实氮氧化物排放水平，而传统宏观工具存在系统性低估。模型还识别出冷启动排放在高密度城区的空间热点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于深度学习的中观排放建模框架能够以显著更低的计算成本达到接近微观模型的精度，并支持大规模、高空间分辨率的城市排放分析。其对真实排放水平和冷启动空间分布的再现能力，为城市交通排放评估提供了具有扩展性的替代方案。

Abstract: Traffic-related emissions are a major contributor to urban air pollution. However, existing emission models face a fundamental trade-off: macroscopic models are computationally efficient but rely on average speeds, leading to systematic errors, while microscopic models achieve high accuracy but are computationally prohibitive for large scale simulations. To bridge this gap, this study introduces Direct Emission Prediction (DEP), a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU)-based deep learning model. DEP was trained on over one million synthetic trips generated by coupling real-world GNSS speed profiles with the microscopic emission model MOBSim. By processing sequential link-level inputs, including vehicle characteristics, road attributes, and ambient temperature, DEP predicts CO$_2$ and NOx emissions. We integrated DEP into a MATSim-based workflow and applied it to the Ile-de-France region. DEP matches the accuracy of SPG-M, a hybrid mesoscopic-microscopic reference model, within 0.35% for CO$_2$ and 1.87% for NOx across both hot and cold-start scenarios, while reducing computational runtime to just 2.2%. Furthermore, validation against empirical remote sensing data confirms that DEP accurately reproduces real-world NOx emission levels across Euro 2-6 diesel vehicles, whereas traditional macroscopic tools like COPERT and HBEFA systematically underestimate them.
