The boundary is corroborated once apparatus effects are harmonized hierarchically: 77.8% of kinetic variance is between-laboratory, and pore filling carries a significant kinetic penalty (p = 0.002). Standard hypervolume over-credits feasibility-agnostic methods by 13.9-16.1% (vs. 11.1% constrained), and three surrogate-based analyses---the constrained front, a regeneration-energy third objective, and harmonized kinetics---consistently point to a conservative deployable window at pore filling phi_hat ~ 0.4-0.5 (45-55 wt% loading). The benchmark and associated analysis outputs accompany this submission.

</details>


### [174] [Cell-Type-Specific Expression Is Hard to Predict from Bulk, but It Is Still Useful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34166)
*Gadi Naveh,Amit Miller,Gal Keinan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文推导去卷积结果可预测性的理论界限，并发现细胞类型特异表达特征能在保留预后预测能力的同时，将生存信号归因于具体细胞类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞信息辅助的去卷积可从临床混合组织转录组中提取细胞类型层面的信息。现有方法多估计细胞类型比例，较少估计细胞类型特异表达；后者能够揭示比例变化无法反映的细胞状态，但基准研究显示其更难准确估计。论文旨在解释这种难度差异，并考察去卷积特征的临床价值。

Method: 作者为每个基因的细胞类型特异表达推导线性估计器的决定系数上限；对细胞类型比例，则推导跨基因汇总证据的最佳线性估计器的决定系数，并给出可计算的下界，以判断哪些细胞类型较易学习。研究还针对一百零六组经生物学筛选的细胞类型与基因组合开展定向去卷积，并在非小细胞肺癌队列中评估总体生存预测。

Result: 经筛选的目标组合事后表现出较高的可学习性评分。然而，在这些目标中，估计难度并不能预测其预后价值。以去卷积特征（主要为细胞类型特异表达）建立的稀疏生存模型，其预测效果与直接使用混合组织基因表达建立的稀疏模型相当，同时能将预测信号归因于具体细胞类型。部分去卷积表达特征还揭示了对应混合组织基因中未显现的预后信号。

Conclusion: 估计准确性与临床预测价值并非一一对应。理论界限有助于识别可学习的细胞类型或表达目标，而细胞类型特异表达特征可在不牺牲生存预测表现的前提下提升结果的生物学可解释性，并可能发现常规混合组织分析遗漏的预后信号。

Abstract: Single-cell-informed deconvolution provides cell-type-resolved features for clinical bulk RNA cohorts. Most existing methods predict the proportions $π_k$ of the $K$ constituent cell types (CTs). Fewer predict cell-type-specific expression (CTSE) $μ_{kg}$ of gene $g$ in CT $k$, which can capture cell states that proportions miss, but recent benchmarks find it much harder. We explain this gap with closed-form accuracy bounds. For CTSE, we derive an $R^2$ ceiling for per-gene linear estimators. For proportions, the best linear estimator, which pools evidence across genes, has a closed-form $R^2$, and a computable lower bound on it predicts which CTs are learnable. We study targeted deconvolution of 106 biologically curated (CT, gene) pairs, found post hoc to have higher learnability scores. On overall survival in non-small cell lung cancer, estimation difficulty does not predict prognostic value across these targets. Yet a sparse survival model on deconvolved features, mostly CTSE, predicts survival as well as a sparse model on the bulk genes themselves, while attributing its signal to specific cell types. Some deconvolved CTSE features reveal prognostic signal not seen in the corresponding bulk gene.

</details>


### [175] [Text-Conditioned Low-Rank Adaptation of Time-Series Foundation Models for Multimodal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34206)
*Kunqi Li,Bin Liang,Zhidong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种利用文本生成临时低秩参数更新的方法，在冻结时间序列基础模型的情况下融合上下文信息；在多个领域、架构和随机种子上的实验显示，该方法优于冻结模型与静态低秩适配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型能够提供可迁移的数值预测先验，但与预测相关的信息有时只出现在文本中，未体现在观测序列里。现有多模态方法通常把文本作为输入或中间表示来融合，本文则探索另一种互补途径：让文本直接调节模型参数。

Method: 提出“上下文到低秩适配时间序列”方法：对文本进行编码，并据此为冻结的时间序列基础模型中选定的权重矩阵生成临时低秩更新。数值模型骨干与文本编码器均保持冻结；若没有文本上下文，方法可精确恢复原冻结模型的输出。

Result: 在包含九个领域的时间序列基准上，针对三种基础模型架构进行评估。该方法在每种架构和每个随机种子下，整体预测误差均低于冻结模型和静态低秩适配基线。将实例特定文本替换为统一的固定文本后，所有架构的性能都下降。摘要未提供具体误差数值。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，依据实例文本动态生成模型参数，是在冻结时间序列基础模型条件下融合文本信息的一种可行策略。实例相关的上下文具有重要作用，单一全局固定文本难以替代它。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide transferable numerical forecasting priors. However, forecast-relevant information may be conveyed in text without being reflected in the observed series. Many multimodal forecasting methods integrate such context through model inputs or intermediate representations. We investigate a complementary mechanism: text-conditioned parameter adaptation. We propose Context-to-LoRA for Time Series (C2LoRA-TS), which encodes text and generates temporary Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) updates for selected weight matrices of a frozen TSFM. Both the numerical backbone and text encoder remain frozen, and the method exactly recovers the frozen TSFM output when context is absent. Evaluated on Time-MMD across nine domains and three TSFM architectures, C2LoRA-TS achieves lower aggregate forecasting error than the frozen-TSFM and Static LoRA baselines for every architecture and seed. Replacing the instance-specific text with a single globally fixed context degrades performance for every architecture. These results support text-conditioned parameter generation as a viable strategy for multimodal forecasting with frozen TSFMs.

</details>


### [176] [Compute-Aware Submodular Online Learning in Open Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34211)
*Jing Liu,Luca Ballotta,Fangfei Li,Yang Tang,Ruggero Carli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究开放式多智能体系统中的协作任务分配，提出一种限制在线评估次数的策略学习方法，在降低计算开销的同时保持较高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作任务分配中的智能体贡献可能相互重叠，导致效用呈现边际收益递减的次模特性。现有方法通常需要评估所有候选动作，在线计算成本过高，因此有必要研究评估预算对策略学习效果的影响。

Method: 算法按预设预算抽取若干智能体—动作对，并利用逆概率加权估计其边际收益，从而得到期望阶段效用的无偏梯度估计；随后采用基于相对熵的镜像上升更新策略。理论分析给出二分之一近似的遗憾界，并将误差拆分为最优策略与已学习策略之间的跟踪误差，以及由查询预算决定的反馈误差。

Result: 在协作式信息覆盖实验中，该方法显著减少在线计算量，同时保持较高性能。理论结果还刻画了查询预算对学习误差的影响。

Conclusion: 通过预算化采样和无偏梯度估计，可以在计算资源受限时有效进行多智能体协作任务分配。该方法兼顾在线效率与策略质量，并提供了关于预算、策略跟踪误差和学习性能之间关系的理论保证。

Abstract: This paper studies policy learning for cooperative task allocation in open multi-agent systems, where submodular utilities model diminishing marginal returns due to overlapping agent contributions. While standard methods evaluate every candidate action to update the policy, this may incur an excessive computational cost online. Hence, we impose a budget on these evaluations and analytically study the effect of query budget on online learning. Our algorithm samples a prescribed number of agent-action pairs and applies inverse-probability weighting to their marginal gains, yielding an unbiased gradient estimate of the expected stage utility for a Kullback-Leibler (KL) mirror-ascent update. We derive a $1/2$-approximate regret bound that elegantly splits into a tracking error between optimal and learned policies, quantified by their KL divergence, and a query-dependent feedback term. Experiments on cooperative information coverage show that our approach retains high performance with significantly less computation online.

</details>


### [177] [Change Point Detection with Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34228)
*Tigran Ramazyan,Denis Derkach,Mikhail Hushchyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过生成式模型扩充变点检测中的窗口样本，降低检测分数的波动，并提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于窗口的变点检测需要在时间精度与统计可靠性之间权衡：窗口较小有利于准确定位变化，却会导致样本不足，使分类器或深度学习模型计算的检测分数方差较大、误判频繁。

Method: 利用生成式模型对窗口数据进行增强，扩充可用样本，以改善检测分数的稳定性和变点检测质量。摘要未说明具体生成模型或训练细节。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该方法能显著提升变点检测效果。样本增强稳定了检测分数分布，使简单分类器的表现超过复杂深度学习基线。

Conclusion: 生成式窗口增强能够缓解小样本造成的检测瓶颈，提升基于窗口的变点检测表现；在此任务中，改善样本与分数的稳定性，可能比采用更复杂的检测模型更有效。

Abstract: Change point detection (CPD) methods aim to identify abrupt changes in the properties of time series, which is a key problem in numerous fields. A major class of CPD approaches relies on computing a CPD score, which is either a discrepancy or divergence between two subsequent samples, called windows. A fundamental limitation of window-based CPD is the 'sample size bottleneck.' To ensure temporal precision, windows must be small; however, small samples provide insufficient statistical evidence for the classifiers and deep-learning models currently used to compute CPD scores. This leads to high variance in the scores and frequent mispredictions. Our study tackles this issue by exploring generative models for window augmentation, effectively expanding available data to improve CPD quality. Experiments on synthetic and real data demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly enhances CPD. Specifically, we show that this approach allows simple classifiers to outperform complex deep-learning baselines by stabilizing the CPD score distribution.

</details>


### [178] [Train-Only-Constrained LM for Synthetic Clinical Trajectories: Leak-Safe Calibration and Auditable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34258)
*Yaser Kord,Moritz Hess*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅用训练集拟合、将跨指标共现与时间变化分开建模的临床时间序列生成流程；在受控模拟数据上改善了分布与共现拟合，且成员推断检测接近随机水平，但尚未在真实电子病历上验证，也不提供差分隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列生成不仅要匹配指标分布和指标间共现，还要保留合理的时间演变，并避免校准过程破坏模型特性。与此同时，隐私风险需要透明评估。本文旨在以可审计、严格区分训练集与测试集的方式处理这些要求。

Method: 采用受约束的语言模型流程，将生成因子化：首先，紧凑型生成式语言模型通过分位数标记学习不同生物标志物之间的共现关系；其次，仅利用训练集为每个指标拟合三状态马尔可夫转移，用于约束时间方向与动态变化。随后仅在训练集上拟合单调分位数映射，将合成训练数据校准至模拟训练数据；固定映射再应用于合成测试数据，不使用测试集统计量。研究还进行多随机种子评估、配对百分之九十五置信区间分析、校准偏差量化及成员推断审计。

Result: 在由五千名模拟患者构成、参数处于已发表临床范围内的测试环境中，受约束语言模型相较经验采样器提高了分布拟合与指标共现表现。经过小范围参数扫描后，合成数据的可检测性仍接近随机水平；成员推断分类器的曲线下面积约为零点五。时间动态因转移约束而按构造符合设定。摘要未给出分布改善幅度或分位数校准偏差的具体数值，仅说明对后者及其置信区间进行了量化。

Conclusion: 结果支持该流程在受控模拟环境中兼顾分布拟合、跨指标共现、时间一致性与隐私风险评估，但证据仍属于第一阶段方法验证。接下来需要在真实电子病历队列中开展第二阶段验证，并评估临床终点相关效用。成员推断结果接近随机水平不等同于形式化隐私保证，本文明确不作差分隐私声明。

Abstract: Simulating clinical time series necessitates temporal realism, calibration that preserves model integrity, and privacy transparency. We present a train-only, constrained language-model pipeline that factorizes generation. A compact GPT-style language model learns cross-biomarker co-occurrence through quantile tokens, and temporal directions are enforced by 3x3 per-biomarker Markov transitions fit on TRAIN, allowing dynamics to be audited. We fit a monotone quantile map (QMap) on TRAIN only (SYN(TRAIN) -> SIM(TRAIN)); no TEST statistics are used. The fixed map is then applied to SYN(TEST) severities. The constrained LM demonstrated improved distributional fidelity and co-occurrence in a 5,000-patient simulated testbed (parameters within published clinical ranges) compared to an empirical sampler, maintained near-chance detectability after a small lambda-sweep, and exhibits temporal adherence by construction. A classifier-based membership-inference audit results in an AUC of approximately 0.5; we make no differential-privacy claim. We present multi-seed results with paired 95% confidence intervals and quantify the severity of QMap's alignment (Delta W1 with confidence interval). This is a Stage-1 method result obtained on a controlled simulator; Stage-2 validation on real EHR cohorts with clinical endpoints is planned.

</details>


### [179] [LLM-coupled interpretable tool wear monitoring with multi-evidence reasoning in CNC machining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34270)
*Yuntong Chen,Yingqi Li,Qiwen Shen,Guobin Zhao,Xitian Tian,Lijiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合磨损分类与大语言模型辅助推理的两层框架，在实现高精度识别的同时，生成便于工程决策的刀具更换建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数控加工刀具磨损诊断模型虽有较高准确率，但通常难以解释诊断结果，也难以为刀具更换提供直接指导。研究旨在将可靠的磨损识别与可理解、可执行的决策支持结合起来。

Method: 框架分为两层：第一层通过主成分分析载荷进行特征筛选，并使用优化后的门控循环单元网络识别三种磨损状态；第二层将分类结果和特征变化趋势输入大语言模型，生成自然语言诊断报告及更换建议。

Result: 在PHM2010数据集上，磨损分类的宏平均F1分数达到98.38%。工程师评估显示，大语言模型生成的报告优于基于规则模板的报告；在美国国家航空航天局铣削数据上的跨数据集测试也为框架的泛化能力提供了支持。

Conclusion: 该框架将深度学习分类与大语言模型解释和建议生成相结合，有望弥合高精度诊断与实际刀具维护决策之间的差距。不过，摘要未提供跨数据集测试的具体指标，也未说明更换建议在真实生产中的长期效果。

Abstract: Interpretable tool wear assessment in CNC machining is challenging. Existing diagnostic models have high accuracy but provide limited guidance for tool replacement. This study proposes a two layer framework combining deep learning wear classification and LLM assisted reasoning. The first layer uses PCA Loading feature selection and optimized GRU to recognize three wear states, achieving 98.38 percent macro F1 score on the PHM2010 dataset. The second layer uses LLMs to generate natural language reports and replacement suggestions from classification results and feature trends. Engineer evaluation shows the reports outperform rule templates. Cross dataset tests on NASA milling data support the generalizability of this framework.

</details>


### [180] [LatentCP-DPO: Conformal Pessimism for DPO with Finite-Sample Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34275)
*Hongwei Xu,Jingyi Liu,Vincent Y. F. Tan,Zai Yang,Yulian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合保形预测与悲观偏好优化的方法，在偏好数据有限或标签噪声较大时，利用潜在偏好奖励的不确定性训练响应策略，并分析其有限样本性能与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好优化在偏好数据有限时，容易过拟合噪声较大或信息不足的偏好标签。已有方法对未观测潜在偏好参数的不确定性考虑不足，因此需要一种能量化并稳健利用该不确定性的优化框架。

Method: 提出潜在保形预测直接偏好优化框架，适用于布拉德利—特里成对反馈和普拉克特—卢斯排序反馈。在偏好模型设定正确且样本满足可交换性时，利用可观测偏好构造保形预测集，进而为潜在相对奖励建立具有有限样本边际覆盖保证的不确定集合。随后，使用一组独立且无偏好标签的提示及候选回答，最大化这些不确定集合上的最坏情形期望偏好对数似然，以学习响应策略。

Result: 理论上，在适当假设下，证明正则化策略次优性的有限样本上界，并将误差分解为策略类近似误差、保形不确定性和统计估计误差。对于成对反馈，还证明了与上界对未标注优化样本数的倒数依赖相匹配的最坏情形下界；该下界同时表明，不确定集合可能产生随优化样本增加仍持续存在的偏差。实验结果支持该方法在有限偏好数据下的优势。

Conclusion: 该框架将保形预测得到的不确定性纳入偏好优化，既提供了有限样本覆盖与策略性能分析，也揭示了不确定性可能带来的持久偏差。研究因此刻画了基于保形不确定集合进行偏好优化的统计准确性及其潜在局限。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) learns a response policy from human preference data, but with a limited dataset it can overfit to noisy or weakly informative preference labels. We introduce LatentCP-DPO, a framework that combines conformal prediction with pessimistic preference optimization for Bradley-Terry (BT) pairwise feedback and Plackett-Luce (PL) ranking feedback. Building on LatentCP's construction of uncertainty sets for unobserved model parameters, we address how to incorporate this uncertainty into preference optimization and characterize its effect on policy performance. Under exchangeability and a correctly specified preference model, we construct uncertainty sets for latent relative rewards with finite-sample marginal coverage from conformal prediction sets for observable preferences. We then learn a response policy by maximizing the worst-case expected preference log-likelihood over these uncertainty sets, using an independent collection of prompts and candidate responses without preference labels. Under suitable assumptions, we establish finite-sample upper bounds on regularized policy suboptimality that separate policy-class approximation error, conformal uncertainty, and statistical estimation error. For BT feedback, we further establish a worst-case lower bound for LatentCP-DPO that matches the upper bound's reciprocal dependence on the number of unlabeled optimization examples. The lower bound also shows that the uncertainty sets can induce a bias that persists as the optimization sample grows. Together, these results characterize both the statistical accuracy and potential limitations of preference optimization using conformal uncertainty sets. Experiments support the benefits of the proposed framework in finite preference datasets.

</details>


### [181] [Scaling Looped MoE Language Models under Matched Budgets: Information Flow and Parameter Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34276)
*Shipeng Bai,Jiayu Qin,Hong Liu,Xili Wang,Jianchao Tan,Jiacheng Li,He Zhang,Yifan Lu,Jiaqi Zhang,Kefeng Zhang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过改进循环式变换器的信息流，并优化参数配置，在不增加计算量的条件下提升模型表现；实验显示其优于预算匹配的非循环基线及其他循环式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式变换器能够在参数量不变时增加计算深度，已有研究显示增加计算可带来性能收益。然而，在参数量和浮点运算量严格匹配的约束下，如何设计循环结构并分配参数，仍缺少系统研究。

Method: 研究以改善循环模型中的信息流为目标，引入门控的内循环状态更新和跨阶段残差连接。随后通过受控实验研究循环参数配置，发现将单次通过的核心网络深度设为展开后总深度的百分之二十五至百分之三十五，并把节省的参数分配给每层更多路由专家，是一种有效配置；该方案无需增加计算量，且兼容现有训练流程。

Result: 在一万亿个预训练词元后，六百九十亿参数和五百一十亿参数的混合专家模型，在四项基准测试上的平均成绩分别比计算预算匹配的非循环基线高一点一六和二点二九个百分点。另在共同骨干网络及两千亿词元训练预算下的复现实验中，该方法的困惑度低于其他循环式方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，循环模型的性能不仅取决于增加计算深度，也受到信息流设计和参数分配方式的显著影响。通过改进状态传递并将节省的参数用于扩展专家模块，可以在固定计算预算下有效提升模型表现。

Abstract: Looped Transformers decouple computational depth from parameter count, and prior studies have demonstrated promising performance gains with increased computation. However, systematic design principles for configuring these models under strict iso-parameter and iso-FLOP constraints remain scarce. We contribute to this direction by improving information flow in looped models and systematically studying loop parameter configurations. Specifically, we refine this information flow via gated inner-loop state updates and a cross-stage residual connection. Building on this design, controlled studies identify a configuration combining a single-pass Core depth of 25%-35% of the total unrolled depth with reallocating saved parameters into extra routed experts per layer, improving performance at zero extra compute while preserving compatibility with existing training infrastructure. Evaluated after 1T pre-training tokens, our 69B MLA and 51B KDA Mixture-of-Experts models outperform budget-matched non-recurrent baselines by an average of 1.16 and 2.29 percentage points across four benchmarks, respectively. Further reproduction experiments on a common backbone with a 200B-token training budget show that our method achieves lower perplexity than other looped methods. Code for these reproduction experiments is available at https://github.com/lxi01766/Scaling-Looped-MoE-Language-Models-under-Matched-Budgets-Information-Flow-and-Parameter-Allocation.

</details>


### [182] [RAP: Radial-Angular Random Projection for Hyperbolic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34298)
*Jiahong Liu,Wenhao Yu,Yifan Li,Yanyu Chen,Rex Ying,Menglin Yang,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出径向—角向随机投影方法：保持双曲表示的径向坐标不变，仅压缩角向信息；理论分析与多组实验表明，该方法能有效降低双曲表示的压缩失真，并改善检索表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧氏表示已有随机投影及其维数保证，但双曲表示缺少相应的通用降维工具。直接将欧氏方案迁移到双曲空间存在困难：切空间方法受局部坐标限制且受到曲率影响；双曲空间内的直接投影则会混合径向与角向误差，使双曲距离计算中的反双曲余弦函数条件数恶化。

Method: 提出径向—角向随机投影，精确保留每个点的径向坐标，将角向分量映射到欧氏空间并进行随机投影，再将结果归一化并提升回目标双曲面。该方法只需一次高斯矩阵与向量乘法，并将压缩分析转化为球面上的归一化角向随机投影问题。论文还给出有限点集的误差保证，明确刻画与曲率和点的径向距离相关的条件数，并分析切空间方案及直接双曲投影的性质。

Result: 在原点中心的宽半径点云、平衡树、聚类高斯数据及词网名词层级等测试中，该方法在所有测试规模与目标维数组合下均取得最低平均相对失真；在宽半径数据上，相较最强双曲基线，失真约降低百分之四十八至百分之四十九。实验还显示，该方法可扩展至百万级数据，并能提升视觉—语言表示上的检索效果。

Conclusion: 径向与角向分离为双曲表示提供了一种结构清晰、计算高效的压缩途径。理论结果解释了其误差与几何条件数的关系，实验则支持其在失真、可扩展性和检索任务上的优势。

Abstract: Random projection is a basic compression primitive for Euclidean representation learning, with the Johnson--Lindenstrauss lemma giving a target dimension $k=O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log n)$ independent of the ambient dimension. As hyperbolic representations gain increasing attention in modern Transformers, vision-language models, and large language models, the same need for oblivious dimensionality reduction arises, but no analogous primitive yet exists for hyperbolic representations. We study this gap. Two natural attempts fail in dual ways: a tangent-space scheme gives a clean local guarantee but is chart-bound and pays a curvature penalty, while a fully hyperbolic projection in the hyperboloid model is geometrically native but mixes radial and angular error before feeding them into the conditioning of $\operatorname{arcosh}$. We propose radial-angular random projection (RAP), which preserves the radial coordinate exactly and applies a Euclidean random projection only to the angular direction, lifting the result back to the target hyperboloid. RAP is hyperbolic-to-hyperbolic by construction, costs one Gaussian matrix-vector product, and reduces hyperbolic compression to a normalized angular JL question on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$. We prove a finite-set RAP guarantee with explicit $\sinh(\sqrt{c}\,r_x)\sinh(\sqrt{c}\,r_y)$ conditioning, complemented by a local tangent-space JL theorem and an arcosh-conditioning lemma for the fully hyperbolic comparator. Across an origin-centered broad-radius point cloud, a balanced tree, a clustered Gaussian, and the WordNet noun hierarchy, RAP attains the lowest mean relative distortion in every $(n,k)$ cell, reducing distortion over the strongest hyperbolic baseline by approximately $48\%$--$49\%$ on broad-radius data. RAP also scales to million-point datasets and improves retrieval on vision--language representations over hyperbolic projection baselines.

</details>


### [183] [Interpretable identification of constitutive parameter field for three-dimensional heterogeneous and graded hyperelastic materials from full-field displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34310)
*Kanhaiya Lal Chaurasiya,Vaskar Halder,Prajyot Patil,Gaurav Navnath Shinde,Debashish Das,Akshay Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释且计算高效的有限元方法，仅利用位移场数据，识别非均质超弹性材料中多个本构参数的相对空间分布；在合成与实验基准上取得可接受的精度，且计算时间显著短于近期深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭实验测得的位移场和边界反力识别非均质材料的空间变化特性仍具挑战。准确表征梯度材料、复合材料及生物组织，有助于材料设计、制造和生物医学应用。相较于均质材料，非均质与梯度材料的反问题研究较少；已有部分方法依赖参数量较大的深度学习框架，计算成本较高。

Method: 提出一种可解释、时间效率较高的识别框架，使用位移场数据预测非均质超弹性材料多个本构参数的三维相对空间变化。该方法利用超弹性材料反问题的线性特征，并在有限元层级直接优化本构参数，无需依赖在材料内部指定配置点来施加力平衡的深度学习框架。

Result: 框架在涵盖非均质、梯度及各向异性超弹性材料的三维合成基准和二维实验基准上进行了验证。摘要报告其预测精度达到可接受水平，且与近期深度学习方法相比，计算时间显著降低；未提供具体误差或耗时数值。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了直接在有限元层级优化参数、仅凭位移数据识别超弹性材料空间参数变化的可行性，为非均质材料表征提供了一种较轻量且可解释的替代方案。其适用性已在若干合成与实验基准上验证，但摘要所述结果尚未给出定量指标。

Abstract: The inverse problem of identifying spatially varying material properties of heterogeneous solids using experimentally available displacement fields and boundary reaction forces alone remains challenging. Being able to characterize heterogeneous and graded solids would be advantageous in the design and manufacturing of functionally graded materials and composites. Additionally, an efficient technique for identifying heterogeneous material properties from in vivo measurements would be useful in biomedical contexts to characterize heterogeneous biological tissues. Although several techniques developed over the past two decades have addressed inverse identification for homogeneous materials, including linear elastic, hyperelastic, viscoelastic, and elastoplastic solids, comparatively fewer studies have considered heterogeneous and graded materials. Furthermore, several recent approaches to characterizing heterogeneous and graded materials employ parameter-heavy, deep learning-based frameworks trained to enforce force equilibrium at specified collocation points within the material. In this work, we propose an interpretable and time-efficient framework that predicts the three-dimensional relative spatial variation of a constitutive parameter field for multiple parameters of a heterogeneous hyperelastic material, using only displacement-field data. In this work, we validate our framework on 3D synthetic and 2D experimental benchmarks involving heterogeneous, graded, and anisotropic hyperelastic materials. By leveraging the linear nature of the inverse problem for hyperelastic materials and directly optimizing for the constitutive parameters at the finite-element level, we achieve acceptable prediction accuracy with significantly lower computational time compared to recently reported deep-learning-based frameworks.

</details>


### [184] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning with State-Dependent Foresight for Human Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34351)
*Jinil Kim,Jinwoo Jeong,Hyeongjun Yun,Min-hwan Oh,Woo-Young Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种考虑有限且随状态变化的前瞻能力的逆强化学习方法，在行人过街和高速公路超车任务中，比标准方法更准确地模仿人类行为，并能估计个体在不同状态下的规划深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然情境任务能呈现真实环境中的复杂性，但产生的行为轨迹维度高、难以解释。标准逆强化学习通常假设个体进行近似无限期的规划，这与人类前瞻能力有限且会随当前状态变化的特点不符，因此可能难以准确刻画真实行为。

Method: 研究提出状态条件对抗式逆强化学习方法。模型设置多个规划深度不同的策略，各策略共享同一奖励函数，但显式累加奖励的前瞻范围不同；另设状态条件路由器，根据当前状态对这些策略进行加权组合，从而刻画规划深度随情境变化的现象。

Result: 在留出的行人过街任务中，模型的最高排名准确率由标准方法的0.549提升至0.647；在高速公路超车任务中，由0.629提升至0.718。模型生成的行为轨迹也更接近参与者实际行为。此外，推断出的规划深度与参与者自我报告的焦虑程度和运动冲动性相关。

Conclusion: 该方法将有限且状态依赖的前瞻能力纳入行为建模，在两类自然情境任务中提高了模仿表现，并提供了可解释的、由模型推断的状态依赖规划深度指标。

Abstract: Computational modeling of behavior represents individuals with a model fitted to their observed behavior and characterizes them through its parameters. Naturalistic tasks incorporate the complexity of real environments and yield high-dimensional behavioral trajectories. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) infers the latent reward function underlying these trajectories. Standard IRL, however, assumes effectively infinite-horizon planning, whereas human foresight is both bounded and state-dependent. Here we introduce SC-AIRL (State-Conditional Adversarial IRL), which builds these two cognitive properties into the planner. Per-depth policies share one reward but differ in how far ahead they sum it explicitly. A state-conditional router mixes them according to the current state. Across held-out episodes in pedestrian crossing and highway overtaking tasks, SC-AIRL improves imitation over Standard AIRL, raising top-1 accuracy from 0.549 to 0.647 and from 0.629 to 0.718, respectively, while more closely reproducing participant behavior in rollouts. Inferred planning depth correlates with self-reported anxiety and motor impulsivity. SC-AIRL thus reproduces human behavior more closely than Standard AIRL while providing an interpretable, model-derived measure of state-dependent foresight.

</details>


### [185] [RAWPrism: A Three-Task Dataset and a Unified Model for RAW-Domain Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34359)
*Tianyi Hu,Yangdong Li,Shuyan Liu,Zihan Cao,Jingze Liang,Shaojie Wu,Chenyang Feng,Jincheng Di,Weijie Xiao,Liangjian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出原始传感器数据域的统一多任务图像融合数据集与轻量模型，覆盖多曝光、多焦点和红外—可见光融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多曝光融合、多焦点融合及红外—可见光融合研究主要基于经过图像信号处理的彩色图像；在图像信号处理之前的原始数据域中，跨任务统一研究仍较少。论文旨在提供数据与方法，推动探索曝光、焦点和光谱互补等不同融合任务之间可共享的机制。

Method: 论文构建原始数据域融合数据集，包含自采集的422个可用多曝光场景、150组配准多焦点图像，以及1500组由现有红外—可见光数据集衍生的样本；其中可见光原始数据由原始数据重建方法生成，彩色对应图像则通过图像信号处理模拟方法渲染。数据集提供场景级划分，以及针对不同任务的渲染与评分协议。与此同时，论文提出统一融合网络：采用感光元件彩色滤波阵列感知表征、由任务条件控制的输入源门控机制和共享骨干网络，以一个358万参数的模型支持三类任务。

Result: 论文的主要成果是发布覆盖三类融合任务的数据集及统一模型，并建立配套的数据划分、渲染和评估流程。摘要称该模型据作者所知是首个直接在原始数据域统一处理多曝光、多焦点和红外—可见光融合的模型。摘要未提供具体的定量对比结果或性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作为原始数据域的跨任务图像融合研究提供了数据与轻量化基线，支持研究者考察不同融合任务能否共享表征与机制。其贡献侧重于资源建设和统一建模；实际性能优势及其相对现有方法的效果，仍需结合论文中的实验结果进一步判断。

Abstract: Research on multi-exposure fusion (MEF), multi-focus fusion (MFF), and infrared-visible image fusion (IVIF) has largely focused on rendered RGB images, while unified fusion across these tasks in the RAW domain before image signal processing (ISP) remains relatively underexplored. To support research in this setting, we introduce the RAWPrism dataset, covering all three tasks, and the RAWPrism model, a lightweight unified multi-task fusion network. The RAWPrism dataset comprises 422 usable MEF scenes and 150 registered MFF pairs from our RAW captures, together with 1,500 FMB-derived IVIF samples, in which the visible RAW inputs are reconstructed by ReRAW and their RGB counterparts rendered by CycleISP. Scene-level partitions and task-specific rendering and scoring protocols connect these resources to method development and evaluation. To our knowledge, the RAWPrism model is the first to unify MEF, MFF, and IVIF directly in the RAW domain. It combines a CFA-aware representation, task-conditioned source gating, and a shared backbone to support all three tasks with one 3.58M-parameter checkpoint. Together, the dataset and model support the study of shared fusion mechanisms across exposure, focus, and spectral complementarity.

</details>


### [186] [DiversePlace: Diversity-Seeking Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Macro Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34472)
*Wenrui Zhou,Jiashun Liu,Wenji Fang,Zhiyao Xie,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向宏单元布局的强化学习方法，在保持线长质量竞争力的同时，生成多个具有差异性的候选布局，供后续阶段选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏单元位置会强烈制约后续物理设计，但布局决策必须在下游实现质量尚无法准确评估时作出，因此训练通常依赖半周长线长等快速但不完美的代理指标。现有强化学习方法多追求单一代理指标最优解，且决策序列较长，往往需要大量搜索辅助或离线专家数据，也难以保留多个有竞争力的布局。

Method: 提出多样化布局框架，将受质量约束的多样性作为明确优化目标。方法维护一个规模紧凑的候选布局集合，筛选线长表现有竞争力且彼此差异显著的方案。训练采用近端策略优化，并结合质量门控的多样性奖励与逐步扩展的布局决策时域，使模型能够从零开始优化，同时将探索限制在具有竞争力的线长区域内。

Result: 在国际标准化组织二〇〇五年基准测试的八个设计上，该方法均降低了线长或取得几乎相同的线长，并持续提升排名前五、前十和前二十候选集合的多样性，同时具有更高的效率。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，宏单元布局不必只追求单一代理指标最优解；通过联合控制布局质量与多样性，可以高效地产生更丰富且具有竞争力的候选方案，为后续设计阶段提供更好的选择空间。

Abstract: Macro placement is a critical early-stage decision in physical design because macro locations strongly constrain subsequent implementation stages. The task is difficult because these high-impact discrete decisions must be made before downstream quality can be measured accurately, forcing training to rely on fast but imperfect training-time oracles such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL). Existing RL-based placers therefore mostly optimize single HPWL-driven solutions, and their long sequential horizons often require heavy search assistance or offline expert data while leaving little room to preserve multiple competitive layouts. We address this gap by making quality-controlled diversity an explicit objective for macro placement. Instead of relying on a single proxy-optimal solution, we maintain a compact archive of HPWL-competitive yet meaningfully different placements for downstream selection. We realize this idea in DiversePlace, a curriculum proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework that combines a quality-gated diversity reward with an expanding placement horizon, enabling from-scratch RL optimization while keeping exploration inside competitive HPWL regions. On the ISPD2005 benchmarks, DiversePlace consistently improves top-5, top-10, and top-20 archive diversity and achieves lower or virtually identical HPWL on all eight designs with better efficiency. Code: https://github.com/William030422/NIPS2026-DiversePlace.

</details>


### [187] [Auditing Identity and Timing Signals in Operational Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34493)
*Andrew Hendel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两项预测前审计，用于检测运维日志中单位身份与时间信息造成的潜在捷径，并展示数据划分方式会显著影响模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运维日志预测模型可能并非真正识别事件规律，而是利用记录所属患者或设备的身份，以及记录发生时间等线索获得高分。若不先检查数据本身携带的这些信息，模型效果可能被高估，尤其在训练集与测试集包含同一单位记录时。

Method: 提出两项简单审计，并应用于六个领域的七个公开数据集：第一项检验分类器能否判断两条记录是否来自同一单位；第二项检验分类器能否判断同一单位的两条记录哪条时间更晚。在相应零假设下，两项审计的预期曲线下面积均为百分之五十。研究还比较不同预处理、特征组合和数据划分方案对预测结果的影响，并提供可用于含单位标识列和时间列数据的审计工具与指南。

Result: 在重症监护数据中，前向填补会使单位身份审计的曲线下面积从零点九一六升至零点九九三。脓毒症预测任务中，若同一患者的记录同时出现在训练集和测试集，前向填补相较原始值带来的曲线下面积提升为零点一六三；按患者隔离划分时仅为零点零四六。患者记录重叠导致的夸大现象也出现在另一项以死亡为结局的重症监护数据中。完整随访的时间顺序对照实验未发现正向的平均重叠差距。仅使用住院时长和测量次数、不使用临床数值，在患者留出测试中便达到零点七六一；加入原始临床数值后仅提高零点零一八。此外，日历切分仍存在重叠问题：百分之九十五的硬盘和百分之七十五的贷款对象在切分点两侧都有记录。

Conclusion: 数据集审计能够揭示身份、时间及预处理步骤带来的预测捷径，但这些线索最终会造成多大评估偏差，取决于数据划分与评估方案。研究建议在训练预测模型前检查数据携带的身份和时间信息，并谨慎设计能够避免单位重叠及时间泄漏的评估协议。

Abstract: Models that predict events from operational logs, such as sepsis in ICU records or failures in hard drives, can score well by recognizing which patient or drive a record came from, or when it was recorded, instead of predicting the event. I introduce two simple audits that measure how much of this information a dataset carries before any event-prediction model is trained, and apply them to seven public datasets in six domains. One asks whether a classifier can tell that two records came from the same unit; the other asks which of two records from the same unit came later. Under the stated nulls, both have expected AUC 0.5. In ICU data, the identity audit flags a common preprocessing step: carrying the last measurement forward raises identity AUC from 0.916 to 0.993. On a 12-hour sepsis forecasting task, carrying forward makes a model look 0.163 AUC better than raw values when a patient's hours are split between training and test, but only 0.046 better when patients are held out. The exaggeration under patient overlap also appears in a second ICU dataset with mortality as the outcome. On the sepsis task, a chronological control with complete follow-up, in which training sees only a patient's earlier, resolved hours, shows no positive mean overlap gap. Timing is a strong predictor: with complete follow-up, hour of stay and measurement counts alone, with no clinical value, score 0.761 on held-out patients, and adding the raw clinical values changes that by +0.018. Outside the ICU, 95% of drives and 75% of loans have records on both sides of a calendar cut, and the overlap gap persists under a calendar split. The audits show which features and preprocessing choices to check; the evaluation protocol decides the consequences. I release the code, a library that runs the audits on any table with a unit column and a time column, and a guide to using the audits.

</details>


### [188] [SMOTE 25 Years of Magic: What Has Been Done, What the Evidence Shows, and Whom It Reached](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34511)
*Thanapong Intharah,Pharamut Makjaroen,Kanvara Thawarorit,Supawit Chaingam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了合成少数类过采样技术及其衍生方法：变体虽常在提出者自己的研究中胜过基准方法，但独立证据显示优势会减弱，且存在校准受损和数据泄漏等风险。因此，单独提出新变体已不足以构成贡献，评估应采用更强基线并报告校准情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该技术问世二十五年来被广泛引用，并应用于计算机科学以外的众多领域，但其衍生方法究竟改变了什么、支持其有效性的证据有多可靠，以及技术传播是否带来实际收益，仍缺乏系统梳理。本文旨在评估方法变化、证据质量与应用覆盖范围。

Method: 研究审查了经筛选的七十六篇论文，其中三十七篇提出该技术家族的新型数据生成方法；另以系统检索方式筛查二万二千四百二十六条记录，并随机抽取三百项应用进行覆盖度评估。研究整理了一千七百五十六条数据集记录、一千五百一十一项与基准方法的比较，以及七十一项研究的评估设计；同时采用预先制定规则的人工智能代理进行信息提取、筛选与编码，并报告各环节的核验方式。

Result: 所有变体都重新设定了原始规则中的至少一项决策；三十七种变体中有二十四种仅在二分类任务上得到测试。提出者自己的论文中，变体在百分之七十九的已判定比较中胜过基准方法；但独立研究发现，采用强学习器和经过调优的阈值后，优势会缩小，重采样还会损害概率校准。系统检索筛选通过的三千零九十七条记录中，审查语料仅覆盖五十二条，对早期研究覆盖较充分，对近期研究覆盖不足。应用范围虽广，实际结果层面的收益尚未得到证实；研究还记录了四类危害，并估计约百分之三十七的应用在划分训练集与测试集之前就对完整数据集进行了重采样，可能造成数据泄漏。

Conclusion: 对于表格分类任务，仅提出一种新的过采样变体不应再被视为独立贡献。新方法应逐项检验其设计决策，并与类别加权、经过调优的分类阈值及强学习器进行比较，同时报告校准表现。论文还提供了方法选择指南、包含十五项内容的报告清单及后续研究议程。

Abstract: Background. SMOTE was published in this journal in 2002 and reviewed here at its fifteenth anniversary in 2018. At twenty-five it is cited by more than 34,000 indexed works, half of them outside computer science. Objectives. To establish what the methods built on SMOTE change, how strong the evidence for them is, and whom the technique has reached. Methods. We audited a curated corpus of 76 papers, 37 of which propose a SMOTE-family generator, and tested its coverage with a systematic search of 22,426 records in the PRISMA 2020 format. From the corpus we collected 1,756 dataset entries, 1,511 published comparisons with SMOTE and the evaluation design of 71 studies; from the search we drew 300 applications of SMOTE at random. We used an agentic AI approach to extract, screen and code under our written rules, and we report how each step was checked. Results. Every variant re-decides some of the six decisions of the SMOTE rule, and 24 of the 37 are only ever run on two classes. In their own papers, proposed methods beat SMOTE in 79.0% of the decided comparisons, but independent studies find that the gains shrink with strong learners and tuned thresholds, and that resampling damages calibration. The corpus holds 52 of the 3,097 records that pass the screen and covers the early years closely, the recent ones thinly. The reach is very wide, but a benefit at the level of outcomes is undemonstrated, four harms are documented, and an estimated 37% of applications resample the whole data set before splitting it. Conclusions. For tabular classification, a new SMOTE variant should no longer count as a contribution in itself: a new generator should be tested one decision at a time against class weighting and a tuned threshold with a strong learner, with calibration reported. We close with a selection guide, a reporting checklist of fifteen items and a research agenda.

</details>


### [189] [Resilience Analysis Through Automated Stage Identification and Quantitative Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34527)
*Anna Strzoda,Krzysztof Grochla,Rafael Munoz-Carpena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种不依赖特定领域或威胁类型的机器学习方法，用于识别系统性能时间序列中的韧性阶段，并据此计算韧性指标；在网络和机场交通系统数据上的实验显示，该方法具有较好的分类与量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂工程系统会受到各类扰动，但不同领域和威胁下的韧性评估方式往往难以通用。论文旨在自动识别系统面对扰动时的准备、吸收、恢复和适应阶段，为跨系统、与威胁类型无关的定量韧性分析提供基础。

Method: 研究比较了随机森林、支持向量机、轻量梯度提升机和极端梯度提升四种机器学习模型，以系统性能时间序列识别韧性阶段。随后利用模型预测结果，结合扰动发生前、期间及之后观测到的性能数据，计算相应的系统韧性指标。评估数据来自两种不同运行领域的网络系统，以及一个机场交通系统。

Result: 各韧性阶段分类的最佳调和平均分数为百分之七十四点三至百分之九十九点四。整体韧性指标的最佳平均绝对百分比误差，在无线局域网和长期演进网络数据上为百分之零点三五至百分之零点八二，在机场数据上为百分之一点六五至百分之九点五六。结果表明，该方法在不同领域均可用于韧性阶段识别与指标估计，但机场系统上的估计误差相对更高。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了机器学习方法用于跨领域韧性评估的可行性，可支持分析复杂工程系统承受扰动、吸收冲击和恢复运行的能力。其主要价值在于降低韧性分析对特定领域和威胁类型的依赖；后续仍可进一步检验其在更多系统、扰动情形和数据条件下的适用性。

Abstract: This paper introduced an ML-based approach, designed to be domain- and threat-agnostic, for automated recognition of PARA (Plan/Prepare, Absorb, Recover, Adapt) resilience stages in system-performance time series, enabling subsequent quantitative resilience analysis across different systems. We investigate four ML models for resilience stage recognition: Random Forest (RF), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM), and Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost). We then use the ML models' predictions to quantify system resilience by computing corresponding resilience metrics from system-performance data observed before, during, and after disruptive events. We evaluate the proposed approach using datasets from two distinct operational domain network systems and an airport transportation system, demonstrating its domain-agnostic applicability to resilience assessment. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of resilience stage classification, with the best F1-scores ranging from 74.3% to 99.4% across all resilience stages. The overall resilience metrics R_t and RL are estimated, with the best models' MAPE ranging from 0.35-0.82% on IEEE 802.11 and LTE to 1.65-9.56% for the airport. These results provide a basis for threat-agnostic resilience analysis of complex engineering systems, enabling further assessment of how effectively a system withstands, absorbs, and recovers from disruptive events and stress conditions.

</details>


### [190] [Kant-Q: A Q-Learning Algorithm for Kantian Equilibrium in Symmetric Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34536)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Sujit Gujar,Yadati Narahari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出康德式强化学习算法，使对称博弈中的智能体学习共同采取同一行动，并证明该方法可收敛至康德均衡；实验显示其在囚徒困境和渔业资源管理任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习通常以纳什均衡为目标，但混合动机博弈中的纳什均衡可能帕累托低效。由于相同的人工智能系统可能彼此交互，论文转而研究一种促进共同利益的解概念：每个智能体选择自己希望所有智能体共同采取的行动，即康德均衡。

Method: 作者提出康德式动作价值学习算法，限制智能体学习所有人采取相同动作的行动组合。这样，每次更新都可视为来自单智能体马尔可夫决策过程的样本，因此能够继承标准动作价值学习的收敛保证。算法让各智能体独立更新各自的价值表，并依据贪心策略选择行动。

Result: 理论上，论文证明每个对称马尔可夫博弈都存在纯策略康德均衡；所有智能体的价值表收敛到相同极限，其贪心策略对应一个康德均衡。当每个状态下的最优共同动作唯一时，贪心行动也会收敛。此外，在一类对称单调博弈中，康德均衡相较于纯策略组合具有帕累托效率，并满足严格的个体理性。实验中，该算法在重复囚徒困境和最多含两百个智能体的渔业管理任务中学得康德均衡，表现优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在对称交互中，要求智能体考虑所有同类系统共同采取某一行动，可形成具有理论收敛保障的合作学习机制。康德式普遍化原则因此可作为设计人工智能系统间合作行为的一项思路；其适用范围仍受对称性及相关博弈条件限制。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly interact with one another, and many of these interactions are mixed-motive games, which have posed a persistent challenge for learning algorithms. Most multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithms are designed to reach Nash equilibria, yet in many such games these equilibria are Pareto-inefficient. We instead study Kantian equilibria, an economic solution concept in which each agent plays the action it would want all agents to adopt. In symmetric games, this corresponds to an optimal common policy. We propose Kant-Q, a Q-learning algorithm in which each agent learns action profiles where all agents play the same action. Each update is then a sample from a single-agent MDP, so Kant-Q inherits the convergence guarantees of standard Q-learning. We show that every symmetric Markov game has a pure Kantian equilibrium. We also prove that all agents' Q-tables converge to the same limit, whose greedy policy is such an equilibrium, and that greedy play converges whenever the optimal common action is unique at every state. We further show that in a subclass of symmetric monotone games, the Kantian equilibrium is Pareto-efficient among pure strategy profiles and strictly individually rational. Empirically, on the iterated Prisoner's Dilemma and the GovSim fishery with up to 200 agents, Kant-Q learns the Kantian equilibrium, while the baselines underperform. Since copies of the same AI system naturally face one another in symmetric games, these results suggest universalization as a design principle for cooperative AI-AI interaction.

</details>


### [191] [Investigating the efficacy of vortex beam speckle patterns for the recognition of atmospheric turbulence and fractional topological charge with a deep learning model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34573)
*Ratikanta Swain,Allarakha Shikder,Naseem Babu,Niladri Sarkar,Aryan Raj,Raghavan K. Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文探索仅凭单幅散斑图像回归标量涡旋光束的分数拓扑荷，并评估大气湍流的影响。现有结果显示，无湍流时预测准确；湍流增强后误差明显上升，表明方法仍受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数拓扑荷可扩展轨道角动量通信的可用模式，但大气湍流会扭曲光束，使干涉或衍射测量依赖完整光束形态与精确对准。既有学习方法较少处理细密变化的拓扑荷和湍流条件，因此论文研究能否直接从一幅散斑图中读出拓扑荷，以及这种读出在多强湍流下会失效。

Method: 通过数值传播模拟光束在1500米路径上经过150个冯·卡门相位屏，再由固定随机散射体产生散斑，记录不同拓扑荷和湍流强度下的图像。研究使用其中2万幅、分辨率为256×256的16位图像训练卷积神经网络；网络回归拓扑荷，并以辅助输出识别湍流类别。测试还包含训练中未出现的拓扑荷。

Result: 在统一强度尺度下，无湍流时拓扑荷平均绝对误差为0.031；湍流强度参数为10和50时，误差分别升至0.78和1.85，仍低于恒定预测基线的2.5。湍流类别识别准确率为78%。作者指出，拓扑荷和湍流都会影响散斑颗粒尺寸，可能导致二者难以区分。

Conclusion: 现有训练规模下，该方法仅在无湍流条件下实现高精度拓扑荷读取；湍流增强会显著削弱性能。研究证明了单幅散斑图回归的可行性，但尚未解决强湍流下的可靠识别问题。结论目前基于数值模拟，实验验证以及向矢量涡旋光束的扩展仍待开展。

Abstract: Fractional topological charge (TC) enlarges the usable mode set of orbital angular momentum (OAM) links, but reading it reliably after atmospheric turbulence remains difficult. Interferometric and diffractive methods need an undistorted beam and a careful alignment, while most learned approaches treat integer or coarsely spaced charges and do not include turbulence. Here we ask whether the charge of a scalar vortex beam can be regressed directly from a single speckle image, and at which turbulence strength this stops working. We propagate vortex beams over 1500~m through 150 von Kármán phase screens, scatter them with a fixed random diffuser, and record $256\times256$, 16-bit speckle images for TC from 0 to 10 in steps of 0.01 and turbulence strengths $D/r_0$ from 0 to 50; the results reported here use a subset of 20\,000 images, and a data set of $10^{6}$ images is in preparation. A convolutional neural network estimates the TC and, through an auxiliary head, the turbulence category. On charges withheld from training, the mean absolute error is 0.031 without turbulence (with a common intensity scale) and rises to 0.78 at $D/r_0=10$ and 1.85 at $D/r_0=50$, against 2.5 for a constant prediction, and the turbulence category is identified correctly in 78\% of the images. The results show that, at the present training size, the charge is read accurately only without turbulence, and that the charge and the turbulence oth act on the speckle grain size, which may limit the readout. Experimental validation and the extension to vector vortex beams are left for future work.

</details>


### [192] [Tune for Retention, Not Just Gain: SFT Forgets Far Less than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34579)
*Nirmit Joshi,Nathan Srebro,Eran Malach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，监督微调并非必然导致灾难性遗忘；通过联合调整学习率、批量大小等超参数，可以在提升新任务表现的同时较好保留既有能力，其效果可与旨在减少遗忘的在线策略相竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型微调可能使模型严重丧失先前习得的能力。已有研究常将这种遗忘归因于监督微调的离线学习特性，并认为在线学习是实现持续学习的关键。本文旨在检验这一解释是否成立。

Method: 研究比较不同训练方式及超参数设置下的新任务增益与旧能力保留情况，重点考察学习率和批量大小，并将超参数选择同时依据任务增益和能力保留进行评估；同时与一种在线自蒸馏微调方法进行比较。

Result: 追求新任务性能最大化时，监督微调确实可能引发灾难性遗忘；但采用较低学习率和较小批量大小，也能取得相近的新任务增益，同时显著减少遗忘。经过兼顾增益与保留的超参数选择，普通监督微调对既有能力的保留优于先前观点所暗示的水平，并可与在线自蒸馏微调相竞争。后者对超参数变化可能更稳健，但仍需依据能力保留来筛选配置。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了“在线训练是实现学习且不遗忘的关键”这一观点。灾难性遗忘可能更多取决于训练目标与超参数选择，而非单由训练是否在线决定；其具体成因仍值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting, the drastic loss of previously acquired capabilities when learning new ones, is a central challenge in language model fine-tuning. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely believed to be prone to such forgetting. Recent works have attributed this to the off-policy nature of SFT, arguing that on-policy learning is key to continual learning. In this paper, we challenge this premise. We show that while maximizing new-task performance can induce catastrophic forgetting, alternative hyperparameter choices (lower learning rate and batch size) can yield similar gains with little to no forgetting. With hyperparameters selected jointly for gain and retention, vanilla SFT retains prior capabilities far better than previously suggested and can be competitive with Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT), an on-policy approach designed to mitigate forgetting. While SDFT can be more robust to hyperparameter choices, it also requires a retention-aware criterion to identify hyperparameter configurations that avoid catastrophic forgetting. Our results challenge the view that on-policy training is what enables learning with retention and raise broader questions about what drives catastrophic forgetting during fine-tuning.

</details>


### [193] [ChemoMAE: Self-Supervised Spectral Representation Learning Reveals Chemical Heterogeneity in Historical Wood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34581)
*Ryuji Yamaguchi,Miyuki Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用自监督学习分析日本历史木建筑木材的近红外高光谱图像，识别与木材解剖结构及长期暴露相关的空间退化模式，并据此提出内部和表面退化的机制假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史木材历经数百年的环境作用，且不同解剖区域的化学组成存在差异，因此退化模式复杂。理解这些模式的形成机制有助于历史建筑的保护与修复，但相关机制仍不清楚；同时，不同木种和服役经历也使样本间比较更具挑战。

Method: 研究分析了日本多处历史建筑中的49份木材横截面近红外高光谱图像。采用自监督变换器，通过遮蔽后去噪学习像素级光谱表征，并利用光谱变量间的相关性；随后对表征进行聚类，生成无需预设化学状态类别的空间分布图。研究还提出邻域标签一致性指标，用于衡量相邻像素聚类标签的一致程度，并结合木材解剖结构、服役历史和既有研究解释结果。

Result: 在验证样本中，仅使用光谱信息的自监督模型，其平均邻域标签一致性高于基线方法。对全部样本重新训练并聚类后，所得空间结构与木材解剖特征及预期的表面暴露影响相吻合。基于跨样本的区域光谱与空间分布比较，研究提出了木材内部退化和表面退化的机制假说。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种结合自监督学习与领域知识的探索性材料分析框架，可帮助研究者从模型学习到的潜在空间模式出发，提出并检验历史木材状态形成机制的假说；这些退化机制仍属于有待进一步验证的解释。

Abstract: In historical timber buildings, multiple environmental factors act over centuries on wood whose chemical composition varies across anatomical regions, producing complex patterns of degradation. Understanding how timber members degrade is important for the effective conservation and restoration of historical buildings, yet the mechanisms underlying these patterns remain unclear. To investigate these mechanisms, we used self-supervised learning to establish a common basis for comparing specimens from timber members that differ in wood species and service history. We examined transverse sections of 49 wood specimens from several historical buildings in Japan using near-infrared hyperspectral imaging. ChemoMAE, a self-supervised Transformer, learns pixelwise spectral representations through masked denoising that exploits multicollinearity among spectral variables. Clustering these representations yields spatial maps without predefined chemical-state categories. Because anatomy and degradation can produce spatially continuous regions, we formulated Local Label Agreement (LLA) to quantify agreement among labels assigned to neighboring pixels. Using spectra alone, ChemoMAE yielded higher mean LLA than baseline methods on validation specimens. We then trained ChemoMAE anew and fitted cluster centers across all specimens. Spatial structures consistent with wood anatomy and the expected effects of surface exposure emerged in the resulting maps. By cross-referencing regional spectra and spatial distributions across specimens in light of wood anatomy, service histories, and prior studies, we proposed models of internal and surface degradation as mechanistic hypotheses. This exploratory AI for Science framework gives researchers a basis for linking latent patterns learned through self-supervision with domain knowledge when investigating how material states form.

</details>


### [194] [Learning from Crowds with Unsupervised Item Response Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34602)
*Pascal Wullschleger,Majid Zarharan,Donnacha Daly,Marc Pouly,Jennifer Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将众包标注建模为无监督项目反应理论问题，并评估其对标注者与任务特征估计、标签推断及分类器训练的作用。结果显示，该方法在特征恢复和校准稳定性方面有优势，但在标签推断或分类性能上并非总是最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包标注中的标注者能力和任务难度存在差异，简单多数投票难以充分利用这些信息。论文希望借助项目反应理论刻画标注者与任务的潜在特征，并考察这种建模能否改善标签推断和分类器训练。

Method: 研究将标注者视为能力不同的答题者，将标注任务视为难度不同的题目，评估多种无监督逻辑项目反应理论模型。在六个具有众包标签和已知真实标签的数据集上，将无监督模型与使用真实标签训练的监督模型比较，以检验能力、难度和区分度等特征的恢复效果及标签推断表现。此外，研究利用项目反应理论信息指导分类器训练，并与现有众包学习方法比较。

Result: 在多数数据集上，无监督模型能够较可靠地恢复标注者和任务特征。与多数基线相比，项目反应理论模型的标签推断表现较稳定，但不一定达到最佳水平。众包学习方法能够提升相较于多数投票基线的Ｆ１指标，却没有任何方法同时显著改善Ｆ１指标和概率校准。项目反应理论引导的课程学习方法较能维持校准，但Ｆ１表现未达到领先水平；Ｆ１最高的方法则往往存在校准偏差。

Conclusion: 项目反应理论为分析众包标注中的标注者与任务差异提供了有效框架，尤其适用于潜在特征恢复及较稳定的标签推断。不过，其对分类性能的收益取决于数据集和评价目标；实践中需要在Ｆ１表现与概率校准之间权衡。

Abstract: Item Response Theory (IRT) is the study of tests, including the properties of both test items and test takers. Crowdsourced labelling can be cast as an unsupervised IRT problem: annotators play the role of test takers with varying ability, and annotation tasks play the role of items with varying difficulty. We evaluate unsupervised variants of standard logistic IRT models for latent trait recovery and label inference on six datasets with crowdsourced labels and known ground truths. Unsupervised IRT models can recover annotator and item traits (e.g., ability, difficulty and discrimination) reliably across most datasets when compared to supervised variants trained on the ground truth. IRT models seem to be more stable in label inference compared to most baselines, but might not be the best possible choice depending on the dataset. We further propose to use IRT models to inform classifiers during training and compare against existing crowd-learning methods. We find that crowd-learning methods can improve F1 over majority-vote baselines, but no method significantly improves both F1 and calibration. IRT-informed curriculum methods seem to preserve calibration better than other crowd-learning methods, but are not at the frontier regarding F1, and the best crowd-learning methods in terms of F1 seem to be miscalibrated.

</details>


### [195] [Automated Batch-Variable Selection for ComBat-Based Radiomic Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34618)
*Jose Lozano-Montoya,Gemma Urbanos,Carmen Prieto-de-la-Lastra,Emilio Soria-Olivas,Ana Jimenez-Pastor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 帕尔西康巴特通过数据驱动方式筛选用于康巴特校正的影像采集参数，在去除技术批次效应与保留特征结构之间取得平衡，并在无需校正时主动弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心影像组学常用康巴特方法消除不同中心或设备造成的批次效应，但校正变量通常依赖人工选择，缺乏统一标准。选择过多参数可能破坏特征结构；若技术差异本就很小，校正也可能没有必要。因此需要一种可复现的参数筛选方法，并能判断何时应当不做校正。

Method: 帕尔西康巴特先依据批次效应对候选采集参数排序，再剔除冗余参数，并逐步评估康巴特校正效果，以平衡批次效应去除和特征结构保留；当证据表明技术变异可忽略时则弃权。研究通过模拟、肺癌计算机断层扫描数据、乳腺磁共振数据，以及临床预测任务进行评估，并与人工选择、逐步纳入所有可用参数和自动康巴特方法比较。

Result: 模拟中，该方法在存在真实批次变量时有百分之九十二点九的试验成功识别该变量，在无批次效应时有百分之九十九点二的对照成功弃权。计算机断层扫描中，仅校正重建核便将批次效应检验值从零点二七六降至零点零三二，接近校正六个变量的效果。磁共振中，校正层厚和制造商后，该值从零点六八零降至零点三二五，略优于人工选择，但不及校正七个变量的方案。各校正策略均未显著改善临床预测区分能力，曲线下面积与帕尔西康巴特之间也无显著差异。

Conclusion: 该方法以较少且可依据采集方案核验的参数去除技术批次效应，同时尽量保留特征结构和临床预测能力，并能在无需校正时弃权。它为多中心影像组学提供了更透明、可复现的校正流程；不过，批次效应减弱并不必然带来临床预测性能提升。

Abstract: ComBat harmonizes multicentre radiomic features, but batch variables are usually chosen manually, without a standardized criterion across image acquisition parameters. We propose ParsiComBat, which selects acquisition parameters for ComBat, balancing batch-effect removal against feature-structure preservation and abstaining when technical variation is negligible.
  ParsiComBat ranks candidate acquisition parameters by batch effect, filters redundancy, and scores sequential ComBat corrections. We evaluated it in three experiments: in simulations, testing whether it recovered a known batch variable and abstained without one; in CT (NSCLC-Radiomics) and MRI (MAMA-MIA) cohorts, comparing batch-effect removal against manufacturer (manual), every feasible parameter (greedy) and AutoCombat; and in clinical prediction, internally and externally.
  In simulations, ParsiComBat identified the true batch variable in 92.9% of positive trials and abstained in 99.2% of null controls. In CT, correcting for reconstruction kernel reduced mean k-nearest neighbour batch-effect test from 0.276 to 0.032, almost matching greedy correction of six variables. In MRI, correcting for slice thickness and manufacturer reduced it from 0.680 to 0.325, slightly more than manual selection but less than greedy correction of seven variables. Removing technical variation did not improve clinical discrimination: no strategy, including raw features, differed significantly from ParsiComBat in area under the curve.
  ParsiComBat provides a data-driven framework that removes technical batch effects using minimal acquisition parameters, preserving feature structure and clinical discrimination. It selects variables verifiable against the acquisition protocol and abstains when no correction is needed, supporting transparent, reproducible multicentre radiomics.

</details>


### [196] [DecisionReplay: A Corpus and Offline Simulator for Ensembling Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34627)
*Dean Ariel,Seffi Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出决策重放数据集，可在不重新调用模型的情况下，快速模拟集成、路由与级联策略，并比较其准确率和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较不同模型组合与调用策略，通常需要取得每个模型对每个样本的输出，代价高昂；现有用于路由研究的数据也缺少各候选答案的概率信息，难以公平、快速地复现实验。

Method: 构建决策重放数据集，收录17个商业决策模型在35个基准上的答案选项概率向量，并提供每次调用的令牌数、计费成本和实测延迟。研究者可通过确定性模拟器重放任意选择、组合或级联策略，无须新增模型调用。

Result: 每个基准上精心挑选的单一模型表现很强：汇总所有模型的集成准确率比该模型低2.7个百分点；堆叠集成虽提高1.0个百分点，但成本是其3.4倍。模型错误具有较强相关性：两个模型都答错时，72%的情况下会选中相同错误选项。最佳路由器以该单一模型64%的成本达到相同表现；经过认证的提前退出策略可在不改变答案的前提下，将堆叠集成成本降低43%。

Conclusion: 模型数量增加并不必然带来更好的准确率或性价比，模型间错误相关性会限制集成收益。该数据集与模拟器为评估路由、集成和级联方法提供了低成本、可复现的工具，也便于检验新方法是否真正改变现有结论。

Abstract: Comparing ensembles, routers and cascades requires the output of every model on every item, and its cost. Stored collections built for routing keep no per-option probabilities. We provide a dataset on which ensemble methods, routers and cascades are simulated quickly, without new model calls. DecisionReplay holds the probability vectors of 17 commercial decision models, endpoints that return a probability for every answer option, on 35 benchmarks, with the token counts, billed cost and measured latency of every call, all released. A deterministic simulator replays any policy that selects, combines or cascades them. One well-chosen model per benchmark is hard to beat: pooling all models loses 2.7 accuracy points to it, and stacking gains 1.0 points at 3.4 times its cost. The models are wrong together: two that are both wrong choose the same wrong option in 72% of cases. The best router matches that model at 64% of its cost, and certified early exit cuts the cost of a stacked ensemble by 43% without changing an answer. DecisionReplay makes it cheap and fast to test whether a new method changes these results.

</details>


### [197] [Predicting acute food crises and discovering actionable drivers with causal machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34629)
*Mélissande Machefer,Michele Ronco,Michele Meroni,Jordi Cerdà-Bautista,Francesco Collivignarelli,Chungmann Kim,Nicolas Rost,Alessia Matanó,Esther Rodrigo-Bonet,Philipp Zimmer,Gustau Camps-Valls,Christina Corbane,Felix Rembold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究整合多类危机驱动因素，建立可应对缺失数据的粮食不安全预测流程，并发现预测重要性与因果作用并不等同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性粮食不安全通常由冲突、气候异常、人口流离失所和经济冲击共同造成，但现有预测研究对驱动因素的覆盖有限，也较少在不同危机分类体系下开展大规模、可比较的预测与因果分析。

Method: 研究扩充开放获取的协调化粮食不安全数据集，加入驱动因素变量，覆盖80个国家、月度及次国家级数据，时间跨度为2007至2024年。研究构建可应对缺失数据的机器学习流程，分别提前3、6、9个月预测危机发生；同时采用嵌套交叉验证评估模型，并以树模型解释方法分析预测依据，再将预测重要性与双重稳健因果估计所得的可干预因素因果效应进行比较。

Result: 在基于粮食危机预警网络和综合粮食安全阶段分类的预测任务中，模型的精确率—召回率曲线下面积较无技能基线高出15至50个百分点。模型表现与危机发生率相关，而非与分类体系相关。解释分析识别出三类反复出现的预测模式，且预测信号具有复合性和地域差异。急性冲突事件的预测信号最弱，却是危机分类的主要因果驱动因素；只有农业气候因素的预测重要性与因果效应方向相吻合。

Conclusion: 多因素机器学习能够提前预测急性粮食不安全，但预测能力不能直接代表因果作用。尤其是冲突可能因预测信号弱而被低估，却仍是重要的可干预因果驱动因素；因此，风险预测与政策干预排序应结合预测分析和因果分析，而不能仅依据模型重要性。

Abstract: Acute food insecurity is driven by a compound of conflict, adverse climate events, displacement, and economic shocks. We enrich the open-access Harmonized Food Insecurity Dataset with driver variables (monthly, subnational, 80 countries, 2007--2024) and develop missing-data-resilient machine learning pipelines forecasting crisis occurrence at 3, 6, and 9-month lead times using both FEWS NET and, for the first time at scale, IPC/CH classifications. Pipelines, evaluated through nested cross-validation, outperform no-skill baselines by 15 to 50 percentage points in PR-AUC, with performance correlated with crisis prevalence rather than the classification system. TreeSHAP-based explainability reveals compound, geography-specific predictive signal profiles, with three recurrent forecasting archetypes. Comparing predictive importance against doubly robust causal estimates of actionable drivers, we find that acute conflict events, having the weakest predictive signals, emerge as primary causal drivers of crisis classification whilst agroclimatology is the sole dimension in which predictive importance and causal effect align.

</details>


### [198] [Distractor-Robust Reinforcement Learning via Variational Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34641)
*Benjamin Freed,Vivaan Singh,Dean Zadok,Roberto Calandra,Jeff Schneider,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双模拟关系的潜在状态学习目标，以奖励和状态转移预测取代像素重建，保留任务相关信息并抑制干扰因素；该方法在多种视觉及高维状态任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习虽有望提高数据利用效率与泛化能力，但常见的重建目标会促使模型耗费表示能力去拟合与任务无关的视觉干扰，导致潜在状态未能聚焦于决策所需信息。本文旨在学习足以支持奖励预测和动力学预测、同时忽略无关变化的状态表示。

Method: 提出一种变分推断与双模拟相结合的潜在状态学习方法，以对抗式目标替代像素重建，要求潜在状态能够预测奖励及下一时刻的潜在状态。作者证明，该目标可衡量编码器偏离奖励充分性和动力学充分性的程度；若能够达到精确充分性，其全局最优解会使历史状态形成双模拟关系。再结合固定容量的潜在瓶颈，促使表示保留任务相关信息、舍弃无关变化。该方法可直接替换既有模型式强化学习算法中的模型学习模块，且不依赖图像专用的数据增强。

Result: 该方法在含干扰因素的控制基准上达到先进水平，并扩展至视觉复杂的真实感操作任务；在高维向量状态任务中也有效，包括具有一百个连杆的游泳机器人。潜在空间可视化和近邻探测进一步表明，学得的表示对任务相关结构敏感、对干扰因素具有不变性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无须重建原始观测，也可通过奖励与动力学充分性来学习适用于控制的紧凑状态表示。该方法兼顾任务相关信息保留与干扰抑制，适用于视觉及非视觉的高维任务，展现了较强的性能与通用性。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) promises data efficiency and generalization, but typical reconstruction-based objectives encourage models to waste representational capacity on task-irrelevant distractors. We introduce VIBES (Variational Inference for Bisimulation-based Encoded States), a new objective that replaces pixel reconstruction with an adversarial term, which enforces that latent states suffice to predict both rewards and the next latent state. We show that this objective measures how far the encoder is from reward and dynamics sufficiency, and that whenever exact sufficiency is attainable, its global optima induce bisimulation relations on histories. Combined with a fixed-capacity latent bottleneck, this encourages latent states to retain task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant variation. Our method serves as a drop-in replacement for Dreamer's model-learning component, achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Distracting Control Suite, and extends to visually complex manipulation in Realistic ManiSkill. Unlike prior approaches, it does not rely on image-specific augmentations and applies equally well to high-dimensional vector-state tasks, demonstrated on a 100-link swimmer robot. Finally, latent-space analyses (UMAP embeddings and nearest-neighbor probes) confirm that the learned representations are sensitive to task-relevant structure while invariant to distractors.

</details>


### [199] [Do We Still Need Reinforcement Learning When We Can Read the World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34656)
*Mingchao Zhang,Miduo Cui,Kangze He,Yuanjun Li,Dapeng Li,Ce Zhang,Chuanchen Luo,Xue Yan,Mingzhe Xing,Zhumin Chen,Hangyu Mao,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出让基于大语言模型的编程智能体阅读环境代码，直接合成并迭代改进控制策略；实验显示，这种方式在多种基准上达到或超过传统强化学习方法，同时显著减少所需交互经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通常需要大量与环境交互，才能学到有效策略。基于模型的强化学习虽利用世界模型辅助规划或策略优化，但论文希望探索另一种路径：直接从可读的环境模型中提取知识，减少对试错和针对具体任务训练的依赖。

Method: 让自主的大语言模型编程智能体检查环境源代码，将环境知识转化为可执行的决策规则，并通过在线反馈或离线数据迭代优化策略。该方法不进行针对具体任务的强化学习训练；论文还将其与无法读取源代码的策略生成方式进行比较，并在更具挑战性的条件下开展实验。

Result: 在多种决策基准上，合成策略的表现与传统强化学习方法相当或更优，且所需交互经验显著更少。进一步分析显示，策略包含可解释的决策规则，并会随反馈逐步演化；能够读取源代码的智能体也表现出优势。

Conclusion: 研究表明，可读世界模型不仅能用于规划或策略优化，也能直接支持策略合成。借助源代码理解和反馈驱动的改进，智能体有望减少试错并降低对针对具体任务的强化学习训练的依赖。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in sequential decision-making, but learning effective policies often requires substantial interaction experience. Model-based reinforcement learning alleviates this dependence by leveraging world models, which are typically used to support planning or policy optimization. In this paper, we investigate an alternative paradigm in which autonomous LLM-based coding agents directly synthesize control policies by reasoning over readable world models. By examining environment source code, these agents translate environmental knowledge into executable decision rules and iteratively refine their policies using online feedback or offline data, without task-specific RL training. Experiments across diverse decision-making benchmarks show that synthesized policies match or outperform conventional RL methods while requiring substantially less experience. Further analyses reveal interpretable decision rules and how they evolve through feedback-driven refinement. Comparisons with policies generated without source code access and experiments under challenging conditions further demonstrate the benefits of readable world models. These findings suggest that direct policy synthesis can reduce reliance on trial and error, challenging the necessity of task-specific RL training.

</details>


### [200] [Learning to Be Truncated: Preparing LLM Checkpoints for SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34667)
*Chenyue Fang,Danila Mishin,Babak Falsafi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多种压缩预算的模型准备方法，使奇异值分解压缩后的模型无需分别微调，也能获得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于奇异值分解的压缩通常会造成模型性能下降，因而需要额外微调或权重修正；而不同压缩比例往往还要重复进行专门优化，增加了部署成本。本文旨在用一次准备过程改善多个压缩比例下的模型表现。

Method: 作者先选定一个压缩比例，以原始模型为教师，对其低秩近似进行微调。随后将学习到的更新合并回原始权重，同时保留训练前向过程中未使用的互补权重分量。最后，对准备后的目标矩阵进行奇异值分解，并在不同截断秩下复用该分解结果。

Result: 在拉玛七十亿参数模型上，单个准备模型在三种压缩比例下均带来准确率提升。提升效果还扩展到不同模型家族、模型规模及奇异值分解压缩算子。作者通过梯度分析和早期训练更新，提供了局部证据，说明在一种压缩比例下学得的更新可能有益于其他比例。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，可以通过一次针对低秩近似的准备过程，改善多个压缩预算下的模型性能，避免为每个压缩模型单独微调。不过，摘要未提供具体准确率、计算成本或适用范围的定量细节。

Abstract: Compression based on singular value decomposition (SVD) often requires subsequent fine-tuning or weight correction to mitigate performance degradation. Budget-specific optimization must be repeated for different compression ratios. We introduce LLift, which prepares a pretrained model for SVD compression across ratios without further fine-tuning. LLift fine-tunes a low-rank approximation at a single ratio using the original model as a teacher. We merge the learned updates into the original weights while preserving the complementary components excluded from the training forward pass. We apply SVD to the prepared model's target matrices and reuse the decompositions at different truncation ranks. On LLaMA-7B, we demonstrate accuracy gains across three compression ratios using a single prepared model. The gains extend across model families, model sizes, and SVD compression operators. We analyze gradients and an early training update to provide local evidence that updates learned at one ratio can benefit compression at other ratios. These results show that LLift can improve compression across deployment budgets without fine-tuning each compressed model separately.

</details>


### [201] [Provably Efficient Concave Utility Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34678)
*Max Qiushi Lin,Ahmed Magd,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对凹效用强化学习中的高维占用测度估计与样本效率问题，提出基于双线性马尔可夫决策过程的乐观策略优化框架，并给出具有理论样本复杂度保证的在策略和离策略算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凹效用强化学习涵盖标准强化学习、模仿学习和最大熵探索等任务，但通常需要估计高维状态—动作占用测度。已有方法要么回避探索问题，要么局限于表格情形，要么缺乏计算高效的策略优化；部分既有工作还依赖积分预言机。因此，研究目标是在具有低维结构的模型中，高效估计占用测度并进行策略优化。

Method: 研究有限时域、分回合的双线性马尔可夫决策过程，利用已知特征空间中的双线性转移结构，将占用测度估计转化为低维特征期望估计，并构建基于模型的乐观凹效用策略优化框架。在在策略算法中，每轮使用独立轨迹估计凹效用策略梯度；在离策略算法中，提出反向特征回归子程序，复用历史策略收集的轨迹来估计当前策略的占用测度。进一步将框架扩展至线性马尔可夫决策过程。

Result: 在策略算法的样本复杂度为约束阶̃O(ε⁻⁴)。在满足适当策略类假设时，离策略算法的样本复杂度降至约束阶̃O(ε⁻²)，并存在可控的投影误差；例如，具有足够表达能力的对数线性策略满足相关假设。在线性马尔可夫决策过程中，离策略算法仍保持相同的样本复杂度。其关于精度参数的依赖与采用线性函数逼近的标准强化学习相匹配，且算法无需积分预言机。

Conclusion: 利用双线性结构和历史数据复用，可以在非表格环境下高效优化凹效用目标。所提出的离策略方法显著改善了精度相关的样本复杂度，并将理论保证扩展到线性马尔可夫决策过程，为多类强化学习问题提供了统一且计算可行的解决框架。

Abstract: Concave utility reinforcement learning (CURL) unifies a diverse range of problems, including standard reinforcement learning (RL), imitation learning, and maximum entropy exploration. Unlike standard RL, CURL generally requires estimating high-dimensional state-action occupancy measures. Consequently, existing methods for CURL either sidestep the exploration problem, are restricted to the tabular setting, or lack computationally efficient policy optimization. To address this, we study CURL in finite-horizon episodic bilinear MDPs, where the transition model admits a low-dimensional representation in known feature spaces. We use the bilinear structure to efficiently estimate occupancy measures via low-dimensional feature expectations, and propose a model-based optimistic concave utility policy optimization (OCUPO) framework. We first instantiate OCUPO in the on-policy setting, where each iteration uses independent trajectories to estimate the concave utility policy gradient, and prove an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity. To improve sample efficiency, we then instantiate OCUPO in the off-policy setting. We propose a novel backward feature regression subroutine that reuses trajectories collected by past policies to estimate the occupancy measure of the current policy. Under suitable technical assumptions on the policy class (satisfied, e.g., by log-linear policies with sufficiently expressive features), we prove that the resulting algorithm achieves an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity, up to a controllable projection error. We further extend our framework to linear MDPs and show that the resulting off-policy algorithm retains the same sample complexity. In these settings, the dependence on $ε$ matches that for standard RL with linear function approximation. Moreover, our algorithms do not rely on the integral oracle required by prior work.

</details>


### [202] [Evacuation Traffic Prediction for Dynamic Transportation Networks over Multiple Hurricanes: A Generalized Graph Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34687)
*Md Nafees Fuad Rafi,Samiul Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种动态图卷积长短期记忆模型，利用十二场历史飓风数据预测疏散交通，能较好迁移至未见过的飓风，并优于多种基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飓风疏散期间交通需求高且变化迅速，拥堵会造成延误与安全风险。由于飓风特征、居民响应及检测器网络结构各不相同，单场飓风数据训练的模型往往难以推广至其他飓风，因此需要具备跨事件泛化能力的实时交通预测方法。

Method: 研究整合十二场历史飓风的交通检测器与疏散数据，构建动态图学习框架，采用图卷积长短期记忆模型，利用交通网络随时间变化的结构预测未来最长六小时的疏散交通。另设计模块化训练方法，逐步纳入不同飓风的数据，以评估增加训练事件对模型表现的影响。

Result: 在未参与训练的二〇二四年飓风米尔顿数据上，模型的一小时预测均方根误差为二百八十点六，决定系数为零点九六；整体六小时预测的均方根误差为四百五十九点八，决定系数为零点八九。模型在整体六小时预测中优于先进基线模型；与静态版本相比，动态图模型的均方根误差降低百分之八点四。

Conclusion: 该模型能够利用多场飓风数据学习具有迁移性的时空交通规律，对未见飓风的疏散交通进行较准确的提前预测。研究成果可辅助交通管理部门预判未来路况并采取主动措施，以缓解疏散延误。

Abstract: High traffic demand during hurricane evacuations causes severe congestion on major highways, leading to delays and safety risks for evacuees. Predicting evacuation traffic in real-time can significantly enhance evacuation management efforts. However, evacuation traffic is highly dynamic and uncertain due to evolving hurricane characteristics and diverse population response. Each hurricane has unique characteristics and evacuation contexts, resulting in different spatial-temporal traffic patterns. Moreover, the structure of detector-based transportation networks varies across multiple hurricanes. Hence, a model trained on the data from one hurricane may not generalize well for others. This study proposes a generalized dynamic graph learning framework using traffic detectors and evacuation data from 12 historical hurricanes. Leveraging the dynamic nature of transportation network, we develop a Graph Convolutional Long Short-Term Memory (GCN-LSTM) model that predicts evacuation traffic up to 6 hours in advance. For an unseen hurricane (Milton, 2024), the model shows strong generalizability, achieving an RMSE of 280.6 and R2 of 0.96 for 1-hour prediction, and an RMSE of 459.8 and R2 of 0.89 for overall 6-hours prediction. We also introduce a modular training approach, where we evaluate performance improvements by sequentially incorporating data from multiple hurricanes. The proposed model outperforms state-of-the-art baseline models for overall 6-hour prediction horizons. Ablation experiments show an 8.4% improvement in RMSE for the proposed dynamic GCN-LSTM model compared to its static counterpart. Traffic management officials can use the developed model to understand future traffic conditions during hurricane evacuation in advance and take proactive measures to reduce delays during evacuation.

</details>


### [203] [Effect of class proportion shifts on classifier curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34702)
*Maxim Bolshim,Alexander Kugaevskikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，类别占比变化与输出层损失曲率变化相关；利用初始类别梯度和训练类别比例，可以更准确地预测这种变化，但类别占比效应尚未与训练图像差异完全分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别构成变化可能改变平均损失，即使分类器本身没有变化。研究旨在区分这种由类别重新加权造成的影响，并考察类别占比变化是否能预测损失曲率的变化。

Method: 在十类图像分类任务中，针对两个目标类别分别设置训练分支，并在固定目标类图像上计算损失关于输出层参数的海森矩阵迹。特征保持冻结，只更新输出层；实验跨十次新初始化重复。研究者先依据首轮实验设计预测方法，再不作修改地用于后续前瞻性测试，并比较该方法、零变化预测及等权类别梯度预测。

Result: 经过六十次输出层更新，目标类占比从百分之十升至百分之十九的分支中，海森矩阵迹平均下降百分之零点一五二；占比降至百分之一的分支中，平均上升百分之零点零七一。对于直接从百分之十升至百分之十九的前瞻性测试，新预测方法的平均绝对误差为零点零二二个百分点，优于零变化预测的零点一五二和等权梯度预测的零点一零四。纳入实际分配的类别比例后，预测效果有所改善。

Conclusion: 初始类别梯度结合训练类别比例，能够有效预测类别占比上升时输出层损失曲率的变化。不过，现有实验无法将类别占比的作用与所分配训练图像的作用完全区分，因此结果支持预测能力，但不足以单独确立类别占比变化的因果效应。

Abstract: Changing the class mix can alter mean loss without changing the classifier. To distinguish this reweighting from changes in the model itself, we studied ten-class CIFAR-10 classification in separate branches for two target classes, measuring the trace of the loss Hessian with respect to the output-layer parameters on fixed images of each target class. Across ten new initialisations, after 60 updates to the output layer with frozen features, the trace changed by -0.152% on average in branches where the target class share rose from 10% to 19%, and by +0.071% in branches where it fell to 1%, relative to each branch's initial trace. These changes do not isolate the effect of class share from that of the assigned training images. We developed a forecast based on initial class gradients after examining the first experiment's results and then tested it prospectively without modification. For direct increases from 10% to 19%, rather than increases after a deficit, its mean absolute error was 0.022 percentage points on the initial-trace scale, compared with 0.152 for a zero-change forecast and 0.104 for equally weighted gradients. Using the assigned class proportions improved the forecast of how much the trace would change.

</details>


### [204] [Maco-RL: Scaling Agent Capabilities with Mixed-Task Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34703)
*Jiacheng Zhang,Qixing Zhou,Haiyang Wang,Qipeng Gu,Shuzhe Wu,Feiyang Pan,Lue Fan,Dandan Tu,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的混合任务强化学习方法，通过异步配额调度和三缓冲区采样，提升多领域训练的稳定性、计算分配效率与持续扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合任务强化学习可用统一策略跨领域训练，避免分别训练专门策略或额外蒸馏，但规模扩大后容易出现训练不稳定、滚动采样计算资源分配不当，以及新增领域时难以兼顾探索与已有能力保持等问题。

Method: 作者提出 Maco-RL。其一，采用基于配额的异步调度器管理多个任务领域的滚动采样，通过平衡不同领域对梯度的贡献来稳定训练，并减轻离策略影响。其二，采用三缓冲区采样，根据样本掌握程度分配计算资源，兼顾前沿探索与已习得能力的保留。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，Maco-RL 将通义千问 3.5 的 270 亿参数版本总体得分从 43.6 提升至 54.2；持续扩展后进一步达到 60.1，追平通义千问 3.8 的 270 亿参数版本所处水平，并将性能前沿再提升 5.7 分。此外，相较单任务强化学习，该方法在策略更新所需计算量更少的情况下取得更好表现。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，Maco-RL 能有效缓解混合任务强化学习扩展过程中的稳定性、计算分配和持续扩展难题。混合任务强化学习因此展现出成为实用且计算高效的后训练方案的潜力，有望增强通用智能体能力。

Abstract: Mixed-task reinforcement learning (MixRL) directly trains a unified policy across domains to continually expand agent capabilities without training separate specialists or requiring an extra distillation stage. Despite its appeal, scaling MixRL poses significant challenges in achieving stable and efficient mixed-task learning, effectively allocating rollout compute, and supporting continual domain expansion. To address these challenges, we propose Maco-RL, an approach that effectively scales MixRL. Specifically, Maco-RL employs a quota-based scheduler for asynchronous rollouts across multiple task domains, thereby stabilizing training by balancing gradient contributions and mitigating off-policy effects. Along an orthogonal axis, Maco-RL further introduces Tri-Buffer Sampling (TBS) to allocate compute among samples with varying levels of mastery, promoting sustained frontier exploration while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on five benchmarks show Maco-RL raises Qwen3.5-27B's overall score from 43.6 to 54.2 and then to 60.1 with continual expansion, reaching the frontier held by Qwen3.8-27B, which is further pushed outward by +5.7. Maco-RL also outperforms single-task RL with less compute for policy updates. These results support MixRL as a practical and compute-efficient post-training approach with substantial potential to strengthen general agent capabilities.

</details>


### [205] [Spec2Weights: Certifying Networks that a Language Model Writes from a Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34711)
*Soumik Bhattacharjee,Musa Tur Farazi,Md Nasir Uddin,Tishna Sabrina,Chowdhury Mohammad Rakin Haider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套证明小型神经网络在实际单精度浮点运算中逐输入精确性的工具，并验证了多种网络；同时分析了仅凭训练样例学习某些目标函数的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能够依据自然语言规格直接写出小型神经网络的权重，无须数据训练或梯度更新，但这些网络是否会在真实的单精度浮点运算中，对指定输入域完全正确，尚缺少严格验证。论文也希望厘清仅从样例学习时，哪些目标函数会受到信息缺失或梯度信号不足的限制。

Method: 作者建立浮点安全引理，使对单精度浮点数的穷举验证能够覆盖任意求和顺序；对于权重并非有限位二进制小数的网络，则使用分层误差界。验证过程中结合动态规划、模板检查、可满足性求解器证明、广度优先搜索，以及针对权重共享递推的归纳证明。此外，论文分析两类样例学习限制：训练中从未激活的输入坐标无法仅靠更多域内数据学得；类卢恩校验和目标在其函数族上的目标相关梯度方差会随规模呈指数级缩小。作者还用相同架构上的调优梯度下降结果进行对照。

Result: 在十五个由语言模型编写的网络中，十二个获得了对规定输入域逐输入精确的证明，其中一个在修正单个常数后通过。输入域规模最高可达一千万亿个输入，也包括有大小上限的图或网格；每个证明耗时不超过一分钟中央处理器时间。证明还能指出精确性的边界：最短路径网络仅在距离不超过四千零九十六时精确，并在预测的跳数处失效。另有三十份由同一模型系列的新会话撰写的规格，首轮提交中二十五个被证明对任意求和顺序均精确；五个税率表网络在一次固定的单精度运行中，对所有整数美元金额的误差不超过零点一一美元。一个二百七十亿参数的开放模型通过其中二十三项，在增加思考和代码执行后通过二十五项。梯度下降对照支持两类学习局限，这两类局限涵盖作者归纳的五种失败类别中的两种。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对小型神经网络进行穷举与形式化验证，可以为其实际浮点实现提供严格、成本较低的正确性保证，并揭示精确性失效的具体边界。这类方法也部分适用于不同规模的语言模型生成结果。与此同时，某些目标函数存在无法靠增加域内样例消除的信息或梯度限制；因此，直接生成并验证网络与依赖样例训练并非总能互相替代。

Abstract: A language model can write the weights of a small MLP, CNN, RNN or GNN from a plain-language specification, with no data and no gradient step. We ask when such a network can be proved exact in the float32 arithmetic it actually runs in. A float-safety lemma makes exhaustive float32 enumeration a proof for any summation order, and a layered error bound covers networks whose weights are not short binary fractions. With dynamic programming, template checks, an SMT proof, breadth-first search and induction for weight-tied recurrences, this certifies 12 of 15 written networks (one after a single constant fix) on every input of a stated domain (up to $10^{16}$ inputs, or every graph or grid up to a size bound), in a minute of CPU or less each. The certificates also say where a network stops being exact: the shortest-path network is exact iff the distance is at most 4,096 and fails at the predicted hops. Thirty new specifications were each written by a fresh session of one model family (Claude Opus 5.5). Twenty-five first submissions are certified exact for any summation order; the five tax schedules stay within \$0.11 of the true tax at every whole dollar in one fixed float32 run. An open 27B model passes 23 of the 30, and 25 with more thinking and code execution, so the method transfers in part. We also prove two limits of learning from examples: input coordinates never active in training cannot be learned from more in-range data, and Luhn-type checksums have exponentially small target-dependent gradient variance across their family. Tuned gradient descent on the same architectures agrees with both, which cover two of our five failure classes.

</details>


### [206] [Privileged On-Policy Distillation for Calibrated Alzheimer's Diagnosis With Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34721)
*Vo Minh Khoi Nguyen,Dat Tien Chung,Quang Minh Khoa Le,Duc Khang Nguyen,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Hoang Thien Nguyen,Minh Huu Nhat Le,Phat K. Huynh,Cuong Tuan Nguyen,Thanh Huy Nguyen,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种针对检查模态缺失的阿尔茨海默病诊断方法：模型逐项补全缺失信息，并由训练阶段可访问额外信息的教师评估补全结果，从而提升诊断表现与概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病诊断依赖临床、生物样本和影像等互补信息，但患者往往未完成所有检查。缺失模态存在多种合理取值；现有方法通常直接忽略缺失项，或只用单一估计训练，既未让分类器接触补全结果的多样性，也未依据诊断结果检验补全是否合理。

Method: 提出特权教师引导的分级滚动插补方法。模型按一次一个模态的顺序生成多个补全方案；了解诊断结果的插补教师逐步评价补全，使诊断结局参与插补学习；可查看全部真实模态的诊断教师则提供软目标。模型在多样化补全结果上训练，学习反映缺失检查不确定性的置信度，推理时仅需进行一次滚动补全并完成诊断。

Result: 在阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议队列上，该方法优于当前先进基线。在两个外部队列上，其判别能力仍具竞争力；所有方法经重新校准后，该方法的校准误差均与两队列中最低水平相当。将教师机制加入三种基线后，三者表现也均有所改善。

Conclusion: 该方法能够适应患者实际接受的不同检查组合，并利用多种可能的缺失信息补全来学习诊断不确定性。研究结果表明，它有望提供兼具竞争性判别能力与良好校准表现的单一诊断模型。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is diagnosed from clinical, biospecimen and imaging evidence, but few patients undergo every test. Because these modalities are complementary, a missing one has many plausible values. Most existing methods leave it out or train on one estimate, so their classifier never sees its other values, and the estimate is never checked against the diagnosis. We address both gaps with on-policy distillation: the model fills in the missing modalities itself, and teachers with information available only in training grade these fills. Our method, Graded Rollout Imputation with Privileged teachers, draws several fills, one modality at a time. An imputation teacher that knows the diagnosis grades each step, bringing the outcome into the imputation as statistical practice recommends. A diagnosis teacher that sees all real modalities supplies a soft target. Trained on fills that vary, the model learns a confidence that reflects what the missing tests leave open, and it diagnoses from a single rollout. On the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On two external cohorts, it remains competitive in discrimination and, even after every method is recalibrated, its calibration error is level with the lowest on both. Added to three of these baselines, the teachers improve each of them. Our method offers a single, well-calibrated model for whatever combination of tests a patient has received.

</details>


### [207] [Robust Recourse under Causal Graph Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34723)
*Muyan Anna Li,Juliette Sinnott,Mohammad Kohandel,Amir-Hossein Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究因果图存在歧义时如何提供可靠的算法补救建议，提出要求所有候选图均有效或仅满足指定图权重比例的两种方法，并展示放宽稳健性可提高建议覆盖率，但可能降低实际成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果算法补救方法通常假设系统由单一因果图描述，主要考虑因果机制本身的不确定性。然而，同一数据往往兼容多个因果图，而不同图对同一行动的效果预测可能不同。因此，基于单一图推荐行动可能不可靠；但若要求行动在所有可能图下都成功，随着候选图增加，找到可行行动也可能变得困难。

Method: 论文提出两种面向因果图歧义的补救方法：最坏情形补救要求行动在全部候选因果图下成功；图加权补救则要求行动在达到指定权重比例的候选图下成功。研究者为每个候选图拟合高斯过程结构方程，并依据后验均值与标准差构成的判据评估成功可能性，再通过基于梯度的搜索寻找成本最低的行动。最后，使用新的后验样本检验行动是否有效。

Result: 在合成与半合成基准上，放宽“所有候选图下均成功”的稳健性要求，能够增加获得有效补救建议的个体数量，并降低行动成本。以包含二十个节点的高斯过程基准为例，放宽要求后，覆盖率从一千个个体中的三百四十一人升至四百三十六人；但在生成模型下真正成功、即确实能够改变决策的行动数量，则从二百五十一项降至一百八十四项，体现了覆盖率与有效性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 当系统的因果机制尚未完全确定时，将多个合理因果图纳入补救决策，有助于避免依赖单一模型，并让决策者在稳健性与行动成本、建议覆盖率之间进行权衡。放宽对所有图都有效的要求可以让更多人获得建议，但也可能增加实际无法改变决策的风险。

Abstract: Causal algorithmic recourse recommends actions to reverse unfavorable decisions by modeling how interventions change an individual's features. Existing methods typically assume one causal graph that describes the system and account for uncertainty in the causal mechanisms. However, there usually exist several graphs that fit the same data but can predict different effects for the same action. Certain actions may achieve recourse across multiple graphs, but as the set of potential graphs grows, a single best action may become infeasible.
