  We propose Worst-Case Recourse and Graph-Weighted Recourse to find a low-cost action that succeeds across all candidate graphs or a specified fraction of graph weight, respectively. Assuming a set of plausible graphs, for each graph we fit Gaussian process structural equations and assess success using the posterior mean--standard-deviation criterion. We use gradient-based search to determine minimal cost actions and check the validity of the actions on fresh posterior samples. In synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks, relaxing robustness, the requirement that an action succeed under every candidate graph, can increase the number of valid recommendations for recourse and lower costs of actions for both methods. In the 20-node GP benchmark, relaxation increases the number of factual instances offered recourse (coverage) from 341 to 436 out of 1,000, while the number of true successes, actions that flip the decision under the generating model, falls from 251 to 184, illustrating the coverage--validity trade-off. Accounting for graph ambiguity can help decision-makers recommend actions that remain effective even when the causal mechanisms of the system are not fully known.

</details>


### [208] [Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34726)
*Ioannis Kalogeropoulos,Giorgos Bouritsas,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出分层图元网络，利用神经网络自身的层级结构处理模型参数，在保持置换对称性的同时降低计算成本，并在多种模型分析与编辑任务中展现出更高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间学习方法大多逐个处理输入模型的神经元和权重，计算成本随参数数量增长，因此难以扩展到大型网络。与此同时，神经网络具有层、模块等层级结构，许多重要性质也由不同层级的量组合而成；忽略这种结构会限制模型元网络的效率与表达能力。

Method: 提出分层图元网络，依据输入网络的架构层级，通过保持置换对称性的池化与反池化操作，对图进行逐级粗化和细化。针对变换器模型，论文还设计了兼容置换对称性并支持多层级池化的图构造方式。

Result: 实验覆盖残差网络、视觉变换器及参数规模最高达八千六百万的模型集合，并涉及利普希茨界近似、泛化与鲁棒性预测、后门检测和剪枝。与非等变方法及扁平图元网络相比，该方法样本效率明显更高；随着元网络深度增加，其计算成本基本保持稳定，使更深、更具表达力的处理成为可能，并在部分任务中提升准确率。

Conclusion: 将神经网络的层级结构纳入权重空间学习，有助于扩展图元网络处理大型模型的能力。该方法在降低深层处理的内存与计算压力的同时，保持任务表现，并在部分场景中取得更好的效果。

Abstract: The emerging field of weight space learning has demonstrated the potential to enable metanetworks, i.e. models that process neural network (NN) data, to support several steps of the machine learning lifecycle, from model optimisation to evaluation and adaptation. However, this potential has yet to materialise beyond small-scale MLPs or CNNs: most current models, including message-passing based graph metanetworks (GMNs), operate exclusively at the finest level of granularity, processing every neuron and weight of the input model. Their cost therefore scales with the number of parameters. Yet NNs are inherently organised hierarchically, with neurons forming layers and layers forming blocks and modules. Additionally, several of their properties, such as Lipschitz upper bounds and generalisation measures, are compositions of layer- and block-level quantities. To incorporate this inductive bias, we introduce Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks (H-GMNs), which leverage the input architecture's hierarchy to coarsen and refine the graph using permutation-symmetry-preserving pooling and un-pooling layers. Further, we extend H-GMNs to Transformers by introducing a graph construction that preserves permutation symmetries and enables multi-level pooling. Our evaluation includes new ResNet-18, ViT and varying ViT model zoos of up to 86M parameters, on tasks spanning model evaluation (an analytic Lipschitz bound proxy, generalisation and robustness prediction), testing (backdoor detection) and editing (pruning). Experimental results show that, compared to non-equivariant alternatives and flat GMNs, H-GMNs are markedly more sample-efficient, and their cost remains nearly constant as metanetwork depth grows, allowing deeper and more expressive processing where flat GMNs run out of memory, without compromising accuracy, which in certain cases considerably improves.

</details>


### [209] [From NP-Hardness to Exact Tractability Through Sample Abundance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34734)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文表明，在不改变目标函数和可行集的前提下，少量来自同一数据源的额外观测，就能使原本最坏情形下属于NP困难的噪声奇偶拟合问题实现精确优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声奇偶拟合通常难以精确求解。论文探讨能否利用关于同一生成源的额外观测，在不修改优化目标或可行解集合的情况下提升求解能力，并刻画所需观测量及其理论极限。

Method: 研究对象是均匀抽样的秩一符号张量观测：各因子符号独立且均匀取正负，观测误差彼此独立。对于固定交互阶数至少为三、每个分块含有个符号、平均度为对数级且信道偏置已知的情形，利用同一数据源的额外读数辅助精确优化；同时分析带独立读数标记的浅层局部估计器、共同有符号场分布，以及剩余坐标的几何结构。

Result: 以高概率增加至多数量级为“每块符号数除以对数项的相应幂次”的额外读数，即可在最坏情况下用时为每块符号数乘对数平方的时间完成精确优化。对于深度为对数对数级、且读数标记相互独立的局部估计器，实现弱恢复所需额外读数的首项系数是尖锐的。共同有符号场分布还刻画了相较生成源本身的性能增益、并列最优解的数量，以及后续重加权所需的维数；剩余坐标的几何结构则决定了高分配赋值可被局部验证的尖锐半径。

Conclusion: 额外观测不仅能改善恢复效果，也可能让原本困难的精确优化变得可行，而无需改变问题本身。论文进一步给出了观测需求的尖锐界，并用统一的概率与几何刻画解释性能增益、最优解并列现象及局部验证能力。

Abstract: Additional observations can make exact optimization tractable without changing the objective or feasible set. We establish this phenomenon for noisy parity fitting, a worst-case NP-hard problem, with uniformly sampled rank-one sign tensor entries, independent fair factor signs and independent errors. For fixed interaction order $k\ge3$, $n$ signs per block, logarithmic mean degree and known channel biases, $O(n/(\log n)^{1/(k-2)})$ further readings of the same source suffice with high probability for exact optimization in $O(n\log^2 n)$ worst-case work. The leading coefficient is sharp for weak recovery by depth-$O(\log\log n)$ local estimators with independent reading marks. A common signed-field law determines the gain over the generating source, the number of tied optima and the dimension needed for later reweightings. The geometry of the remaining coordinates determines the sharp radius of local verification for high-scoring assignments with high probability.

</details>


### [210] [BRIAR: Martingale Belief Smoothing on Bifurcation-Guided Reasoning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34746)
*Tianyi Yang,Haoran Zhang,Yifan Chen,Zixuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种依据推理过程中的概率鞅结构来分配采样与梯度信用的方法，旨在减少推理早期错误、答案确定后的冗余生成，并提升树状强化学习训练的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带可验证奖励的强化学习通常不区分推理轨迹中各位置的价值，因而可能在早期已经偏离正确路径后仍继续消耗计算，也可能在答案已确定后继续生成冗长的验证内容。论文指出，这两类问题都与前缀通过验证的概率有关：该概率沿推理轨迹构成取值在零至一之间的道布鞅，其可学习信用的方差受该概率与其补数之积限制，因此在失败或答案已确定的边界附近趋于零。

Method: 论文提出面向自适应推理树的强化学习框架。首先，沿着具有较高上置信度、且可能产生结果分化的节点扩展与推理话语对齐的树，优先探索尚未解决的决策点，避免在答案已确定的分支上反复生成。其次，以边界缩放的异方差边缘方差描述潜在节点价值，并通过两遍树上平滑算法在线性时间内计算全局后验，避免高阶矩阵求逆并抑制边界附近的虚假噪声。最后，识别最小的答案已确定前沿节点，仅对其后的生成后缀施加零和、按长度居中的优势信号，同时保证确定答案之前的推导不受影响。

Result: 摘要提出了该框架的理论依据与算法性质：信用方差具有边界约束；全局后验计算复杂度为树节点数的线性阶；后缀优势仅作用于答案确定之后，并保持此前推导不变。摘要未提供具体实验数据或与基线方法的定量比较，因此无法据此判断实际性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 论文将推理轨迹中的信用分配、树扩展和答案确定后的冗余控制统一到前缀通过概率的鞅几何结构中。其核心主张是：把计算资源集中于仍有结果不确定性的节点，并将训练信号限制在答案确定后的冗余部分，从而更有针对性地训练长链推理。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has rapidly advanced long-chain reasoning, yet standard policy optimizers distribute rollout compute and gradient credit blindly across a trajectory. On hard problems, an early misstep derails the remaining trace, whereas on solvable problems, the policy keeps generating redundant verification long after the answer is already determined. We trace both pathologies to a single structural fact: under an on-policy verifier, the true prefix pass probability $V^π(u)$ along a reasoning trace forms a $[0,1]$-bounded Doob martingale whose learnable credit variance is strictly conserved and bounded above by $V^π(u)(1-V^π(u))$, vanishing at both the derailed ($V^π\to 0$) and absorbed ($V^π\to 1$) boundaries. We introduce BRIAR (Bifurcation-guided Rollout and Inference on Adaptive Reasoning trees), a tree-structured RLVR framework built directly around this martingale geometry. First, BRIAR expands discourse-aligned reasoning trees along an upper-confidence outcome-bifurcation frontier, steering rollouts away from absorbed rumination and toward unresolved decision points. Second, it models latent node values with boundary-scaled heteroscedastic edge variances and computes exact global posteriors in linear $\mathcal{O}(|V|)$ time via two-pass Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) tree belief smoothing, eliminating $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ matrix inversions while gating out spurious boundary noise. Third, BRIAR detects minimal absorbed frontier nodes and applies zero-sum log-length centered suffix advantages strictly post-absorption, leaving pre-absorption derivations provably invariant.

</details>


### [211] [When to Trust a PFN: Detecting Harmful Shift in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34767)
*Viet Nguyen,Herman Bergström,Stephan Rabanser,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量、无监督的部署监测方法，通过微调后的锚点损失识别可能导致先验拟合网络失效的有害分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先验拟合网络在多种表格分类任务中表现良好，但部署时遇到分布偏移可能悄然失效。现有监测需要在测试批次上获取标签，或容易因无害偏移频繁误报，因此需要一种无需测试标签且能区分风险偏移与无害偏移的方法。

Method: 提出校准锚点损失分歧方法。该方法微调解码器，使其与测试集预测产生更大分歧，同时维持模型在同分布锚点集上的准确性；随后利用微调后在锚点集上产生的损失监测测试批次，无需测试批次标签即可触发预警。论文还证明，在偏移足够严重时，该锚点损失的期望严格低于同分布评估时的期望，从而构成单侧检验。

Result: 在涵盖表格数据及结构化分布偏移的14个数据集上，并在两种先验拟合网络架构中进行评估。该方法在有害偏移检测方面达到或超过基线方法，同时对无害偏移引发的误报表现出明显更强的抵抗力。

Conclusion: 校准锚点损失分歧为先验拟合网络部署提供了一种轻量、无监督且无需测试标签的风险监测方案，能够在检测有害偏移的同时减少无害偏移造成的误报。

Abstract: Tabular prior-fitted networks (PFNs) work well across tabular classification problems but can fail silently at deployment when distributions shift. We propose CALD (Calibrated Anchor-Loss Disagreement), a lightweight unsupervised monitor that triggers an alarm to warn when a PFN might fail. By finetuning the decoder to maximize disagreement with the test-set prediction while maintaining accuracy on an ID anchor set, CALD identifies harmful OOD batches through the resulting anchor loss, requiring no labels on test batches. We prove that the post-finetune anchor loss has a strictly lower expectation under sufficiently severe shift than under ID eval, yielding a one-sided test. Across 14 datasets covering tabular and structured distribution shift, on both TabPFN and TabICL, CALD matches or outperforms baselines on harmful shifts and is markedly more resilient to benign-shift false alarms.

</details>


### [212] [Adaptive Reliability Reserves for Residential Battery Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34779)
*Maruthi Vemula,Neeraj Praneeth Gajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布住宅电池调度基准与控制器，评估市场收益和停电备用之间的权衡。休斯敦二〇二四年回放中，按县级风险设定备用阈值，相较固定保留百分之二十电量，模拟的五小时备用覆盖率由百分之七十五升至百分之八十六点五，年化收益每户增加十点二美元；但覆盖率提升的配对区间包含零，结果属于条件模拟，并非实测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅电池参与电力市场放电可带来收益，却会消耗家庭停电时所需的电量。现有评估若只看市场价值或备用能力，可能忽视两者间的冲突。论文旨在提供可复现的评估基础，同时考察控制策略在不同时间、地区、车队规模及风暴情境下的表现。

Method: 作者发布住宅电池车队基准，结合实测电价、县级停电记录、模拟家庭以及经过识别的因果控制器输入。研究比较独立家庭优化与二十一次近端策略优化训练，并评估十种成本权衡候选策略；另测试基于预警的充电覆盖策略、合成故障情境、静态交流潮流约束及电池老化影响。预警测试包含预警过短、漏报、误报、负荷上升、容量衰减和设备退出等情况。

Result: 休斯敦二〇二四年回放显示，县级风险阈值策略的五小时备用覆盖率为百分之八十六点五，固定保留百分之二十电量的策略为百分之七十五；模拟年化收益每户增加十点二美元，配对百分之九十五区间为增加一点七至二十一点七美元。不过，覆盖率增益的区间包含零。十种候选策略的覆盖率介于百分之三十一点一至百分之九十六点三，其中十种的模拟收益低于固定策略，三种的覆盖率也更低。四十一个县级大范围停电事件中，仅百分之十九点五提供了足够连续的预警时间，使所有电池预设方案均能满足能量与功率条件。静态交流审计保留了既有馈线违规，老化比较则显示结果对模型设定敏感。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，调整备用电量策略可能改善住宅电池的停电覆盖与市场收益，但收益和可靠性并非必然同步提升，且关键结果受预警条件、系统约束与老化模型影响。论文提供的基准、控制器和训练检查点有助于复现并比较这些权衡；模拟结果不应被解读为企业实际可靠性或已验证的普遍效果。

Abstract: In a Houston 2024 replay, replacing a fixed 20 percent battery reserve with a county-risk threshold raises observed five-hour backup coverage from 75.0 percent to 86.5 percent and adds USD 10.2 in modeled annualized margin per home (paired 95 percent interval: USD +1.7 to USD +21.7). The paired coverage-gain interval includes zero. This is a conditional simulation result, not measured company reliability. Residential batteries face this tradeoff because market discharge consumes energy also needed for household outages. We release HomeFleet-ERCOT, combining observed prices and county outages with identified simulated homes and causal controller inputs. We compare independent-home optimization with 21 proximal policy optimization (PPO) training runs; ten promise-cost candidates span 31.1 percent-96.3 percent backup coverage. Of these, 10 have lower modeled margin than fixed 20 percent, and 3 also have lower coverage. Chronological, geographic, fleet, and storm tests examine transfer. An alert charging override has a conditional energy-and-power sufficiency guarantee, but only 19.5 percent of 41 county mass-outage episodes provide enough continuous warning for every battery preset. Synthetic tests include short, missed, and false warnings, load uplift, capacity loss, and dropout. A static AC audit retains existing feeder violations; aging comparisons expose model sensitivity. The released benchmark, controllers, and checkpoints provide a reproducible basis for comparing these tradeoffs. This work supports the design of residential battery programs that assess market value and household backup service together.

</details>


### [213] [Split-RL: Local Conflict Resolution in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34799)
*Benjamin Fuhrer,Chen Tessler,Gal Dalal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种利用指导标签引导树结构划分的强化学习方法，将局部冲突的学习信号分配到不同状态区域，从而提升受约束及离线强化学习表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习常需同时优化多个目标，而这些目标可能只在特定时空区域内相互冲突，例如开阔区域中的速度与狭窄空间中的精度。神经网络参数通常由不同来源的反馈共同更新，容易混合冲突信号；常规树模型虽然能划分状态空间，却会在建树前聚合梯度，因而也可能丢失各目标的独立信息。

Method: 提出分裂强化学习方法，采用梯度提升树，并将指导标签融入树的构建过程。标签可来自启发式规则、传感器事件或已有规则系统，用于把不同目标产生的更新路由到互不重叠的状态区域，减少信号干扰。论文还从理论上分析了提前聚合梯度造成的信息损失，并刻画该方法何时能够隔离冲突更新；实验重点考察受约束强化学习和混合数据集上的离线强化学习。

Result: 在涉及空间、稀有事件和时间冲突的多个领域中，该方法相较现有方法表现更稳定，能够满足每回合成本预算，同时保持有竞争力的奖励。在离线场景中，它能够按状态区域区分模仿与策略改进之间的权衡，优于其他方法，并在不同数据集规模下达到接近专家的表现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，借助指导标签让树结构在学习过程中显式隔离局部冲突目标，可以弥补常规梯度聚合带来的信息损失。该方法为将规则知识融入可学习策略提供了途径，并在受约束和离线强化学习任务中展现出效果；其表现仍依赖可用指导标签及其对冲突区域的指示能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are often trained by mixing contradictory feedback that is localized in space and time. For example, a robot may prioritize speed in open areas versus precision in tight gaps. We introduce Split-RL, a Gradient Boosting Trees (GBT) algorithm that uses guidance labels to locally isolate these conflicting signals. Standard neural networks (NNs) lack the structural mechanism to do this, as their parameters share feedback from all sources. While GBT provides the inductive bias to explicitly partition the state-space per objective, standard tree-fitting procedures aggregate contradictory gradient signals before they reach the leaves. Split-RL leverages this bias to integrate guidance labels into tree construction, routing these updates into disjoint state-space regions and preventing signal interference. Such labels can arise from heuristics, sensor-derived events, or existing rule-based systems, allowing Split-RL to incorporate rule-derived structure while retaining a learned policy. We theoretically show how early gradient aggregation loses objective-specific information and characterize when the Split-RL score isolates conflicting updates. We focus on constrained RL and offline RL with mixed datasets, each balancing two objectives that conflict locally. Across domains with spatial, rare-event, and temporal conflicts, Split-RL consistently outperforms existing methods by meeting the episodic cost budget while maintaining competitive rewards. In offline settings, Split-RL localizes the tradeoff between imitation and policy improvement, outperforming other methods and achieving near-expert performance across varying dataset sizes.

</details>


### [214] [Barron Smoothness for Layers in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34805)
*G. Welper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将深度网络逼近误差分解为无限宽极限的选择与有限宽度离散化两个步骤，并揭示离散化与逐层巴伦范数、局部最优解与误差均匀分布原则之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的逼近误差如何随网络结构和宽度变化，缺少系统的界定方法。论文希望将这一问题拆解，从而分别分析理想的无限宽模型以及有限规模网络的近似代价。

Method: 先为每一层选择最优的无限宽极限，再研究如何将各层离散化为有限规模网络。作者证明，按期望衡量时，最优离散化问题可由逐层推广的巴伦范数等价刻画；同时分析优化景观，考察局部极小值的结构。

Result: 研究发现，局部极小值满足一种误差或资源在各层间均匀分配的原则，与自适应样条逼近和有限元逼近中的均匀分布思想相似。摘要未提供具体的数值实验或定量误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作为深度网络逼近误差提供了分层分析框架：无限宽极限的选择与有限宽度离散化可分别处理，而逐层巴伦范数能够刻画后者；优化的局部结构则受到均匀分布原则约束。

Abstract: In order to bound approximation errors for deep neural networks, we split the problem into two steps: First the selection of optimal infinite width limits for each layer and second an optimal discretization of all layers to obtain finite sized networks. We show that in expectation, the latter problem is equivalently characterized by a layer wise generalization of Barron norms. Moreover, we provide an analysis of the optimization landscape and show that local minima are characterized by an equidistribution principle akin to similar ideas in adaptive spline and finite element approximation.

</details>


### [215] [Transferring Architecture as an Optimization Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34816)
*Vighnesh Subramaniam,Boris Katz,Brian Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出在正式训练前，让目标网络对齐随机初始化的引导网络的表示几何，从而改变目标网络的优化先验，并提升其下游表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初始化不仅决定权重尺度，也塑造输入样本之间的初始几何关系，并影响梯度下降优先优化哪些表示。因此，作者关注：能否借助不同架构的网络，在不改变目标网络物理结构的情况下，改变其优化倾向。

Method: 作者设计一种初始化流程：在下游训练开始前，使目标网络的表示几何与随机初始化的引导网络对齐。引导网络不传递已学习特征，不接收梯度，也不使用标签。研究考察了两类跨架构对齐：以深层网络引导浅层网络，以及以高分辨率表示的网络引导表示较粗糙的网络。

Result: 摘要报告，两类对齐均改善了目标网络的下游表现：浅层网络获得了深度带来的优化优势；表示较粗糙的网络则通过对齐获得了更细致的表示粒度。摘要未提供具体数据或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 模型的优化倾向并非完全由其实际架构决定。通过初始化阶段的跨架构几何对齐，可以显式调整网络的优化先验，使其部分摆脱物理结构所限定的“命运”。

Abstract: Initialization determines the optimization "fate" of a model, going far beyond just setting the scale of its weights. It establishes a fundamental initial geometry over the input data, dictating certain aspects such as which examples are considered close and which representations gradient descent will naturally refine first. In this paper, we view the initial geometry of a network as an optimization prior and ask whether a network can change its optimization prior by referencing a completely different architecture. To investigate this question, we use an initialization procedure where a target network is aligned to the representational geometry of a randomly initialized guide network before any downstream training occurs. Crucially, alignment to the guide network cannot transfer learned features, never receives gradients, and does not use labels. We demonstrate that this cross-architecture transfer of "fate" benefits the target network in two key domains: (1) instilling the optimization benefits of depth into shallow networks by aligning them to deeper networks, and (2) transferring representational granularity by using fine-resolution networks to shape the geometry of networks with coarser representations. In both cases, the alignment results in improved downstream performance of the target network. Ultimately, this demonstrates that a model's architectural destiny can be decoupled from its physical structure and explicitly modified at initialization.

</details>


### [216] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Multi-UAV Data Harvesting Under Variable Team Sizes and Agent Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34818)
*Thiago de Souza Lamenza,Bruno José Olivieri de Souza,Markus Endler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可适应无人机与传感器数量变化、并能应对任务中无人机永久损失的多智能体强化学习方法；实验显示其整体优于固定结构基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏远地区的无线传感器可能因通信基础设施不足而无法上传数据，无人机可通过机动采集数据，多机协同则能提升覆盖效率与冗余性。现有方法通常需针对不同团队规模分别训练策略，成本较高，而且固定规模策略容易受到无人机故障影响。

Method: 提出灵活型多智能体深度确定性策略梯度方法，使策略能够适用于不同数量的传感器和无人机，并允许任务期间有无人机发生灾难性故障、永久退出。研究将其与采用多层感知机的基线方法进行比较，同时评估团队规模采样方式及训练中是否模拟智能体退出的影响。

Result: 该方法能在实验评估的多种团队规模下学习，平均完成任务的速度快于基线。与均匀采样相比，按团队规模倒数进行采样效果更好。即使无人机永久损失，该方法仍能维持有效表现；训练时加入故障经历则没有带来稳定、持续的收益。

Conclusion: 灵活型方法可降低针对不同团队规模单独训练策略的需求，并增强无人机数据采集任务对团队规模变化及永久损失的适应能力。团队规模采样策略会影响训练效果，而故障训练是否有益则取决于具体情形，未显示出一致优势。

Abstract: Collecting data from wireless sensors can be challenging when limited communication infrastructure prevents them from offloading their measurements. In such settings, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can exploit their mobility to harvest data locally, while coordinated UAV teams can provide efficient and redundant coverage of remote areas. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables such teams to learn decentralized coordination policies. However, training separate policies for different team and sensor populations is costly, and fixed-population policies may be vulnerable to agent failures. This work proposes F-MADDPG, a flexible variant of Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) that supports data harvesting with varying numbers of wireless sensors and UAVs. The framework also accommodates unexpected catastrophic failures that permanently remove agents during a mission. We compare F-MADDPG with a MADDPG baseline that uses multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and evaluate training strategies based on team-size sampling and exposure to agent terminations. The results show that F-MADDPG learns across the evaluated population sizes, completes missions faster than the baseline on average, benefits from inverse-cardinality team-size sampling compared with uniform sampling, remains effective after permanent agent losses, and gains no consistent benefit from exposure to failures during training.

</details>


### [217] [LoopTitan: What Does Parameter Revisit Buy a Mixture-of-Experts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34821)
*Zichen Tian,Yudong Lu,Xuansheng Wu,Na Zou,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Qianru Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过严格对照实验指出，循环式混合专家模型的优势主要来自网络形状、优化器和额外计算，而非参数重访本身；据此提出更强的非循环基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究报告称，循环式混合专家模型在参数量、浮点运算量和键值缓存等预算相同的条件下优于标准模型。但这些提升可能同时来自网络形状、残差缩放等设计，无法说明参数重访本身的作用。论文旨在通过匹配预算的非重访对照，分离并评估参数重访的独立贡献。

Method: 研究分两步进行。首先，在预算匹配条件下逐项拆解先进循环模型方案，并将参数重访留作最后考察；通过非重访对照区分网络形状、残差缩放和重访的影响，同时分析优化器对各层更新幅度的作用。其次，在参数量固定时增加计算量，比较额外重访、增加专家数量，以及采用更深的标准混合专家模型所带来的收益。研究还提出循环泰坦基础模型作为非循环基线，并公开预训练模型、实验记录及预先登记的预测。

Result: 预算匹配实验中，移除参数重访后仍保留了循环方案的全部已报告增益；主要收益来自网络形状，其余主要来自残差缩放。论文证明，残差缩放相当于降低被重访层的学习率：它能缓解自适应矩估计与权重衰减优化器下各层更新幅度不均的问题，但在穆恩优化器使各层步长近乎一致时，这一效果消失。在固定参数量下增加计算时，额外重访优于将专家数量翻倍；然而，使用穆恩优化器的更深标准模型，在参数量与计算量相同的条件下，能取得该收益的百分之九十点二。循环泰坦基础模型在穆恩优化器下无需重访或残差缩放，即可匹配最强循环方案。

Conclusion: 参数重访本身几乎没有独立收益；它提供的主要是一种在固定参数量下以深度换取专家数量的选择，而标准混合专家模型也能通过调整网络结构实现类似权衡。循环模型的优势更应归因于网络形状、优化器选择和额外计算。因此，评估循环架构时需要使用充分匹配的非循环基线，并将循环泰坦基础模型作为更有力的比较对象。

Abstract: Looped Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures revisit layer parameters across depths. This design increases effective depth without adding parameters. Recent looped MoE studies report substantial gains over the standard MoE at matched budgets (parameters, FLOPs and KV cache). However, the reported gains still confound parameter revisit with other design choices, e.g., network shape and residual scale. We ask, what does parameter revisit itself buy? Using matched non-revisit controls, we find that parameter revisit itself buys almost nothing. We argue that revisit only offers a freedom an MoE already has, i.e., trading experts for depth (more layers, fewer experts each) at fixed parameters. First, at matched budgets, we decompose the state-of-the-art looped recipe, with revisit last. A non-revisit control retains all the gain. Most comes from network shape, and the rest from residual scale. We prove this scale acts as a lower learning rate on revisited layers. This helps under AdamW's uneven layer step sizes, but disappears under Muon, which keeps them nearly equal. Second, beyond matched budgets, we evaluate revisit by adding compute at fixed parameters. One extra visit gains more than doubling the experts. Yet without revisit, a deeper standard MoE at equal parameters and compute obtains 90.2% of that gain under Muon. Overall, gains come from network shape, the optimizer and added compute, which a standard MoE can adopt. Accordingly, we propose LoopTitan-Base as a stronger MoE baseline. Under Muon, it matches the strongest looped recipe without revisit or residual scale. We open-source LoopTitan and release 17 pretrained models (including LoopTitan-Base and the control). Our experiment record is public as GitHub issues, with predictions pre-registered and the full exploration history kept. This is our Phase 1 technical report, laying the foundation for follow-up studies.

</details>


### [218] [Routeability Before Routing: Routeability Audit Protocol (RAP) and RouteabilityBench for Audited LLM Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34825)
*Yihang Lu,Denica Kjorvezir,Ana Gjorgjevikj,Carola Doerr,Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出先审计基准是否适合路由，再解读路由器效果；实验发现，通用路由改进并不能稳定胜过单一最佳模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型路由只有在不同模型能针对不同题目各有优势时才有意义。但在粗粒度评分的基准上，多个模型经常并列最佳，单一最佳模型本身也可能已接近可达到的上限，使路由收益有限。因此，仅比较路由器表现不足以证明路由具有普遍价值。

Method: 作者提出以“可路由性优先”为核心的回顾性审计框架，并实现为可运行的主张验证工具包。审计在基准与模型组合的得分矩阵生成后、解读路由器比较结果之前进行，检查题目层面的模型优势差异及并列最佳情况。研究覆盖四个基准、六十六个大语言模型，并考察扩大嵌入特征集、降维、选择性弃答、聚类和更强回归器等路由增强方法。

Result: 各类通用路由增强方法并未稳定超过单一最佳模型。较明确的正向证据主要出现在依赖模型组合的数学基准设置中；其他基准则显示出不同的路由困难，或对预测器选择较为敏感。作者建议同时报告可路由性诊断、并列最佳分析、模型组合构建细节，以及经过模型家族校正的统计检验。

Conclusion: 路由效果不能脱离基准特性和模型组合来判断。应先确认基准是否存在足够的题目级模型优势差异，再评估路由器；只有结合可路由性诊断与稳健统计检验，路由收益的结论才具有可信度。

Abstract: LLM routing aims to choose, for each prompt, which model in a portfolio is most likely to answer correctly. This only makes sense when the benchmark contains enough prompt-specific variation for different models to be best on different inputs. On coarse-scored LLM benchmarks, however, many models often tie for the best score, and the single best model may already leave little room for improvement. We therefore propose a routeability-first audit, instantiated as RAP and RouteabilityBench, a runnable claim-verification package. The audit is retrospective: it runs once the benchmark-portfolio score matrix exists and before router comparisons are interpreted as general evidence, rather than certifying a portfolio that has not yet been evaluated. Across four benchmarks and 66 LLMs, generic routing enhancements such as larger embedding sets, dimensionality reduction, selective abstention, clustering, and stronger regressors do not consistently outperform the single best model. The clearest positive evidence is concentrated in portfolio-dependent Omni-MATH settings, while GPQA, MMLU-Pro, and IFEval show different ways in which routing can remain hard or predictor-sensitive. Our practical recommendation is that routing gains should be reported together with routeability diagnostics, tied-best analysis, portfolio construction details, and family-corrected statistical tests.

</details>


### [219] [Gradient TD($λ$) for Non-Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34826)
*Sujeet Lahane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将基于投影贝尔曼误差的梯度时序差分方法，从线性函数逼近扩展到非线性函数逼近，以支持更复杂的价值函数表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半梯度时序差分方法可能发散；基于均方贝尔曼误差或均方时序差分误差构造的算法则较难分析。梯度时序差分方法通过投影价值函数实现了收敛的离策略学习，但此前的拉姆达回报算法仅适用于线性函数逼近，难以适应复杂的非线性函数近似器。

Method: 将既有的拉姆达回报梯度时序差分思路推广至非线性函数逼近，并继续以投影贝尔曼误差作为优化目标。该方向不同于近期采用广义投影贝尔曼误差目标的相关方法。

Result: 摘要说明了方法的扩展方向与所采用的目标函数，但未提供具体算法细节、理论证明内容或实验结果，因此无法据此判断其性能提升幅度或适用范围。

Conclusion: 本文旨在使基于投影贝尔曼误差的梯度时序差分学习能够处理非线性价值函数近似，从而弥补拉姆达回报方法仅限线性近似的不足。

Abstract: Temporal difference (TD) learning is central to reinforcement learning. Many methods use semi-gradient TD, which may diverge, or develop algorithms with mean-squared Bellman or TD-error objectives that are difficult to analyze. Gradient TD algorithms overcome these challenges by using projection of the value function and present convergent off-policy algorithms. However, the algorithm for $λ$-return was limited to linear function approximation. Here, we extend the same to non-linear function approximation, which is desirable since many strong modern function approximators are complex and non-linear. Recent works have derived similar algorithms with a generalized projected Bellman error objective. Alternatively, we continue working with the projected Bellman error.

</details>


### [220] [Not all world models can doubt: how training objective and architecture shape epistemic uncertainty in JEPA and RSSM world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34830)
*Simon Baur,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文比较两类世界模型的认知不确定性估计，发现联合嵌入预测模型能更完整地保留环境变化信号；循环状态空间模型则可能在估计前削弱或忽略变化，进而影响下游决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型可在执行前预测行动后果，但只有在模型经验覆盖范围内，预测和据此作出的决策才可靠。认知不确定性用于标示模型经验的边界。论文关注训练目标与模型架构如何影响这种不确定性，以及不确定性估计能否准确反映模型实际未掌握的状态。

Method: 作者在十个模拟视觉控制环境中，开展受控的状态扰动实验，追踪扰动从环境状态经过模型各处理阶段直至最终不确定性估计的变化。比较对象是两类模型：一种直接预测下一时刻的潜在嵌入，不使用解码器；另一种通过循环潜在状态重建观测。两者均采用事后不确定性估计方法，包括末层拉普拉斯近似和模型集成，并进一步评估其在五项不确定性相关任务中的表现。

Result: 循环状态空间模型会通过两种机制削弱扰动：循环更新将单帧变化与上下文平均，使变化仅以较小幅度进入状态，并需持续一段时间才会恢复；重建目标按物体覆盖的像素数量赋权，因而可能忽略小物体。相比之下，联合嵌入预测模型没有这两种机制，能够将变化信号传递至不确定性估计。下游任务结果也显示，前者的不确定性可能漏报其自身后验状态已记录的偏差，而后者报告的内容更贴近模型实际接收到的变化。

Conclusion: 世界模型的不确定性不仅取决于所用估计器，也受架构和训练目标影响。若模型在表征阶段已经压低或忽略某些环境变化，事后不确定性估计难以弥补这一缺陷。因而，评估和设计可靠的不确定性机制时，应同时考察模型的信息保留能力及其下游决策效果。

Abstract: World models predict how an environment responds to actions, so that candidate actions can be evaluated before execution. Executed actions, or decisions, taken in this manner are only as reliable as the model's predictive capabilities, and predictions in turn are only reliable within the model's experience. Epistemic uncertainty (EU) is the signal that marks where that experience ends. In this work we offer an extensive empirical study on how training objectives and architectures shape epistemic uncertainty of world models. More precisely, we present a controlled comparison of post-hoc EU (last-layer Laplace and ensembles) between two dominant models of different paradigms: 1) LeWorldModel (LeWM): A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), which predicts the next embedding without a decoder, and 2) DreamerV3: A recurrent state-space model (RSSM), which reconstructs the observation from a recurrent latent state. In ten simulated visual-control environments, we follow a controlled state perturbation of the environment state through every stage of the models up to the final EU estimate. We find that the RSSM attenuates the state perturbation through two mechanisms before it reaches an estimator on the predictor feature. Its recurrent update averages over context, so a change in a single frame reaches the state at a fraction of its size and recovers only as the change persists. Its reconstruction objective weights an entity by the pixels it covers, so a small object can be lost due to the training objective. The JEPA has neither mechanism and passes the change through all stages to the uncertainty. We further show that this translates to gaps in five uncertainty related downstream tasks, from unfamiliar-state detection to uncertainty-penalised planning: the RSSM uncertainty misses deviations that its own posterior registers, whereas the JEPA uncertainty reports what its model sees.

</details>


### [221] [Provably Efficient Offline Discounted Reinforcement Learning under Recursive Entropic Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34840)
*Amirparsa Bahrami,Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究有限折扣无限时域马尔可夫决策过程中的递归熵风险强化学习，提出离线模型算法，并给出样本复杂度上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归熵风险度量可用于刻画风险厌恶与风险偏好决策，但离线强化学习在该框架下缺乏严格的样本复杂度保证，尤其缺少覆盖两种风险取向的理论分析。

Method: 针对非零风险参数β，分别考虑β大于零的风险厌恶情形与β小于零的风险偏好情形。提出基于模型的递归熵风险价值迭代下置信界算法，利用固定离线数据学习近最优策略，并分析其样本复杂度。

Result: 论文建立了该算法的概率近似正确型样本复杂度上界，并证明相应下界，表明上界中关于风险参数绝对值与折扣因子所产生的指数依赖，在最坏情况下无法避免。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为递归熵风险度量下、同时涵盖风险厌恶与风险偏好情形的离线强化学习提供严格样本复杂度保证，并揭示了问题固有的最坏情况复杂度。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted infinite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with recursive entropic risk measures (ERM), parameterized by $β\neq 0$. We consider both risk-averse ($β>0$) and risk-seeking ($β<0$) settings. We consider the offline RL setting, where the agent is provided with a fixed dataset and wishes to learn a near-optimal policy using this dataset. We introduce a model-based algorithm, called ERM Value Iteration Lower Confidence Bound (ERM-VI-LCB) and derive a PAC-type bound on its sample complexity. We also establish a corresponding lower bound, demonstrating that exponential dependence on $|β|/(1-γ)$ asserted by the upper bound is unavoidable in the worst case. The presented bounds provide the first rigorous sample complexity guarantees for offline RL under recursive ERM across both risk-averse and risk-seeking regimes.

</details>


### [222] [Put the World Back: Timestamps Matter in Time Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34848)
*Tingrui Huang,Yuanyuan Deng,Qingren Yao,Joaquin Vanschoren,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Songgaojun Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，模型在时间序列任务中的高准确率，并不能证明其真正利用时间戳将数值观测与现实事件对应起来；它们可能依赖输入顺序或相关性捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测通常按时间顺序提供事件，并预先将事件与时间序列匹配，因此无法判断模型是否借助时间戳完成对齐，还是仅利用输入结构等捷径。论文旨在区分基于时间戳的真实推理与表面上有效的相关性推断。

Method: 论文从“现实世界向时间序列投影”的视角重新定义时间序列推理，将时间戳视为连接数值观测、外部事件与共同世界状态的显式索引，并考察其揭示的三类结构：时间因果结构、时间语义和事件—序列对齐。作者设计诊断方案，对两个任务中的三百一十道题进行受控干预，通过改用相对时间、移除时间戳、打乱事件顺序或破坏事件与序列的对齐，判断模型是否依赖时间戳进行推理。

Result: 实验发现，改用相对时间或移除时间戳后，模型仍可能保持较高准确率；但破坏事件与时间序列的对齐会导致系统性失误。上下文已预先对齐时，模型几乎不需要时间戳；事件顺序缺失时，模型会利用日期回忆世界知识；没有日期时，模型更倾向于使用相关性捷径，且很少察觉时间戳错误。

Conclusion: 在事件已按序排列且预先匹配的输入上取得高准确率，不足以证明模型能利用时间戳同步现实事件与数值观测。评测应通过受控干预检验模型对时间信息及事件—序列对齐的真实依赖，而不能仅依靠准确率衡量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to time series reasoning. However, current evaluations often give models events in chronological order and already matched to the time series. This makes it unclear whether models use timestamps to connect events with observations or simply take shortcuts. To expose this ambiguity, we reformulate time series reasoning from a world-to-series projection perspective, viewing timestamps as explicit indices that connect numerical observations and external events to shared world states. We identify three structures that timestamps can make explicit: temporal causal structure, time semantics, and event-series alignment. Building on this perspective, we develop a diagnostic protocol that distinguishes timestamp-grounded reasoning from correlational shortcuts, a distinction that accuracy-based benchmarks cannot make. Controlled interventions on 310 questions from two tasks show that high accuracy can persist when timestamps are replaced by relative offsets or removed, while breaking event-series alignment induces systematic failures. When context is pre-aligned, models barely need timestamps. When order is missing, they use dates to recall world knowledge. Without dates, they rely on correlational shortcuts and rarely notice incorrect timestamps. These findings show that high accuracy on pre-aligned, ordered inputs does not establish that a model uses timestamps to synchronize observations with the world. We release our code, model outputs, and trajectory labels at https://github.com/tingrui-huang/timestamps-matter.

</details>


### [223] [From Prediction to Decision: Decision-Focused Fine-Tuning of Autoregressive Tabular Transformers for Cost-Sensitive Financial Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34858)
*Ashkan Golgoon,Giacomo Domeniconi,Samuel Atkins,Sergio Servantez,Zain Khandwala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向金融事件决策的微调方法：直接优化模型在机构成本规则下的预期效用，而非仅依赖事后概率阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构需要依据决策及其后果采取行动，而不是单纯预测事件概率。漏掉高金额欺诈与误拒小额交易的代价可能截然不同，且随交易金额变化。传统做法是在模型训练后，基于校准概率和成本矩阵设定贝叶斯最优阈值；论文探讨能否通过直接针对决策目标微调模型，获得更好的决策表现。

Method: 研究以预训练的自回归模型为基础，对其标签词元上的受限概率分布进行微调，使其在机构指定、随金额缩放的成本矩阵下最大化预期效用。由于奖励由决策、真实结果和金额确定，且真实结果可从数据中观测，目标函数及其梯度可直接计算；每个样本的成本敏感损失对预测概率呈线性，不需要抽样、评论家模型或优势估计。方法还加入相对于预训练模型的散度约束，以及辅助语言模型训练项，以保留概率校准能力和事件生成能力；另提出一种按组归一化的抽样变体，用于扩展到轨迹级奖励。

Result: 摘要未提供定量实验结果或与事后阈值方法的具体比较，因此无法判断该方法在实际数据上的提升幅度。摘要明确说明了其核心优化机制及可扩展方向。

Conclusion: 该方法将金融事件预测从概率拟合进一步转向成本感知的行动优化，并利用已知成本规则和观测结果直接计算效用梯度。其设计避免了复杂的策略梯度估计，同时通过约束项尽量维持原模型的校准与生成能力；实际有效性仍需由实验结果验证。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers trained on tokenized tabular event streams are a simple and strong baseline for predicting financial events such as fraud. Yet institutions do not act on probabilities; they act on decisions whose consequences are asymmetric and heteroscedastic: the cost of a missed high-value fraud and that of a wrongly declined small purchase are not the same. The standard bridge is post-hoc Bayes-optimal thresholding of calibrated probabilities under a cost matrix. We ask whether fine-tuning the model to the decision objective can do better. We fine-tune the same pretrained model so that its restricted softmax over label tokens (already a distribution over business actions) maximizes expected utility under an institution-specified, amount-scaled cost matrix. Because the reward is a known function of (action, outcome, amount) and the outcome is observed in the data, the expected-utility objective and its gradient are available in closed form. This is example-dependent cost-sensitive learning whose per-example loss is linear in the predicted probability, with no sampling, critics, or advantage estimation. A KL anchor to the pretrained model and an auxiliary language-modeling term preserve calibration and the generative event model. A sampled, group-normalized (GRPO-style) variant provides the extension point to trajectory-level rewards.

</details>


### [224] [Interpretable and Accurate Hybrid Decision Trees with Selective Case-Based Splits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34887)
*Cristiano Landi,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可在特征划分与案例划分之间灵活选择的贪心决策树，并通过选择性案例划分提升预测效果，同时保持树结构浅而易解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树划分方式多样，包括基于特征值、特征组合或相似案例的划分，但没有一种方法在所有任务中都占优。单一划分策略难以兼顾预测性能与模型简洁性，因此需要能结合不同策略的树结构。

Method: 提出两项改进：一是构建混合决策树，使不同节点可分别采用基于特征或基于案例的划分；二是设计选择性案例划分，在需要分裂时寻找最有信息量的特征子集来确定划分，以兼顾案例方法的针对性与特征方法的简洁性。

Result: 在多种表格数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于标准决策树，性能可与集成方法相当；同时生成的树较浅，并能保持甚至提升预测准确性。

Conclusion: 混合划分与选择性案例划分为贪心决策树提供了更灵活的建树方式。实验结果显示，这种方法有望在维持模型可解释性的同时获得具有竞争力的预测性能。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most well-known interpretable models in Machine Learning. Over the years, a wide variety of strategies have been proposed to define split conditions within the greedy learning framework, aiming to guide instance routing and enhance predictive performance. These strategies range from axis-parallel and oblique splits based on feature values to proximity-based splits using exemplar instances and more complex hybrid approaches. However, no single method has emerged as universally superior. In this work, we present two key contributions to the construction of greedy decision trees. First, we propose a hybrid tree architecture that integrates both feature-based and case-based splits, allowing for heterogeneous splits throughout the tree. Second, we introduce a novel type of split that leverages a selective case-based approach: when a split is needed, our method searches for the most informative subset of features to define the split, effectively balancing the specificity of case-based methods with the simplicity of feature-based ones. Experiments on a diverse set of tabular datasets show that our method outperforms standard decision trees and achieves performance on par with ensemble methods, while producing shallow trees that retain or even improve predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [225] [A gene informed benchmark for BioScoring of pathology foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34930)
*Paul Vilars,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Maria Vakalopoulou,Dimitris Samaras,Stergios Christodoulidis,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“生物评分”基准，用同一组织的空间转录组数据衡量图像编码器是否捕捉到细胞身份；评测显示，病理基础模型整体优于自然图像模型，且可分析模型对不同细胞身份的表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型通常依据分类、检索等下游任务进行比较，这类评测能反映模型的实用性，却难以说明模型从图像中学到了哪些生物学信息。本文希望直接评估图像编码器是否表征了组织中的细胞身份。

Method: 作者提出“生物评分”基准，以同一组织的空间转录组数据作为参照。首先，使用无监督稀疏自编码器将图像编码器生成的图像块嵌入拆分为稀疏特征，并将这些特征聚类为固定数量的组。随后，针对由标记基因定义的五十二种细胞身份，评估各组在组织空间中的分布与相应基因表达的特异性对应程度。由于自编码器训练不使用细胞身份标签，若某组与某种细胞身份对应，说明模型可能仅凭图像捕捉到了该身份。

Result: 作者评测了八种病理基础模型和三种自然图像模型。所有病理模型的得分均高于所有自然图像模型：前者为零点二九九至零点三七一，后者为零点二一四至零点二三二。配对检验进一步将病理模型区分为三个层级。此外，该基准无需标签，可利用待测数据构建参照，并能在仅有少量组织切片时评分；其模型排序与监督式探针一致，还能按细胞身份揭示各编码器的优势与不足。

Conclusion: “生物评分”为病理图像模型提供了面向生物学表征的评测方式，补充了传统下游任务基准。结果表明，病理模型比自然图像模型更能表征细胞身份；逐细胞身份的评分也有助于定位模型已捕捉或尚未捕捉的生物学特征。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are usually compared by their performance on downstream tasks such as classification and retrieval. While such benchmarks measure how useful a model is, they provide no insight into the biology it represents. We introduce BioScore, a benchmark that quantifies how well an image encoder captures cell identities, with spatial transcriptomics of the same tissue as the reference. A sparse autoencoder splits the encoder's patch embeddings into sparse features, which we cluster into a fixed number of groups. BioScore then measures, for each of 52 cell identities defined by marker genes, how specifically one group follows the identity's expression over the tissue. Because the autoencoder training is fully unsupervised, a match shows that the model represents the cell identity from the image alone. We score 8 pathology foundation models and 3 natural-image models. Every pathology model scores above every natural-image model (0.299--0.371 \vs 0.214--0.232), and paired tests separate three tiers among the pathology models. Unlike a supervised probe, BioScore needs no labels, builds its reference from the data and scores even from few sections, yet it ranks the encoders as a probe does. Its per-identity breakdown indicates which cell identities each encoder captures or misses.

</details>


### [226] [Attack Policy Selection can Mislead Monitor Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34934)
*Charlie Griffin,Oliver Makins,Asa Cooper Stickland,Alessandro Abate,Yarin Gal,Abby D'Cruz,Xander Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，人工智能监控器的漏报率会随攻击策略生成方法及其优化程度大幅变化；仅用简单攻击评估，可能会让人对监控器的可靠性产生虚假的信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控器通常通过判别攻击策略生成的对话记录来评估，但这些记录只是部署中危险行为的代理。若评估结果对代理生成方式高度敏感，就难以判断其能否反映实际部署表现，也可能因攻击方法过于简单而高估监控器能力。

Method: 作者以一款曾用于生产环境的监控器为案例，比较不同攻击策略生成方法及其优化程度对漏报率的影响，包括强化学习、优化提示和对话记录编辑。攻击策略仅针对代理监控器开发，最终在留出的目标监控器上测试，以考察攻击能否迁移。

Result: 同一种强化学习方法在不同优化程度下，漏报率从百分之零点五变至百分之二十点八；不同方法在各自观测到的最佳优化水平下，漏报率从百分之六点四变至百分之二十三。所有报告的攻击均成功迁移至留出的目标监控器。此外，代理记录可能包含监控器可见的异常线索，例如提及指令中未出现的“附带任务”，从而影响判断。

Conclusion: 监控器规避评估对攻击代理的选择十分敏感，单一或基础攻击方法不足以支撑对部署表现的可靠判断。要将评估结果外推到实际部署，必须论证所用代理具有信息价值，并说明其保守性；如何提出令人信服的论证仍是开放问题。

Abstract: AI monitors, classifiers that flag dangerous behaviour by AI agents, are often evaluated against transcripts generated by attack policies acting as proxies for dangerous behaviour in deployment. We evaluate an ex-production OpenAI monitor as a case study and find that measured false negative rate is highly sensitive to both the method used to generate attack policies and the level of optimisation applied within that method. This rate ranged from 0.5\% to 20.8\% across optimisation levels of a single method (reinforcement learning), and from 6.4\% to 23.0\% across methods at their best observed optimisation level (optimised prompting vs.\ transcript editing). Every attack policy was developed against proxy monitors only: all reported results show attacks transferring to the held-out GPT-5.4 target monitor. Our results suggest that monitor evasion evaluations can easily mislead: an evaluation using only basic attack methods may give false confidence that a monitor is strong.
  The sensitivity to attack-policy choice raises a problem: interpreting any particular result as evidence about deployment performance requires justification. The transcripts produced by attack policies are proxies for the dangerous behaviour a monitor must flag in deployment. These proxies can introduce visible artefacts, such as reasoning about a ``side task'' absent from the instructions shown to the monitor, that affect monitor judgements. Given the sensitivity above, the choice of proxy can change an evaluation's conclusion. Reading these results as evidence about deployment therefore requires a separate legitimacy argument for why the proxies are informative, including any justification of conservativeness. Making convincing arguments remains an open problem.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [CA-RAG: Constraint-Aligned Retrieval for Structurally Faithful Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33497)
*Vimal Kumar Kumar,Meghana Kshirsagar,Conor Ryan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向结构性要求的检索增强生成框架，通过在检索阶段纳入章节约束与政策关系，提升合规长文生成所需证据的充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成主要依据语义相关性筛选证据，可能漏掉满足后续强制条款所必需的信息。合规敏感的长篇生成不仅需要相关证据，也需要证据覆盖明确的结构性义务。

Method: 提出约束对齐检索增强生成框架。在选择证据前，将章节专属查询与本体推导出的结构约束、基于政策的知识图谱关系结合，以引导检索。研究通过人工标注的证据进行纯检索评估，并进一步开展生成实验。

Result: 在不同检索预算下，该方法的检索表现均有提升。检索数量为六时，精确率由0.5268升至0.5692，汇总召回率由0.5402升至0.5950，调和平均值由0.5389升至0.5672。若干模型与领域设置下，生成合规性和可靠性也有所改善；仅在生成阶段施加约束虽能提高条款覆盖率，但整体合规性和可靠性较低。

Conclusion: 将结构约束融入证据检索，有助于改善合规敏感长篇生成中的证据充分性，并可能提升生成结果的合规性与可靠性。研究结果表明，检索阶段的结构对齐具有重要价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically optimises evidence selection for semantic relevance, whereas compliance-sensitive long-form generation also requires evidence supporting explicit structural obligations. Semantically relevant retrieval may therefore omit information needed for mandatory downstream clauses. We propose Constraint-Aligned RAG (CA-RAG), a retrieval-conditioning framework that augments section-specific queries with ontology-derived structural constraints and policy-grounded knowledge-graph relations before evidence selection. Retrieval-only evaluation with manually annotated evidence shows consistent improvements across retrieval budgets. At $k=6$, CA-RAG increases Precision@$k$ from $0.5268$ to $0.5692$, pooled Recall@$k$ from $0.5402$ to $0.5950$, and F1@$k$ from $0.5389$ to $0.5672$ over standard RAG. Generation experiments further show improvements in compliance and reliability in several model--domain settings, while generation-only constraints achieve higher clause coverage but lower overall compliance and reliability. These results indicate that structurally aligned retrieval can improve evidence sufficiency in compliance-sensitive long-form generation.

</details>


### [228] [Agentic Waterfall: An Intent-Driven Information Discovery Framework for Reclaiming User Control in the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34407)
*Yige Luo,Shining Zhang,Jikun Liu,Hongjiao Qiang,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“智能体瀑布”个性化信息发现框架及配套数据集与评测方案；在新任务评测中，该方法较最强基线的表现最高提升百分之十六点一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统往往难以应对用户主动提出需求、持续调整目标与约束的情形。论文将这类需求界定为个性化信息发现问题，强调系统需要结合用户的明确请求和浏览行为，持续理解并适应其变化中的需求。

Method: 提出智能体瀑布框架，由语言模型控制器维护持久化用户状态，并协调内容检索、排序和状态更新。为评估该任务，论文构建智能体瀑布数据集，将真实内容元数据与模拟交互历史结合，并设计基于历史回放的评测协议：在共享历史条件下，通过相关性评分及盲评，衡量推荐是否符合用户当前目标与约束。

Result: 在五个公开基准的传统推荐任务上，该框架与强基线相比具有竞争力。在智能体瀑布数据集上，它在相关性评分和盲评两项评测中均超过最强基线，相对提升最高达百分之十六点一。

Conclusion: 研究将个性化推荐拓展为由用户主动引导、并需随需求变化而调整的信息发现任务，同时提供了相应的框架、数据集和评测协议。摘要结果表明，该框架在新任务上优于基线，但数据集包含模拟交互历史，其评测能否充分代表真实用户长期使用情境仍值得进一步验证。

Abstract: We formalize Agentic Waterfall Feed, a new problem of personalized information discovery in which users actively direct recommendations and the system adapts to evolving needs inferred from both explicit requests and browsing behavior. We address this problem with the Agentic Waterfall framework, whose language-model controller maintains persistent user state and coordinates content retrieval, ranking, and state updates. To evaluate systems on this task, we contribute the Agentic Waterfall Dataset and a replay-based evaluation protocol. The dataset combines real content metadata with simulated interaction histories. The protocol compares systems under shared histories using relevance scores and blind judgments of how well recommendations satisfy current user goals and constraints. Agentic Waterfall remains competitive with strong baselines on conventional recommendation tasks across five public benchmarks. On the Agentic Waterfall Dataset, it outperforms the strongest baseline in both evaluations, with relative improvements of up to 16.1%.

</details>


### [229] [Procedure Inference as Memory: Learning Recruiter Decision Procedures from OKF-Structured Episodes in Multi-Session Hiring Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34576)
*Praveen Kumar Bodigutla,Yizhi Zhang,Sai Vivek Kanaparthy,Arup De*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出以开放知识格式索引的情节记忆和程序记忆，帮助招聘助手跨项目、跨时间追踪偏好并复用决策流程；实验显示，两者能提升准确率与对话效率，同时减少上下文用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮、跨项目的招聘任务要求助手持续推断招聘者不断变化的偏好与决策模式。仅依赖无记忆、完整对话历史或普通对话检索，可能难以有效定位具有时间关联的证据，也会带来较高的上下文成本。因此，研究旨在构建更有结构、能支持长期个性化与时间推理的记忆机制。

Method: 研究采用两类记忆机制：一是以开放知识格式建立索引的情节记忆，按事件之间的谱系关系组织互动并检索相关经历；二是程序记忆，通过提示式反思或强化学习，从情节中学习可复用的决策流程。实验将这些方法与无记忆、完整历史上下文及对话搜索进行比较，并评估不同模型在索引导航和低成本推理方面的表现。

Result: 在招聘任务中，情节记忆在项目偏好重叠度中等和较高时，对低优先级偏好的识别取得百分之九十三至九十五的调和平均分，显著优于完整历史和对话搜索；在高重叠条件下也优于无记忆，并比完整历史少使用百分之六十九的上下文词元。程序记忆最多可将对话轮数减少百分之三十九。强化学习得到的程序在正确拒答和精确字段识别上均达到百分之六十。在另一长期记忆基准上，程序记忆结合索引检索的准确率为百分之五十点二，高于完整历史的百分之四十八点六和无记忆的百分之二十九，同时比完整历史少使用百分之九十点五的读入词元。较高效的索引导航模型虽能提升对话效率，却更容易漏掉与时间相关的证据；较低成本模型的表现则与更高成本模型相近。

Conclusion: 结构化情节记忆有助于从长期互动中筛选相关证据，程序记忆则能将过往经验转化为可复用的决策流程。两者结合可改善个性化、时间推理和对话效率，并显著降低上下文开销。研究也指出，效率优化不能以忽略时间相关证据为代价，证据选择仍是关键。

Abstract: Multi-session hiring assistants must infer evolving recruiter preferences and decision patterns across projects and time. We introduce Open Knowledge Format (OKF)-indexed episodic memory to organize interactions and retrieve related events through their lineage. We also introduce Procedure Memory, which learns reusable decision procedures from these episodes through prompted reflection or reinforcement learning. We compare these approaches with no memory, full-history context, and conversation search, and evaluate Jev-class models for index navigation and GPT-5.6 Luna as a lower-cost alternative to GPT-5.4.
  In our evaluation, OKF-indexed retrieval achieves low-priority preference F1 scores of 0.93-0.95 at medium and high preference overlap between hiring projects. It significantly outperforms full-history context and conversation search in both settings, and no memory at high overlap, while using 69% fewer context tokens than full history. Procedure Memory reduces conversational turns by up to 39% relative to no memory. Qualitative and long-horizon evaluations provide evidence of temporal reasoning, with RL-learned procedures achieving 60% on both correct abstention and exact-field identification. Jev-class models improve conversational efficiency but more frequently miss temporally relevant evidence, highlighting the importance of evidence selection.
  On PersonaMem-v2, Procedure Memory with OKF retrieval achieves 50.2% accuracy versus 48.6% for full-history context and 29.0% for no memory, using 90.5% fewer reader input tokens than full history. GPT-5.6 Luna performs comparably to GPT-5.4 at lower cost. These findings highlight the value of structured episodic memory and learned decision procedures for efficient personalization and temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [230] [How Much Dependency Can a Marginal Rule See? Total Correlation in Diffusion LM Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31990)
*Dovud Asadov,Rustambek Urokov,Qobiljon Toshnazarov,Abduaziz Kayumov,Mardon Hazratov,Husanboy Mansuraliyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估仅依赖各位置边缘分布的解码规则，能否预测并行提交一组词元时损失的依赖信息；结果显示预测能力有限，且对数值读出的正确校验至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型通过每步提交多个词元来提升生成速度，但若各词元独立地从边缘分布采样，就会丢失它们之间的依赖关系，这种损失可由提交集合的条件总相关性衡量。现有解码器通常依据各位置的边缘分布选词元集合，这些信息虽可给出损失上界，却未必能准确预测实际损失。论文因此研究：边缘分布对固定候选集合的条件总相关性究竟能预测到什么程度。

Method: 作者在来自两个模型家族的四个掩码扩散语言模型上，估计固定候选集合的条件总相关性，并比较仅使用边缘分布的预测器与只利用上界的基线。作者还在部分数据上将该估计与并行提交的精确散度代价进行比较，评测六种常见的边缘分布选集合规则，并提出三项检查，用于确认模型预测值在对数几率中的正确位置，避免读出约定错误导致诊断失真。

Result: 四个模型中有三个模型的最佳边缘分布预测器仍有约零点七四至一点一七比特的误差；其误差是仅知上界的基线的约一点八至三点七倍，而且预测能力的大部分来自上界本身。在部分样本上，估计值对集合的排序与精确散度代价相似，但在文本生成及其余文本仍被遮蔽时，估计值的中位数低约零点四至一点一比特。指令微调版的梦境模型是例外，尤其在文本生成时表现不同，主要原因是其条件分布的一致性较差。六种边缘分布规则中，按集合汇总信息的规则在全部二十四组配对比较中均优于由最弱位置决定的规则。此外，梦境模型与拉达模型在对数几率中的预测位置不同；若读取错误，依赖一致性的诊断会被放大十四至四十一次。

Conclusion: 仅凭位置边缘分布，通常无法准确预测固定提交集合的依赖损失；看似有效的预测很大程度上只是复现了理论上界。集合层面的汇总策略优于只关注最弱位置的门控策略，而可靠评估还必须先验证模型输出的读取约定。研究结论仅涉及对固定集合的损失预测，不代表采用依赖感知解码器能够获得多少收益。

Abstract: A masked diffusion language model is fast because it commits several tokens per step. Each token is sampled from its own marginal, so the dependency between the tokens is lost, and the size of that loss is the conditional total correlation C of the committed set. Most decoders choose the set from per-position marginals, which can bound C but cannot determine it. We ask how well they predict it. We estimate C for fixed candidate sets on four masked diffusion LMs from two families. On three of the four, the best marginal-only predictor is off by about one bit (0.74 to 1.17), 1.8 to 3.7 times less than a baseline that knows only the bound, and most of this accuracy comes from the bound itself. On a subset, our estimate ranks sets like the exact KL cost of a parallel commit, but sits a median 0.4 to 1.1 bits below it on prose and when the rest of the text is still masked. Dream-instruct is the exception, mainly on prose, where its conditionals are least self-consistent. Among six common marginal rules, pooling over the set beats gating on its weakest position in all 24 paired comparisons. The measurement needs a validated readout: Dream and LLaDA place a position's prediction at different logit indices, and the wrong index inflates an inconsistency diagnostic 14 to 41 times. We give three checks that measure the convention. Our results concern predicting C for a fixed set, not what a dependency-aware decoder gains.

</details>


### [231] [Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33303)
*Baptiste Juran,Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，按预设证明深度生成的逻辑题，未必真的需要同样多的推理步骤；因此提出可验证的题目难度审计与构造方法，并发现经过认证、类型多样的训练数据更有利于模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序生成的逻辑题常通过先抽取一条指定深度的证明，再把前提写成题目，来设定难度。但这条证明可能不是最短证明，题目也可能存在其他捷径或互不相关的证明，导致标注深度高估实际推理要求。研究旨在准确刻画题目相对于既定推理规则的真实难度。

Method: 对每道题分析所有可能证明，计算每条证明都必须包含的步骤、最短证明长度、最简证明的数量，以及不可或缺的前提，并生成可由独立检查器验证的证明证书。研究还为每套推理规则建立经过证明的捷径目录，据此构造所有证明都具有指定结构的题目；每道新题均进行完整验证并附带证书。

Result: 对八个数据集的审计发现：有些数据集的深度确实由题目强制决定；有些标注为深度五的题目实际只要求约两次特定规则应用；另一些题目的答案则不依赖任何特定推理步骤。在训练实验中，混合不同深度与题型的数据效果最佳；在数据量相同的条件下，包含由模型检查生成、而非正向生成所得题型的认证混合数据，对不同规模模型的迁移效果优于仿照既有推导式数据构造的训练集。

Conclusion: 逻辑题的难度应以所有有效证明共同体现的实际要求衡量，而不能仅依据生成时植入的证明深度。通过完整审计、可独立核验的证书和结构可控的题目构造，可以提升评测与训练数据的可靠性；多样且经认证的题目组合也更有助于模型泛化。

Abstract: Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.

</details>


### [232] [The Platonic Representation Hypothesis in Jacobian-Space: Language and Vision Models Agree Approximately Up to a Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33304)
*Ross Murphy,Gabriel Brostow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，视觉与语言模型不仅形成相近的世界表征，其表征影响输出的方式也大体一致；两者的输出作用空间可通过一个近似旋转相互映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究表明，不同模态的人工智能模型可能形成共享的世界表征。本文进一步追问：这种趋同是否也体现在模型如何利用内部表征生成词语、识别图像类别或影响生成结果上。

Method: 研究在变换器模型的指定层使用雅可比透镜，计算词汇中的每个词或分类器中的每个类别对隐藏状态最敏感的方向，并将这些方向组成输出作用空间。随后比较视觉与语言模型的空间，并检验能否用一个由部分词语拟合出的旋转，将一个模型的空间映射到另一个模型；再将映射后的向量注入视觉语言模型、图像生成器和图像分类器，观察输出变化。

Result: 视觉与语言模型的输出作用空间呈现趋同，且大多可通过单一旋转近似对齐；这一映射还能泛化到未参与拟合的词语和类别。映射后的词语向量可改变视觉语言模型对图像局部区域的描述、改变图像生成器生成的物体，并提高无文本训练的图像分类器对相应类别的评分；反向映射也成立。

Conclusion: 研究为共享表征假说提供了新的证据：不同模态模型的趋同不仅体现在内部表征内容上，也体现在这些表征如何驱动模型行为和输出上。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) posits that AI models converge toward a shared representation of the world, even when trained on different data modalities. Previous work has found evidence for the PRH in the activations of vision and language models. In this work, we ask whether vision and language models also converge in how they act on these representations: precisely, in how these internal representations affect the models' output. At a chosen layer of a transformer, the Jacobian lens gives every word in the vocabulary (or class in an image classifier) a vector in the hidden state: the vector that most increases the model's tendency to generate that word, at that position or later, or to predict that class. Collectively, these vectors make up what is referred to as the model's J-space. We find evidence of the PRH in J-space across vision and language models, and furthermore, we find that J-spaces mostly converge up to a rotation. That is, a single rotation, fitted on a subset of words, approximately maps one model's J-space onto another's, and so applies to words and classes it was not fitted on. A word's vector, rotated from a text-only language model's J-space into the visual patch tokens of a vision-language model, changes the object the vision-language model says is in the image (and only in the image region where it is added). Rotated into an image generator's text conditioning, a word's vector changes the object in the generated image. Rotated into an ImageNet classifier (trained without text), it raises that object's class score above almost all others, and vice versa. This suggests that models converge not only in how they represent the world, but also in how they act on it.

</details>


### [233] [Confidence scores for gated automation of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33394)
*Luiz G. A. Alves,Aaron Vermeersch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督学习的置信度评估框架，用于判断大语言模型答案是否正确，从而自动处理高置信度答案，并将不确定答案交由人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能以很高的自信给出错误答案，限制了其在影响个人或组织的决策中的应用。现有置信度估计往往不可用或粒度过粗，难以据此设定有效的自动化审核门槛。

Method: 研究使用经评分的答案训练正确性预测模型。对于可访问内部状态的模型，在冻结的大语言模型骨干网络最终层隐藏状态上拟合线性置信度评分头；对于封闭模型，则训练仅使用文本的元模型。实验采用一个二十亿参数的骨干模型，数据集主要由问答答案构成，另含少量真实性问答样本，并将该方法与多种基线进行比较。

Result: 在实验设置中，置信度评分头优于所有基线和文本元模型；在较高准确率要求下，它能够自动处理最多的任务。由提示词诱导得到的置信度分数表面上可能校准良好，但区分度不足，难以支持有效阈值筛选。对未见数据集的迁移能力有限，不过置信度评分头仍是最稳健的方法。

Conclusion: 基于模型内部表示训练的置信度评分头，有助于提升大语言模型智能体自动决策的可靠性，并支持将不确定答案转交人工审核。该框架也可扩展用于判断安全性或合规性等其他属性；但其跨数据集泛化能力仍需进一步改善。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated decision-making. However, LLMs may produce incorrect answers with high confidence, limiting their applicability in decisions that affect individuals and organizations. Reliable confidence scores are necessary to gate such decisions, and existing estimates are frequently unavailable or too coarse to support effective gating. This work uses supervised learning to train models to predict the correctness of LLM-generated answers, enabling automated confident responses and routing uncertain ones for human review. A linear confidence score head is fitted to the final-layer hidden state of the frozen LLM backbone, and text-only metamodels are trained as an alternative for closed models. All results come from a single 2B-parameter backbone, on graded answers that are about 98% SQuAD and the remainder TruthfulQA. Against multiple baselines, the confidence score head outperforms all baselines and metamodels, automating the largest fraction of the workload at high accuracy targets. Prompted confidence scores can appear well calibrated yet lack the resolution needed for thresholding. Although transferability to unseen datasets is modest, the confidence score head remains the most robust approach. The proposed confidence scoring framework enhances the safety of automating LLM-based agents and can be adapted to gate other properties, including safety or compliance.

</details>


### [234] [Partial Routing: Trace-Tuned Delegation for Resource-Efficient Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33402)
*Siddharth Arya,Kshitij Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“部分路由”：让前沿主模型在执行任务时，将适合的子任务委派给更便宜的工作模型。该方法依据模型过往表现生成委派建议，无需训练模型；实验显示，它能提升任务表现，并在部分场景下降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型路由通常为整个任务选择单一模型，但复杂的智能体任务往往同时包含简单和困难的子任务。统一使用高价模型会造成浪费，而统一使用低价模型又可能损害效果，因此需要在任务执行过程中按子任务灵活分配模型。

Method: 分析主模型与工作模型的历史运行记录，识别反复出现的子任务类型，并评估两种模型在各类子任务上的质量和成本。根据这些数据生成委派指导，由参数 δ 控制委派程度；执行时由主模型自行决定何时以及将哪些子任务交给工作模型。整个过程不需要训练任一模型。

Result: 在两个未参与指导构建的基准测试上，部分路由的平均任务表现优于仅使用主模型及按整项任务路由的方案。当 δ 为 0.75 时，某基准得分从 70.2 提升至 78.5，同时成本降低 19%；另一基准的通过率从 54% 提升至 61%，成本大致相当。此外，只向主模型提供子任务拆解、不使用工作模型，也能降低两个基准上的成本，并提升后一基准的通过率。

Conclusion: 对历史运行记录的分析可以转化为实用的任务规划与委派指导，使主模型更有效地结合较便宜模型的能力。部分路由在无需训练、实现开销较小的情况下，能够改善性能与成本之间的权衡。

Abstract: AI models vary enormously in capability and price. Model routing exploits this by sending easier tasks to cheaper models. However, a single agentic task often combines easy and difficult subtasks, making one model choice for the entire task inefficient. We introduce partial routing, which guides a frontier lead model to delegate suitable subtasks to a cheaper worker model. From both models' past runs, we identify recurring subtasks and measure each model's quality and cost on them. These measurements produce delegation guidance, with a parameter, $δ$, controlling how much is delegated. The lead decides when and what to delegate during execution, without training either model. On held-out tasks from ViBench and Terminal-Bench 4.0, partial routing achieves higher mean task performance than the lead alone and task-level routing. With $δ= 0.75$, it raises the lead's ViBench score from 70.2 to 78.5 at 19% lower cost, and its Terminal-Bench pass rate from 54% to 61% at similar cost. Giving the lead only the subtask breakdown, without a worker, also reduces cost on both benchmarks and raises the Terminal-Bench pass rate. These results show that analysis of past runs can support more efficient planning and more effective delegation, with little cost and implementation overhead.

</details>


### [235] [PeerReveal: Learning to Generalize in AI Peer Review Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33555)
*Zi Li,André V. Duarte,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种检测器，用于区分完全由人工智能生成的评审与仅借助人工智能改写人类判断的评审；通过结合主张关系、文本特征和对抗训练，在跨学科测试集上显著提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测面临两项挑战：不仅要区分评审内容是否源于人工智能，还要在不同学科、模型与生成流程下保持可靠。学科评审规范和人工智能生成方式的差异，使检测器容易遇到分布变化并失效。

Method: 检测器学习目标评审中的主张与同一论文的人工智能生成评审所含参考主张之间的关系，并结合评审整体的文本信号作出判断。研究还构建了实用的对抗训练框架，利用小型本地生成器生成更具挑战性的评审，以增强检测器的泛化能力。评估使用一个包含五千四百三十篇评审、涵盖机器学习、生命科学和社会科学的新测试集。

Result: 常规训练下，检测器在合并测试集上的宏平均调和均值为百分之七十点五二，比最强基线高十一点四八个百分点。加入对抗训练后，该指标进一步提升至百分之八十二点四零。

Conclusion: 可靠的人工智能评审检测需要利用比单纯文本模式更丰富的评审层面证据，并针对更具挑战性的生成过程进行对抗训练。研究结果表明，这两种思路有助于提升检测器跨领域、跨生成流程的泛化能力。

Abstract: Can we distinguish fully AI-generated reviews from those using AI only to rewrite human judgement? Furthermore, can we do so reliably when reviews are generated from diverse AI models and workflows across scientific domains? To this end, we introduce PeerReveal, a new detector that grounds its decisions in learned relationships between a review's claims and reference claims drawn from AI-generated reviews of the same paper, complemented by review-level textual signals. We evaluate PeerReveal on PeerShift, a new, challenging test set of 5,430 reviews spanning multiple domains, including machine learning, life sciences, and social sciences, combining variation in disciplinary review conventions with diverse AI review-production mechanisms. With regular training, PeerReveal achieves 70.52% pooled Macro-F1 on this benchmark, improving over the strongest baseline by 11.48 points. We develop an effective and practical adversarial training framework that further improves the detector's generalization using a small local generator optimized to produce challenging reviews, raising pooled Macro-F1 from 70.52% to 82.40%. These results show that robust AI-review detection benefits from richer review-level evidence and adversarial training on more challenging generation processes.

</details>


### [236] [Aligned Machine: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Embedding Similarity via Human Comparative Judgements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33594)
*Jiechen Li,Hannah Groos,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向公众的交互平台，通过成对比较收集人类相似性判断，并据此持续构建基准，用于检验嵌入模型的语义判断是否符合人类理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入模型已广泛影响搜索、推荐和信息检索，但现有评测通常依赖静态、由专家标注的样本及粗略评分，难以规模化，也不易获得一致且可信的标注。因此，研究需要一种更易参与、能持续扩充并可靠衡量人机语义一致性的评测方法。

Method: 研究团队开发了一个公开交互平台，让参与者比较两组文本或图像，并选择其中相似度更高的一组。相较于直接给单组样本打分，这种强制选择任务更直观，非专家也能参与，并可通过汇总偏好形成基准。初次部署中，115名参与者共提交2,875条标注，涉及872组不同样本；研究据此评估了12种常用嵌入模型。

Result: 评测显示，嵌入模型与人类相似性判断仅部分一致。纯文本模型的总体一致性始终高于多模态模型。多模态模型的表现还因任务类型而异：图像与图像比较时一致性最高，图像与文本比较次之，文本与文本比较最低。

Conclusion: 该平台将易于参与的公众标注与成对比较评测结合，为持续审计嵌入模型的语义对齐提供了可扩展框架。随着数据不断积累，它还可支持纵向追踪、领域专项研究、偏差检测，以及模型版本间概念变化的测量。

Abstract: Embedding models increasingly inform how people search, recommend, and retrieve information, yet we lack credible ways to test whether these similarity judgments reflect how humans understand meaning. Existing benchmarks typically rely on static, expert-labeled pairs with coarse Likert-style annotations, limiting scalability and making it difficult to obtain consistent, high-confidence annotations. We introduce Aligned Machine, an interactive, public-facing platform that transforms broad participation into a continuously growing Human-Aligned Similarity Benchmark (HASB) for evaluating semantic alignment in embedding-based systems. Participants complete a cognitively-natural, forced-choice task, comparing two pairs of items (text or images) and selecting which pair is more similar. This comparative design is accessible to non-experts, yields clean preference data, and supports robust aggregation. We report results from an initial deployment with 115 participants contributing 2,875 total labeled pairs and 872 unique pairs. Using HASB, we evaluated 12 widely used embedding models and found only partial alignment between embedding similarity and human judgments. Text-only models consistently achieve higher overall agreement than multimodal models, while multimodal performance varies systematically by task type: alignment is strongest for image-image comparisons, weaker for image-text comparisons, and lowest for text-text comparisons. Aligned Machine is designed to evolve over time as participation grows, supporting longitudinal analysis, domain-specific studies, bias detection, and measurement of concept drift across model versions. By combining accessible public participation with rigorous comparative evaluation, HASB provides a scalable and accountable framework for auditing semantic alignment in embedding models.

</details>


### [237] [What Does Skill Lift Actually Measure? A Source-Code-Level Audit and Noise-Aware Reanalysis of SkillsBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33943)
*Haozhe Zhang,Zhen Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对技能基准进行源代码审计和统计复核，指出观测到的负向结果不足以证明技能有害；基于无技能得分筛选技能的策略也未在独立留出数据上显示显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究发现，人工编写的智能体技能平均可提升任务通过率，但部分任务的结果似乎变差，因此后续工作尝试按任务筛选或移除技能。本文旨在核查这些负向结果是否可靠，以及技能筛选策略的表面收益是否确实由技能带来。

Method: 研究者对基准进行源代码级审计，并分析 7,554 次公开运行记录，整理出 1,421 个配对的“配置—任务”单元。审计核查评分器依赖和操作定义等问题；统计分析则考虑多重检验、每种条件最多三次运行造成的抽样噪声，并通过打乱条件标签检验筛选策略是否存在非技能因素带来的虚假收益，最后在未参与拟合和决策的留出运行上评估效果。

Result: 审计确认了两个经控制验证的评分器依赖案例和一个操作定义缺口；这三项任务的平均提升为 74.6 个百分点，整体平均提升为 18.7 个百分点。另有四项任务包含占位技能目录，某执行框架在多数运行中还额外加入了 48 个内置技能。经多重检验校正后，没有任务层面的下降达到统计显著性；由于每种条件最多只有三次运行，负向差异容易受抽样噪声影响，且从算术上看，58% 的单元不可能观察到下降。筛选策略在日志中看似提升 2—3 个百分点，但打乱条件标签后仍能产生 4—7 个百分点的提升；在留出运行上则未见显著改善。

Conclusion: 仅凭负向单元的数量，不能断言技能会损害任务表现；技能筛选策略在现有分析中也未证明具有可泛化的真实收益。研究强调了审计基准实现细节、控制运行噪声以及使用独立留出数据验证策略的重要性，并公开了审计记录和分析脚本。

Abstract: Agent skills are directories of instructions, reference materials, and scripts loaded on demand by coding agents. According to SkillsBench, although human-curated skills can raise the passing rate by about 16.6 percentage points (pp) on average, they have actually reduced the results for some tasks. Later research has started to gate or eliminate skills according to the above per-task gains. We have audited the above benchmark at the source-code level and analysed 7,554 public trials to form 1,421 paired "(configuration, task) cells". Source-code audit identified two controlled-verified cases of grader dependence and one operational definition gap; the average lift on these three tasks was 74.6 pp, and the overall average lift for the full set was 18.7 pp. The audit also found that four tasks contained placeholder skill directories, and a certain execution harness additionally added 48 built-in skills in most runs. No task-level reduction achieved statistical significance in the pooled reanalysis with multiple-testing correction. As there were at most three runs per condition for each cell, the observed negative differences are very susceptible to run-sampling noise, and arithmetically, 58% of the cells could not have shown a decrease. Therefore, the raw numbers of negative cells cannot show that skills cause harm. A gating strategy based on the skill-free score seems to improve by 2-3 pp in the logs; however, even after shuffling condition labels to remove any skill effect, it still achieves an increase of 4-7 pp. On the held-out runs not used for fitting and decision-making, there was no statistically significant improvement. The audit ledger and analysis scripts have been published by us.

</details>


### [238] [Budget, Readout or Reasoning? Where Small Reasoning Models Lose Value in Auction Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34068)
*Mahbod Khaleti,Seyed Reza Tavakoli,Baran Hoseini,Alireza Noori,Amir Amini,Koorosh Aslansefat,Marjan Alirezaie,Ali Habibnia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，推理语言模型根据竞价胜率曲线出价时，主要损失并非来自算术错误，而是模型选择失误及推理流程设置不当；让模型完成推理、正确读取答案，并审查其出价与自身推理是否一致，可显著缩小与精确计算器的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告平台和交易市场日益允许基于推理语言模型的代理选择出价，但模型是否能有效利用平台提供的胜率估计尚不清楚。由于模型已获得竞价胜率曲线，研究可以将收益损失归因于模型本身的决策，或代理运行与答案读取方式等操作因素。

Method: 研究测试了六种规模为四十亿至一百四十亿参数的开放式推理模型。模型依据来自易贝成交价和模拟广告市场拍卖网的胜率曲线，为保留拍卖选择出价，并与精确计算器的出价进行比较。研究分析模型推理过程、错误出价倾向、令牌上限和答案读取方式的影响，并测试一种审查机制：若最终出价与模型推理中列出的数值不一致，则以计算器的出价替换。审查效果通过嵌套交叉验证评估，并检验设置从易贝数据迁移至拍卖网数据的表现。

Result: 模型存在两类自身决策问题：即使推理记录中的数值已将最优选项排在首位，仍会选错；错误答案还倾向于选择菜单中金额最高的出价，这种倾向与选项排列位置无关，且在重复采样后仍然存在。在易贝的自付出价规则下，使用七百六十八个强制令牌时，错误出价造成的损失相当于计算器出价所得剩余收益的百分之七至百分之十七；算术错误则很少。操作设置也会造成显著损失：允许模型在最多四千零九十六个令牌内自行结束推理后，五种模型可追回相对于不推理模型的计算器优势的百分之九十一至百分之百。先关闭推理区块再读取答案，可使表现最弱的模型在易贝上的正确表现从百分之四十二升至百分之八十四，在拍卖网上从百分之二十四升至百分之八十一。最后，一致性审查将七百六十八个强制令牌条件下追回的计算器优势比例，在易贝上从百分之四十二至百分之八十五提升至百分之八十四至百分之一百；同一读取设置也可迁移到拍卖网。

Conclusion: 推理模型的竞价损失主要源于选择与输出流程，而非算术能力不足。给予充分的推理终止空间、正确关闭推理区块后读取答案，并对照模型自身推理审查最终出价，能够大幅改善竞价表现，且部分改进可跨市场迁移。

Abstract: Ad platforms and marketplaces show bidders a bid landscape, the estimated chance of winning at each candidate bid, and increasingly let agents built on reasoning language models choose the bid. We give six open reasoning models of 4B to 14B parameters such landscapes, with win curves from eBay closing prices and from a simulated advertising market (AuctionNet), and score their bids on held-out auctions against an exact calculator. Because the agent is handed the estimates, the lost value can be split by cause. Two failures belong to the models. They pass over the best option even when the values written in their own trace already rank it first, and their wrong answers lean toward the highest bid on the menu. The lean is about the bid's size, not its place in the list, and it survives sampling. At 768 forced tokens on eBay, under pay-your-bid pricing, the wrong answers cost bidders 7-17% of the surplus the calculator's bids earn. Arithmetic errors are rare. Two further losses come from operator choices. A token cap cuts traces short: allowed to stop on their own within 4,096 tokens, five models recover 91-100% of the calculator's lead over a model that does no reasoning. Asking for the answer while the reasoning block is still open misreads it: closing the block first lifts the weakest model from 42% to 84% on eBay and from 24% to 81% on AuctionNet. Finally, an audit that flags bids disagreeing with the trace's own numbers, and overrides them with the calculator's bid, raises the share of the calculator's lead recovered at 768 forced tokens from 42-85% to 84-100% on eBay under nested cross-validation, and the same reader settings transfer from eBay to AuctionNet.

</details>


### [239] [SOLO: Towards Sample-Faithful and Detectable LLM Watermarking under Repeated Sampling via Content-Anchored Single-Owner Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34190)
*Zhongliang Guo,Yan Lin,Yifei Qian,Shuai Zhao,Luwei Xiao,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多次采样请求的语言模型水印方案，在保持响应间独立性的同时提升水印检测能力与编辑鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用常会针对同一提示采样多个回答，用于自一致性判断、择优选择和不确定性估计。现有无失真水印通常只在单个回答内去重密钥上下文；但同一请求的回答往往共享相同的开头模板，导致它们反复使用相同上下文，响应间碰撞并非罕见，而是普遍现象。这种耦合可能影响下游应用。使用多个密钥池会引入多重检验代价；在整个请求间共享上下文历史虽可缓解碰撞，却会使许多位置无法加水印。

Method: 论文提出一种请求级上下文历史方案，并以基于内容的最小哈希选择作为密钥上下文的锚点。这样，同一请求中的不同回答较少争用相同上下文；随机编辑的影响也被限制在局部。该方案使多个回答的联合分布等同于彼此独立、且未加水印时的回答分布，同时每个回答仍可用单个密钥检测。

Result: 在三个基准、五种来自三个模型系列的语言模型上，该方案能够保留自一致性和不确定性估计能力。在百分之一的误报率下，多数任务中的检测回答数超过使用四个令牌密钥池的方法；随机编辑百分之十的令牌后仍保持这一优势。在多数未编辑任务中，其检测效果也优于共享上下文历史的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究指出，多回答场景中的模板重复会使常规水印产生跨回答耦合，并提出通过内容锚定的请求级上下文管理来化解这一问题。实验表明，该方法兼顾响应独立性、下游应用效用、检测性能和一定的编辑鲁棒性。

Abstract: Providers watermark the outputs of large language models, while applications sample many responses per prompt for self-consistency, best-of-n selection, and uncertainty estimation. We show that deployed configurations of distortion-free watermarks, which deduplicate keyed contexts only within a response, couple the responses of a request: answer templates make all responses query the watermark with the same context before the answer, so collisions across responses, usually assumed rare, become the norm. Key pools reduce or avoid the coupling at the cost of multiple testing, and the exact remedy of a context history shared across the request leaves many positions unwatermarked. We propose SOLO, which keeps a request-scoped context history but anchors its keyed contexts on content selected by min-hash, so that siblings rarely compete for a context and random edits stay local. Its responses are jointly distributed as independent unwatermarked responses, each detectable with a single key. On five language models from three families and three benchmarks, SOLO preserves self-consistency and uncertainty estimation and, at a 1% false positive rate, detects more responses than four-token key pools on most tasks, also after randomly editing 10% of the tokens, and more than the context history on most clean tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhongliangGuo/solo-watermark.

</details>


### [240] [MedSkill-E$^{2}$: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34250)
*Jingjing Hua,Shaocong Wei,Jianghan Shen,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Cheng Tang,Ziyan Huang,Jiepeng Li,Fengshuo Liu,Kangkun Mao,Mouxiao Bian,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu,Jiyao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究医疗智能体“技能已提供却未被充分使用”的问题，提出评测框架、基准测试和技能迭代方法；实验显示该问题普遍存在，而迭代后的技能能显著提升自主执行表现，并可迁移至不同模型与工具框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体虽可借助技能获取领域知识和操作流程，却常在自主执行时未能有效应用这些内容。医疗任务中，遗漏一项关键约束就可能产生看似合理但实际错误的结果，因此需要衡量并改善技能的实际使用能力，而不只是检查技能是否存在或已被读取。

Method: 论文提出一套面向医疗智能体的技能评估与演进框架，并构建涵盖十二类医疗任务及相关技能的基准测试。研究以自主使用技能与在明确指导下阅读并应用同一技能时的准确率差值，衡量技能使用落差。进一步提出技能演进方法：利用自主执行和明确指导执行所产生的反馈，提炼有效指导并写入可复用的技能说明。

Result: 所有受测模型与工具框架组合都出现技能使用落差，平均差距为四十三点九四个百分点。同一模型在自主使用技能时，准确率可因工具框架不同而相差最多三十八点九六个百分点。技能演进后，自主执行准确率提升三十三点三三至五十五点一七个百分点；提升也能迁移至多个模型和工具框架，并改善同一技能下未见任务的表现。研究还发现，即使技能已被打开，使用不足仍可能持续，且模型表现排名会随工具框架改变。

Conclusion: 技能是否可用或已被读取，并不保证智能体能在自主执行中正确运用它；技能表现还受到工具框架影响。通过执行反馈持续改进技能说明，能够有效缩小技能使用落差，并在一定范围内跨任务、模型和工具框架迁移。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use skills to access domain knowledge and complete real-world tasks. We observe that even when a skill specifies the rules and procedures needed for a task, agents may fail to apply them effectively during autonomous execution, a phenomenon we call skill Enable But Underuse (EBU). This issue is particularly consequential in medical tasks, where overlooking a single critical constraint can yield a plausible but incorrect result. To evaluate and address EBU, we introduce MedSkill-E$^{2}$, a framework for evaluating and evolving skills for medical agents. We construct EBU-Bench, a challenging benchmark covering 12 medically relevant task groups and associated skills, to evaluate agents' ability to use available skills autonomously. We quantify the EBU gap as the difference in skill-use accuracy between autonomous use and explicitly guided reading and application of the same skill. On EBU-Bench, all evaluated model-harness combinations exhibit EBU, with a mean gap of 43.94 percentage points (pp). For the same model, skill-use accuracy under autonomous use differs by up to 38.96 pp across harnesses. Motivated by these observations, we propose EBU-Evolve, which uses execution feedback from autonomous and explicitly guided skill use to distill effective guidance into reusable skill instructions. Relative to their pre-evolution versions, the revised skills improve skill-use accuracy during autonomous execution by 33.33 to 55.17 pp and continue to yield improvements when transferred across multiple models and harnesses. Our results show that autonomous skill-use accuracy varies across harnesses with the model and skill held fixed, and model rankings can change. EBU can persist even after a skill is opened. EBU-Evolve improves autonomous skill-use accuracy on unseen tasks within the same skill, with gains transferring across models and harnesses.

</details>


### [241] [vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34440)
*Qishuai Wen,Rui Chen,Haiwen Li,Zhiyuan Huang,Qiwen Gu,Bingjie Gao,Geng Li,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种测试时自我改进框架：监督者用自然语言分析智能体失败轨迹，再由超网络将反馈转化为低秩权重更新，使模型在执行任务时持续调整自身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习虽然能够提升模型能力，但通常需要昂贵的在线采样，且奖励信号稀疏，因此难以有效用于测试阶段。仅在上下文中加入反思或建议，也无法直接改变模型参数。论文希望结合自然语言密集反馈与测试时参数更新，让智能体能从失败中更有效地学习。

Method: 提出口头监督微调框架，在智能体与监督者之间建立闭环：智能体产生失败轨迹后，监督者据此生成自然语言指导；超网络再把指导转换为低秩权重更新，用于调整智能体。该过程使模型能够在测试时通过参数变化持续适应任务。

Result: 在阿尔夫世界和网页商店两个任务基准上，使用四十亿参数的昆文三模型进行评测。相较于组相对策略优化方法和反思式智能体，该框架在阿尔夫世界的成功率分别高出百分之八点七和百分之十三点九，在网页商店分别高出百分之四点八和百分之十八点二；整体表现也优于仅通过上下文进行适应的方法及常见强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，测试时将语言反馈转化为参数更新，可以让智能体比单纯依赖上下文提示或既有强化学习策略更有效地从失败中改进。其核心方向是让模型学会如何适应，而不只是使用一个预先适应好的模型。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time.
